3. Correct O1a scope

The early diagram was incomplete if read as only the scheduler half. The accepted definition is the complete physical interval from the previous forward’s R stamp to the next forward’s A stamp. It includes the previous return tail before queue and scheduler work.

setup and state entry
previous R → P → K → q_batches.clear() → Q → U
           → queue/work-loop/scheduler → F → G → H → next A
Ordinary-path concurrency result. The measured O1a path executes serially on the orchestrator/work-queue OS thread. Listener enqueue/countdown and attention/helper work finish before R and are boundary context, not measured O1a leaves. The data does not prove those other workers are idle after R.

4. Accepted markers and additive leaves

BoundaryOwnerExact placement / functionEffect

Twork queueAfter normal work-loop exit, before cleanupDiscards and reports the expected unpublished terminal prefix.
 
record_tail + qbatch_teardown + previous_exit
+ queue_handoff + cv_wait + request_apply + dispatch_delay
+ forward_args + forward_setup + state_entry + zero_logits_body
= O1a

previous_exit is one stored leaf equal to the sum of two disjoint spans, P→K and Q→U. Their separate durations were not recorded. Four mixed leaves—queue_handoff, request_apply, dispatch_delay, and forward_args—can contain lock wait or preemption.

Interpretation boundary. F→G occupies 2.984% of a fast full cycle and 4.681% of a slow full cycle. Its +0.250765 ms change explains 18.115% of full-cycle jitter and 65.812% of O1a jitter. It does not prove lock contention, traversal work, allocator activity, or preemption. O1c is still the larger ring location at +0.579504 ms/nonsentinel global cycle; O8 contributes +0.287740 ms.

Fast group: draw_20, draw_19, draw_15, draw_06, draw_10. Slow group: draw_09, draw_03, draw_02, draw_18, draw_08. Groups are the five lowest/highest mean request generation durations, selected after the campaign; each draw is normalized first and weighted equally.

7. Current exact F/G code and proposed split

Frozen R2 source authority: base commit 0b6cb141389f0d0e6b74f38e8ba8093ffe2dfb16; patch SHA-256 afa90460b5581764196527a51b11c0f48fe77c41140bb15d3b77d0d4bd190387. The line numbers below are from that frozen source, not current trunk.

Current F recorder — h/common/g2_diag_ring.hpp:2225–2237

inline void orch_o1a_forward_entry(const void* scheduler_owner) noexcept {
  if (!o1a_scheduler_owner_gate(scheduler_owner)) return;
  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
  if (o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aNoSeed) return;
  if (!c->o1a_executor_open || c->o1a_executor_kind != 1 ||
      c->o1a_executor_ptr != scheduler_owner) {
    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
    return;
  }
  uint64_t t = tsc();
  if (orch_o1a_transition_at(c, kO1aDispatchDelay, kO1aForwardArgs, t))
    (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitF]);
}

Current tree lock and traversal — h/tron/scheduler/full.hpp:1477–1483

tron::tree_lock tree_guard;
size_t nodes_traversed = 0;
auto [ok, root_masks_ptr, root_ranges, root_shards] =
    compute_forward_args(root.get(), nodes_traversed);

Current G call/recorder — full.hpp:1490–1494, g2_diag_ring.hpp:2239–2251

#if TRON_DIAG_RING
  G2_O1A_FORWARD_ARGS(this, token_jobs.empty());  // G
#endif

inline void orch_o1a_forward_args(const void* scheduler_owner,
                                  bool empty) noexcept {
  if (!o1a_scheduler_owner_gate(scheduler_owner)) return;
  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
  if (o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aNoSeed) return;
  uint64_t t = tsc();
  uint8_t next = empty ? kO1aDispatchDelay : kO1aForwardSetup;
  if (!orch_o1a_transition_at(c, kO1aForwardArgs, next, t)) return;
  (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitG]);
  if (empty) {
    (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_count[kO1aCountEmptySweeps]);
    c->o1a_empty_return_pending = true;
  }
}

F→F1 + F1→F2 + F2→F3 + derived(F3→G) = existing F→G forward_args

Do not copy names blindly. F1/F2/F3 are design labels, not frozen macro or field names. Read current exports, immediate callers, runtime state, parser structures, and schema conventions before naming them. Preserve owner/TID gates before every new TSC read or accumulator store.

8. Candidate record-v8 wire extension

The current v7 record reserves bytes 360–375. A minimal candidate can preserve the 384-byte record, commit offset 376, file geometry, existing d_o1a_forward_args parent at offset 340, and all helper-v6 fields:

OffsetBytesCandidate contentPurpose
3604u32 F→F1 duration sumPoll/local preparation.
3644u32 F1→F2 duration sumtree_lock construction/acquisition wall time.
3684u32 F2→F3 duration sumcompute_forward_args traversal wall time.
3724u32 accumulated nodes_traversedWork-volume counter used to separate more work from more time per node.
3768Existing commit/padding regionDo not move without a separate approved schema change.
This is a design candidate, not an accepted schema. Before implementation, prove the maximum nodes_traversed sum fits its chosen wire type under repeated F/G attempts. If u32 is not provably sufficient, revise the design instead of saturating normal data. Any narrowing overflow must fail closed and invalidate the draw. Because previously reserved bytes become meaningful, bump the record version and update all layout asserts, hashes, parser identity, validator, analyzer, fixtures, and reports.

Three new TSC reads per target F/G attempt are sufficient because G already exists and the final tail is derived. Preserve the existing parent measurement. For repeated empty sweeps, accumulate the three stored children across every target F/G attempt so the exact identity still closes.

9. Complete Tron instrumentation source change set

Complete source is embedded below. The accepted R2 authority is a full patch against base commit 0b6cb141389f0d0e6b74f38e8ba8093ffe2dfb16, not an O1a-only incremental diff. It contains the inherited O-stage, O1c/helper, expert-stat capture, and O1a-v7 instrumentation required by the accepted binary: 21 files, 6,546 insertions, 97 deletions. Patch SHA-256: afa90460b5581764196527a51b11c0f48fe77c41140bb15d3b77d0d4bd190387.

Instrumentation architecture in the source

Compile gate
TRON_DIAG_RING global definition
→
Runtime gate
TRON_DIAG_RING_RECORD=1
→
Call-site hooks
model, queue, scheduler, helpers
→
Writer/state machine
g2_diag_ring.hpp
→
384-byte records
format 4 / record 7
→
Dump paths
normal, exception, crash
→
Offline validation
runtime/threading/generator tests
  • Compile-time off: when TRON_DIAG_RING=OFF, gated hooks and gated class members do not compile into the program. The definition is global to avoid header-layout ODR violations.
  • Compile-time on, recording off: the mapping, first touch, calibration, and baseline coherence work still run, while per-cycle recording remains disabled unless TRON_DIAG_RING_RECORD=1.
  • Recording on: owner-bound, fail-closed hooks write preallocated region-local state and committed records. Parser conversion to nanoseconds happens later; exact identities use raw TSC sums.
  • Lifecycle: initialization happens at model-state creation; normal exit calls the idempotent dump explicitly; terminate/SIGSEGV paths attempt a binary-only crash dump.
  • O1a ownership: one scheduler/model-state pair is registered; the first valid target executor binds the owner thread; owner/TID validation completes before new TSC reads or stores.

+++ b/CMakeLists.txt
@@ -44,6 +44,21 @@ option(COVERAGE "Source-based coverage instrumentation" OFF)
 option(AVX512 "Enable AVX512" ON)
 option(ENABLE_FUSE_STATS "Enable FUSE filesystem stats feature" ON)
 option(TRON_DISTRIBUTED "Enable distributed pipeline parallelism (requires UCX)" OFF)
+option(TRON_DIAG_RING "Gate-2 diagnostic ring instrumentation (10.4f)" OFF)
+# TRON_DIAG_RING must be a GLOBAL compile definition, never per-target.
+# The hooks add gated data members to classes that are DEFINED IN HEADERS
+# (top_k_accum_t in h/common/top_k_accum.hpp; the ptensor fan-in
+# stream_result_t in h/tron/hardware/ptensor.hpp). A per-target
+# target_compile_definitions would let one translation unit see the extra
+# members and another not, so the same class would have two different
+# layouts in one program: an ODR violation. That is undefined behaviour and
+# it does not fail at link time -- it silently corrupts memory. Directory
+# scope here, before every add_subdirectory() below, gives every project
+# target the identical definition.
+if(TRON_DIAG_RING)
+  add_compile_definitions(TRON_DIAG_RING=1)
+  message(STATUS "Gate-2 diagnostic ring enabled (TRON_DIAG_RING=1)")
+endif()
 SET(SPDLOG_BUILD_PIC ON CACHE BOOL "Force PIC for SPDLOG")
 set(CMAKE_POSITION_INDEPENDENT_CODE ON)

+// g2_diag_ring.hpp — Gate 2 per-worker diagnostic ring (writer side).
+//
+// GOVERNING DOCUMENT
+//   review/tron-perf-fluctuation/prelive/10.4g-o1a-substage-ring-schema.md
+//   10.4g O1a contract (DUT smoke pending). This is the additive record-v7
+//   revision of the frozen helper-v6 baseline.
+//   Normative runtime and binary-layout sections implemented here:
+//     §2   architecture conformance (compile gate, preallocated, cache-line
+//          separated, per-worker single writer, the one named shared line set)
+//     §3   clock domains (lfence;rdtsc TSC domain, CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID,
+//          load/dump calibration triples, the 50 ppm rate gate, the
+//          cross-thread TSC coherence probe)
+//     §5   record framing, capacity, footprint, count-and-drop overflow,
+//          memory-ordering discipline, dump trigger and integrity
+//     §6   record layouts REC_WORKER / REC_CYCLE (384 B each) and the
+//          §6.3 first-record rule
+//     §7   the whole-cycle partitions: identity W (worker) and identity C
+//          (cycle), both exact 0-tick sums by construction
+//     §8   arm D (TRON_DIAG_RING_RECORD != "1"): zero PAYLOAD stores and
+//          zero per-cycle stores. The region HEADER is written in arm D:
+//          init writes worker_ix / region_kind / recording, and the attach
+//          path writes tid and tsc_cross_thread_skew_ns before the recording
+//          test — see §8.2/§8.3 and g2_armd_disasm_proof.md §6.
+//     §9.1 dump file format — 31,478,032 B, little-endian
+//     §11  the rx stamp stride rule
+//
+//   Every record-v7 layout constant is duplicated in the versioned parser.
+//   If the two ever disagree, the versioned parser wins and
+//   this header is wrong.
+//
+// PLACEMENT
+//   Copied into the g2 worktree as h/common/g2_diag_ring.hpp and included as
+//   "common/g2_diag_ring.hpp" (the include root is h/, per the existing
+//   "common/top_k_accum.hpp" include in model.hpp).
+//
+// BUILD GATE
+//   Everything is inside `#if TRON_DIAG_RING`. The include in model.hpp is
+//   itself gated, so an unflagged build never opens this file and must be
+//   byte-comparable to the unmodified tree (§2 row 1; a Gate-0 check).
+//
+// RUNTIME GATE (§8.2)
+//   TRON_DIAG_RING_RECORD=1  -> arm E (recording enabled)
+//   anything else / unset    -> arm D (recording disabled; the mapping,
+//                                      first touch, calibration and the TSC
+//                                      original four-worker coherence probe
+//                                      still runs; O1c pool probe is E-only)
+//   TRON_DIAG_RING_DIR       -> output directory for ring.bin/ring.meta.json
+//   TRON_DIAG_RING_STRIDE    -> rx_stamp_stride (§11), default 1
+//   TRON_DIAG_RING_RUN_ID    -> run id string recorded in the file header
+//
+// RECORD-V7 O1A CANDIDATE based on the frozen helper-v6 source. Byte-exact
+// layout is static_assert-ed here. Offline tests and a live smoke must pass
+// before this candidate is frozen for a campaign.
+// The historical g2_armd_disasm_proof.md applies to an older build; the owner
+// removed the recording-OFF/ON comparison from the current plan. The real
+// delphi-3bda/FPGA smoke draw remains pending and is the next validation step.
+//
+// §5.6 EXIT PATH. Three triggers, one dump, in this order of precedence:
+//   1. the explicit tron_diag_ring_dump() in runtron's main() after eval();
+//   2. atexit(&dump) registered at init — the backstop for other callers;
+//   3. dump_from_crash() from tron's existing std::terminate / SIGSEGV
+//      handlers (src/common/backtrace.cpp), before their abort().
+// No new signal handler is installed. `_exit()`, SIGKILL/SIGTERM/SIGINT and a
+// raw abort() still yield no ring.bin; that residual is documented in §5.6.
+//
+// C++20. No allocation after init. No locks. No syscall on the hot path
+// except the CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID reads that the §4 field-16 row
+// specifies: one at worker entry, one per committed worker record, one per
+// cycle for the orchestrator.
+
+#pragma once
+
+#if defined(TRON_DIAG_RING) && (TRON_DIAG_RING)
+
+#include <atomic>
+#include <cerrno>
+#include <cstddef>
+#include <cstdint>
+#include <cstdio>
+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <cstring>
+#include <fcntl.h>
+#include <time.h>
+#include <unistd.h>
+#include <x86intrin.h>
+
+#include <sys/mman.h>
+#include <sys/syscall.h>
+
+namespace tron {
+namespace g2ring {
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// §9.1 / §5.3 fixed geometry — mirrors ring_parse.py module constants
+// ===========================================================================
+inline constexpr uint64_t kFileSize = 31478032ull;     // FILE_SIZE
+inline constexpr uint32_t kFileHdrSize = 256;          // FILE_HDR_SIZE
+inline constexpr uint64_t kRegionStride = 6295552ull;  // REGION_STRIDE
+inline constexpr uint32_t kRegionHdrSize = 128;        // REGION_HDR_SIZE
+inline constexpr uint32_t kCapacity = 16384;           // CAPACITY
+inline constexpr uint32_t kRecSize = 384;              // REC_SIZE
+inline constexpr uint32_t kCommitOffset = 376;         // COMMIT_OFFSET
+inline constexpr uint32_t kNRegions = 5;               // N_REGIONS
+inline constexpr uint32_t kNWorkers = 4;               // N_WORKERS
+inline constexpr uint32_t kOrchRegion = 4;             // region 4 = orchestrator
+inline constexpr uint64_t kPayloadBytes = uint64_t(kCapacity) * kRecSize;  // 6,291,456
+inline constexpr uint64_t kRingBytes = uint64_t(kNRegions) * kRegionStride;
+inline constexpr uint32_t kEndianMarker = 0x01020304u;
+inline constexpr uint32_t kFormatVersion = 4;
+inline constexpr uint32_t kRecordVersion = 7;
+inline constexpr uint16_t kCalibWindowMs = 100;
+inline constexpr uint16_t kCalibSamples = 5;
+inline constexpr char kMetaSchema[] = "10.4g-o1a-substage-ring-schema.md";
+static_assert(kCommitOffset + sizeof(uint64_t) == kRecSize, "commit is last");
+
+// §2 named exception: one publication slot per logit chunk, §5.3 budgets
+// 8 x 64 B. A cycle with more chunks than this drops its worker records
+// (records_dropped != 0 => V7 fatal) rather than misclassifying blocks.
+inline constexpr uint32_t kMaxChks = 8;
+
+static_assert(kFileHdrSize + kRingBytes + 16 == kFileSize, "file geometry");
+static_assert(kRegionHdrSize + kPayloadBytes <= kRegionStride, "region stride");
+static_assert(kPayloadBytes == 6291456ull, "record-v7 zero payload geometry");
+static_assert(kRegionStride - kRegionHdrSize - kPayloadBytes == 3968ull,
+    "record-v7 retains the helper-v6 zero tail pad");
+static_assert(kRingBytes == 31477760ull, "record-v7 mapped ring geometry");
+static_assert(kRegionStride % 4096 == 0, "regions are whole pages (1537)");
+static_assert(kRecSize % 64 == 0, "records are whole cache lines");
+static_assert(kRegionHdrSize % 64 == 0, "payload starts 64-B aligned");
+
+inline constexpr uint8_t kRecWorker = 1;  // §5.1 rec_type
+inline constexpr uint8_t kRecCycle = 2;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kKindWorker = 0;  // §9.1 region_kind
+inline constexpr uint8_t kKindOrch = 1;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kWclsUnavailable = 0;  // §6.1 wcls_src
+inline constexpr uint8_t kWclsOrchPublished = 1;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kWclsWorkerWait = 2;
+
+inline constexpr uint8_t kWFlagSaturated = 0x01;  // §6.1 flags
+inline constexpr uint8_t kWFlagClockAnomaly = 0x02;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kWFlagStrideActive = 0x04;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kWFlagMaskPresent = 0x08;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kWFlagDensePath = 0x10;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kWFlagLogIntermediates = 0x20;
+
+inline constexpr uint16_t kCFlagSaturated = 0x0001;  // §6.2 / §6.3 flags
+inline constexpr uint16_t kCFlagClockAnomaly = 0x0002;
+inline constexpr uint16_t kCFlagFirstRecord = 0x0004;
+inline constexpr uint16_t kCFlagO1SubstageAnomaly = 0x0008;
+inline constexpr uint16_t kCFlagO1cLaneAnomaly = 0x0010;
+inline constexpr uint16_t kCFlagO1aPartitionAnomaly = 0x0020;
+
+inline constexpr uint32_t kO1aLeafCount = 11;
+inline constexpr uint32_t kO1aCountCount = 4;
+inline constexpr uint32_t kO1aHitCount = 16;
+
+inline constexpr uint32_t kMaxPoolWorkers = 64;
+inline constexpr uint32_t kPoolProbeIters = 1000;
+inline constexpr uint32_t kPoolSkewLimitNs = 100;
+inline constexpr uint32_t kCriticalAmbiguityNs = 200;
+
+// releaser_mask bits (§6.1). Exact listeners_notified identity is carried only
+// by REC_CYCLE. Keeping this mask limited to the two pre-commit latches lets
+// every detailed record commit before its notifier decrement.
+inline constexpr uint8_t kRelPrepared = 0x01;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kRelFree = 0x02;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kFinalNotifierExact = 1;
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// §6.1 REC_WORKER — 384 B. Bytes 0..311 preserve helper-v6 exactly; bytes
+// 312..375 are the record-v7 zero extension, asserted below.
+// ===========================================================================
+struct alignas(64) rec_worker_t {
+  uint64_t seq;                   // 0
+  uint64_t cycle_id;              // 8
+  uint64_t worker_cycle_seq;      // 16
+  uint64_t t_entry;               // 24  S0
+  uint64_t t_exit;                // 32  S9
+  uint64_t t_first_rx;            // 40  S3
+  uint64_t t_last_rx_end;         // 48  S4
+  uint64_t t_wcls_seen;           // 56
+  uint64_t cpu_ns;                // 64
+  uint32_t d_pre_wcls_wait;       // 72  P1
+  uint32_t d_stream_prepare;      // 76  P2
+  uint32_t d_pre_first_rx;        // 80  P3
+  uint32_t d_rx_insert;           // 84  P4a
+  uint32_t d_rx_gap;              // 88  P4b
+  uint32_t d_post_last_rx;        // 92  P5
+  uint32_t d_sort;                // 96  P6
+  uint32_t d_post_sort;           // 100  P7
+  uint32_t d_callback;            // 104  P8
+  uint32_t d_countdown;           // 108  P9
+  uint32_t d_max_rx_gap;          // 112
+  uint32_t blocks_before;         // 116
+  uint32_t blocks_after;          // 120
+  uint32_t blocks_expected;       // 124
+  uint32_t entries_before;        // 128
+  uint32_t entries_after;         // 132
+  uint32_t heap_pushes;           // 136
+  uint32_t heap_replacements;     // 140
+  uint32_t poll_false_returns;    // 144
+  uint16_t listener_id;           // 148
+  uint16_t n_listeners_served;    // 150
+  uint8_t rec_type;               // 152
+  uint8_t rec_version;            // 153
+  uint8_t worker_ix;              // 154
+  uint8_t chk_ix;                 // 155
+  uint8_t n_chks;                 // 156
+  uint8_t releaser_mask;          // 157  bit0 prepared, bit1 free; upper bits zero
+  uint8_t wcls_src;               // 158
+  uint8_t flags;                  // 159
+  uint64_t t_countdown_notified;  // 160
+  uint8_t reserved[16];           // 168  zero; nonzero is fatal (V5)
+  uint8_t reserved_v4[64];        // 184  zero; nonzero is fatal (V5)
+  uint8_t reserved_v5[64];        // 248  helper-v6 zero reserve
+  uint8_t reserved_v7[64];        // 312  record-v7 zero reserve
+  uint64_t commit;                // 376  FNV-1a-64 over bytes [0,376)
+};
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// §6.2 REC_CYCLE — 384 B. Existing helper-v6 fields keep their offsets
+// through O1e at byte 172. Dispatch, main, attention, and helper durations
+// overlap; never add those endpoints. critical_ready + join_tail equals O1c.
+// ===========================================================================
+struct alignas(64) rec_cycle_t {
+  uint64_t seq;                           // 0
+  uint64_t cycle_id;                      // 8
+  uint64_t t_entry;                       // 16
+  uint64_t t_return;                      // 24
+  uint64_t t_prev_return;                 // 32
+  uint64_t t_wcls_done;                   // 40
+  uint64_t t_listeners_notified;          // 48
+  uint64_t cpu_ns;                        // 56
+  uint32_t d_upstream;                    // 64  O1
+  uint32_t d_logits_prep;                 // 68  O2
+  uint32_t d_free_wait;                   // 72  O3
+  uint32_t d_stream_alloc;                // 76  O4
+  uint32_t d_prepared_wait;               // 80  O5
+  uint32_t d_wcls_launch;                 // 84  O6
+  uint32_t d_wcls_done_wait;              // 88  O7
+  uint32_t d_notified_wait;               // 92  O8
+  uint32_t d_eagle;                       // 96  O9
+  uint32_t d_epilogue;                    // 100  O10
+  uint32_t n_logit_chks;                  // 104
+  uint32_t n_listeners;                   // 108
+  uint32_t n_empty_listeners;             // 112
+  uint32_t logits_chunk_size;             // 116
+  uint32_t max_top_k;                     // 120
+  uint32_t vocab_size;                    // 124
+  uint8_t rec_type;                       // 128
+  uint8_t rec_version;                    // 129
+  uint16_t flags;                         // 130
+  uint32_t final_notifier_ix;             // 132  task index among all notifier tasks
+  uint64_t t_final_notifier_pre_release;  // 136, before latch becomes zero
+  uint64_t final_notifier_cycle_id;       // 144 cross-cycle reuse guard
+  uint8_t final_notifier_src;             // 152  1 = exact latch finalizer
+  uint8_t o1_substage_pad[3];             // 153  explicit zero alignment pad (V5)
+  uint32_t d_o1_before_run_forward;       // 156 O1a: prev return -> Stamp A
+  uint32_t d_o1_setup;                    // 160 O1b: Stamp A -> Stamp B
+  uint32_t d_o1_model_forward;            // 164 O1c: Stamp B -> Stamp C
+  uint32_t d_o1_post_model;               // 168 O1d: Stamp C -> Stamp D
+  uint32_t d_o1_pre_run_logits;           // 172 O1e: Stamp D -> current t_entry
+  uint32_t dispatch_sum;                  // 176 sum(B -> all tasks submitted)
+  uint32_t main_prejoin_sum;              // 180 sum(B -> main arrives at join)
+  uint32_t attention_done_sum;            // 184 sum(B -> last attention completion)
+  uint32_t helper_done_sum;               // 188 helper-present forwards only
+  uint32_t critical_ready_sum;            // 192 sum(max(main, attention, helper))
+  uint32_t join_tail_sum;                 // 196 sum(C - critical endpoint)
+  uint32_t attention_tasks;               // 200 total expected attention tasks
+  uint32_t helper_tasks;                  // 204 total expected helper tasks
+  uint32_t forwards;                      // 208 contributing forwards
+  uint32_t zero_logit_forwards;           // 212 carried zero-logit forwards
+  uint32_t attention_forwards;            // 216 forwards with attention
+  uint32_t helper_forwards;               // 220 forwards with one or more helpers
+  uint32_t main_critical;                 // 224 unambiguous main-critical forwards
+  uint32_t attention_critical;            // 228 unambiguous attention-critical forwards
+  uint32_t helper_critical;               // 232 unambiguous helper-critical forwards
+  uint32_t critical_ambiguous;            // 236 top endpoints within 200 ns
+  uint8_t reserved[8];                    // 240 zero; nonzero is fatal (V5)
+  uint32_t helper_blend8_return_sum;      // 248 helper-present forwards only
+  uint32_t helper_blend17_return_sum;     // 252 helper-present forwards only
+  uint32_t helper_blend26_return_sum;     // 256 helper-present forwards only
+  uint32_t helper_blend35_return_sum;     // 260 helper-present forwards only
+  uint32_t dispatch_sum_hi;               // 264 high word for offset 176
+  uint32_t main_prejoin_sum_hi;           // 268 high word for offset 180
+  uint32_t attention_done_sum_hi;         // 272 high word for offset 184
+  uint32_t helper_done_sum_hi;            // 276 high word for offset 188
+  uint32_t critical_ready_sum_hi;         // 280 high word for offset 192
+  uint32_t join_tail_sum_hi;              // 284 high word for offset 196
+  uint32_t helper_blend8_return_sum_hi;   // 288 high word for offset 248
+  uint32_t helper_blend17_return_sum_hi;  // 292 high word for offset 252
+  uint32_t helper_blend26_return_sum_hi;  // 296 high word for offset 256
+  uint32_t helper_blend35_return_sum_hi;  // 300 high word for offset 260
+  uint8_t reserved_v6[8];                 // 304 helper-v6 zero reserve
+  uint32_t d_o1a_record_tail;             // 312
+  uint32_t d_o1a_qbatch_teardown;         // 316
+  uint32_t d_o1a_previous_exit;           // 320
+  uint32_t d_o1a_queue_handoff;           // 324
+  uint32_t d_o1a_cv_wait;                 // 328
+  uint32_t d_o1a_request_apply;           // 332
+  uint32_t d_o1a_dispatch_delay;          // 336
+  uint32_t d_o1a_forward_args;            // 340
+  uint32_t d_o1a_forward_setup;           // 344
+  uint32_t d_o1a_state_entry;             // 348
+  uint32_t d_o1a_zero_logits_body;        // 352
+  uint8_t n_o1a_cv_waits;                 // 356
+  uint8_t n_o1a_empty_sweeps;             // 357
+  uint8_t n_o1a_queue_loops;              // 358
+  uint8_t n_o1a_other_executor_forwards;  // 359
+  uint8_t reserved_v7[16];                // 360 zero; nonzero is fatal
+  uint64_t commit;                        // 376
+};
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// §9.1 region header — 128 B. Parser REGION_HDR_STRUCT
+// "<2I5Q4IiBB2s4IiIQ5IB3s".
+// At the base of each mapped region; written by the owning thread only.
+// ===========================================================================
+struct region_hdr_t {
+  uint32_t worker_ix;                     // 0  (4 for the orchestrator region)
+  uint32_t tid;                           // 4  gettid()
+  uint64_t published_count;               // 8  release-stored after each commit
+  uint64_t records_written;               // 16
+  uint64_t records_dropped;               // 24  §5.4 count-and-drop
+  uint64_t first_cycle_id;                // 32
+  uint64_t last_cycle_id;                 // 40
+  uint32_t saturated_durations;           // 48
+  uint32_t clock_anomalies;               // 52
+  uint32_t cpu_clock_failures;            // 56
+  uint32_t cycle_id_mismatches;           // 60
+  int32_t tsc_cross_thread_skew_ns;       // 64
+  uint8_t recording;                      // 68
+  uint8_t region_kind;                    // 69
+  uint8_t pad[2];                         // 70
+  uint32_t lane_anomalies;                // 72 orchestrator only; zero is required
+  uint32_t pool_workers;                  // 76 exact app-pool worker count
+  uint32_t pool_probe_covered;            // 80 workers completing the full probe
+  uint32_t pool_probe_failures;           // 84 deadlines/order/range failures
+  int32_t max_abs_pool_skew_ns;           // 88 max measured |worker-orch skew|
+  uint32_t o1c_ambiguity_ticks;           // 92 ceil(200 ns * load TSC rate)
+  uint64_t covered_mask;                  // 96 pool worker indices proved
+  uint32_t o1a_anomalies;                 // 104 orchestrator only
+  uint32_t o1a_owner_mismatches;          // 108 orchestrator only
+  uint32_t o1a_cross_thread_hooks;        // 112 orchestrator only
+  uint32_t o1a_count_overflows;           // 116 orchestrator only
+  uint32_t o1a_terminal_prefix_discards;  // 120 orchestrator only
+  uint8_t o1a_terminal_prefix_phase;      // 124 orchestrator only
+  uint8_t reserved_v7[3];                 // 125 zero
+};
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// §9.1 file header — 256 B. Parser's FILE_HDR_STRUCT
+// "<8s8IQ6Q3IB3s16s40s32s32s24s". File-only framing, built at dump time.
+// ===========================================================================
+struct file_hdr_t {
+  uint8_t magic[8];                 // 0  "TRONRING"
+  uint32_t format_version;          // 8
+  uint32_t record_version;          // 12
+  uint32_t endian_marker;           // 16
+  uint32_t record_size;             // 20
+  uint32_t n_regions;               // 24
+  uint32_t n_detailed_workers;      // 28  worker regions with P1-P9 data
+  uint32_t capacity_records;        // 32
+  uint32_t header_size;             // 36
+  uint64_t region_stride;           // 40
+  uint64_t calib_load_tsc;          // 48
+  uint64_t calib_load_mono_raw_ns;  // 56
+  uint64_t calib_load_realtime_ns;  // 64
+  uint64_t calib_dump_tsc;          // 72
+  uint64_t calib_dump_mono_raw_ns;  // 80
+  uint64_t calib_dump_realtime_ns;  // 88
+  uint32_t calib_residual_ppm;      // 96
+  uint32_t tsc_flags;               // 100
+  uint32_t rx_stamp_stride;         // 104
+  uint8_t recording_enabled;        // 108
+  uint8_t pad[3];                   // 109
+  uint8_t arm_label[16];            // 112  "D" / "E", NUL-padded
+  uint8_t run_id[40];               // 128
+  uint8_t exe_sha256[32];           // 168
+  uint8_t zerofill_digest[32];      // 200
+  uint64_t calib_load_rate_q32;     // 232  ticks/ns in unsigned Q32
+  uint64_t calib_dump_rate_q32;     // 240  ticks/ns in unsigned Q32
+  uint32_t n_notifier_tasks;        // 248  all listeners_notified decrements
+  uint16_t calib_window_ms;         // 252  each local-rate sample window
+  uint16_t calib_samples;           // 254  median sample count
+};
+
+#define G2_OFF_ASSERT(T, m, o)                                                         \
+  static_assert(offsetof(T, m) == (o), "layout: " #T "." #m " must be at " #o)
+
+static_assert(sizeof(rec_worker_t) == 384 && alignof(rec_worker_t) == 64, "REC_WORKER");
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_worker_t, seq, 0);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_worker_t, t_entry, 24);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_worker_t, cpu_ns, 64);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_worker_t, d_pre_wcls_wait, 72);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_worker_t, d_max_rx_gap, 112);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_worker_t, blocks_before, 116);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_worker_t, entries_before, 128);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_worker_t, poll_false_returns, 144);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_worker_t, listener_id, 148);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_worker_t, rec_type, 152);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_worker_t, flags, 159);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_worker_t, t_countdown_notified, 160);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_worker_t, reserved, 168);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_worker_t, reserved_v4, 184);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_worker_t, reserved_v5, 248);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_worker_t, reserved_v7, 312);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_worker_t, commit, 376);
+
+static_assert(sizeof(rec_cycle_t) == 384 && alignof(rec_cycle_t) == 64, "REC_CYCLE");
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_cycle_t, cpu_ns, 56);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_cycle_t, d_upstream, 64);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_cycle_t, d_wcls_done_wait, 88);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_cycle_t, n_logit_chks, 104);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_cycle_t, vocab_size, 124);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_cycle_t, rec_type, 128);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_cycle_t, flags, 130);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_cycle_t, final_notifier_ix, 132);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_cycle_t, t_final_notifier_pre_release, 136);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_cycle_t, final_notifier_cycle_id, 144);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_cycle_t, final_notifier_src, 152);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_cycle_t, o1_substage_pad, 153);
+G2_OFF_ASSERT(rec_cycle_t, d_o1_before_run_forward, 156);

+// ===========================================================================
+// §3.1 / §3.2 stamps
+// ===========================================================================
+
+// The single sanctioned stamp sequence, uniform at every site (§3.1 row 1;
+// open decision D-10 keeps the serialized form as the draft's choice).
+inline uint64_t tsc() noexcept {
+  __builtin_ia32_lfence();
+  return __rdtsc();
+}
+
+// §3.2: not vDSO-served on Linux x86-64, so this IS a syscall. Called only at
+// the anchors field 16 names. Returns 0 on failure; the caller counts it.
+inline uint64_t cpu_ns_now(bool* ok) noexcept {
+  struct timespec ts;
+  if (clock_gettime(CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID, &ts) != 0) {
+    *ok = false;
+    return 0;
+  }
+  *ok = true;
+  return uint64_t(ts.tv_sec) * 1000000000ull + uint64_t(ts.tv_nsec);
+}
+
+inline bool clock_ns(clockid_t clock, uint64_t* out) noexcept {
+  struct timespec ts;
+  if (clock_gettime(clock, &ts) != 0) {
+    *out = 0;
+    return false;
+  }
+  *out = uint64_t(ts.tv_sec) * 1000000000ull + uint64_t(ts.tv_nsec);
+  return true;
+}
+
+inline uint64_t mono_raw_ns() noexcept {
+  uint64_t out = 0;
+  (void)clock_ns(CLOCK_MONOTONIC_RAW, &out);
+  return out;
+}
+
+inline uint64_t realtime_ns() noexcept {
+  uint64_t out = 0;
+  (void)clock_ns(CLOCK_REALTIME, &out);
+  return out;
+}
+
+inline uint32_t gettid_u32() noexcept {
+  return uint32_t(syscall(SYS_gettid));
+}
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// §5.6 FNV-1a-64 over the record's bytes [0,kCommitOffset). Byte-wise, matching
+// ring_parse.py fnv1a64() exactly (offset basis and prime included).
+// ===========================================================================
+inline uint64_t fnv1a64(const void* p, size_t n) noexcept {
+  const uint8_t* b = static_cast<const uint8_t*>(p);
+  uint64_t h = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
+  for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+    h ^= uint64_t(b[i]);
+    h *= 0x100000001b3ull;
+  }
+  return h;
+}
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// SHA-256 (FIPS 180-4). Needed for the header's exe_sha256 (V4) and
+// zerofill_digest (§8.3 arm-D payload proof). Init/dump only; never hot.
+// ===========================================================================
+struct sha256_t {
+  uint32_t h[8];
+  uint64_t bitlen;
+  uint8_t buf[64];
+  size_t buflen;
+};
+
+inline uint32_t sha_ror(uint32_t x, uint32_t n) noexcept {
+  return (x >> n) | (x << (32u - n));
+}
+
+inline void sha256_init(sha256_t* c) noexcept {
+  c->h[0] = 0x6a09e667u;
+  c->h[1] = 0xbb67ae85u;
+  c->h[2] = 0x3c6ef372u;
+  c->h[3] = 0xa54ff53au;
+  c->h[4] = 0x510e527fu;
+  c->h[5] = 0x9b05688cu;
+  c->h[6] = 0x1f83d9abu;
+  c->h[7] = 0x5be0cd19u;
+  c->bitlen = 0;
+  c->buflen = 0;
+}
+
+inline void sha256_block(sha256_t* c, const uint8_t* p) noexcept {
+  static const uint32_t K[64] = {0x428a2f98u, 0x71374491u, 0xb5c0fbcfu, 0xe9b5dba5u,
+      0x3956c25bu, 0x59f111f1u, 0x923f82a4u, 0xab1c5ed5u, 0xd807aa98u, 0x12835b01u,
+      0x243185beu, 0x550c7dc3u, 0x72be5d74u, 0x80deb1feu, 0x9bdc06a7u, 0xc19bf174u,
+      0xe49b69c1u, 0xefbe4786u, 0x0fc19dc6u, 0x240ca1ccu, 0x2de92c6fu, 0x4a7484aau,
+      0x5cb0a9dcu, 0x76f988dau, 0x983e5152u, 0xa831c66du, 0xb00327c8u, 0xbf597fc7u,
+      0xc6e00bf3u, 0xd5a79147u, 0x06ca6351u, 0x14292967u, 0x27b70a85u, 0x2e1b2138u,
+      0x4d2c6dfcu, 0x53380d13u, 0x650a7354u, 0x766a0abbu, 0x81c2c92eu, 0x92722c85u,
+      0xa2bfe8a1u, 0xa81a664bu, 0xc24b8b70u, 0xc76c51a3u, 0xd192e819u, 0xd6990624u,
+      0xf40e3585u, 0x106aa070u, 0x19a4c116u, 0x1e376c08u, 0x2748774cu, 0x34b0bcb5u,
+      0x391c0cb3u, 0x4ed8aa4au, 0x5b9cca4fu, 0x682e6ff3u, 0x748f82eeu, 0x78a5636fu,
+      0x84c87814u, 0x8cc70208u, 0x90befffau, 0xa4506cebu, 0xbef9a3f7u, 0xc67178f2u};
+  uint32_t w[64];
+  for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i)
+    w[i] = (uint32_t(p[i * 4]) << 24) | (uint32_t(p[i * 4 + 1]) << 16) |
+           (uint32_t(p[i * 4 + 2]) << 8) | uint32_t(p[i * 4 + 3]);
+  for (int i = 16; i < 64; ++i) {
+    uint32_t s0 = sha_ror(w[i - 15], 7) ^ sha_ror(w[i - 15], 18) ^ (w[i - 15] >> 3);
+    uint32_t s1 = sha_ror(w[i - 2], 17) ^ sha_ror(w[i - 2], 19) ^ (w[i - 2] >> 10);
+    w[i] = w[i - 16] + s0 + w[i - 7] + s1;
+  }
+  uint32_t a = c->h[0], b = c->h[1], cc = c->h[2], d = c->h[3];
+  uint32_t e = c->h[4], f = c->h[5], gg = c->h[6], hh = c->h[7];
+  for (int i = 0; i < 64; ++i) {
+    uint32_t S1 = sha_ror(e, 6) ^ sha_ror(e, 11) ^ sha_ror(e, 25);
+    uint32_t ch = (e & f) ^ ((~e) & gg);
+    uint32_t t1 = hh + S1 + ch + K[i] + w[i];
+    uint32_t S0 = sha_ror(a, 2) ^ sha_ror(a, 13) ^ sha_ror(a, 22);
+    uint32_t maj = (a & b) ^ (a & cc) ^ (b & cc);
+    uint32_t t2 = S0 + maj;
+    hh = gg;
+    gg = f;
+    f = e;
+    e = d + t1;
+    d = cc;
+    cc = b;
+    b = a;
+    a = t1 + t2;
+  }
+  c->h[0] += a;
+  c->h[1] += b;
+  c->h[2] += cc;
+  c->h[3] += d;
+  c->h[4] += e;
+  c->h[5] += f;
+  c->h[6] += gg;
+  c->h[7] += hh;
+}
+
+inline void sha256_update(sha256_t* c, const void* data, size_t n) noexcept {
+  const uint8_t* p = static_cast<const uint8_t*>(data);
+  c->bitlen += uint64_t(n) * 8ull;
+  while (n > 0) {
+    if (c->buflen == 0 && n >= 64) {
+      sha256_block(c, p);
+      p += 64;
+      n -= 64;
+      continue;
+    }
+    size_t take = 64 - c->buflen;
+    if (take > n) take = n;
+    std::memcpy(c->buf + c->buflen, p, take);
+    c->buflen += take;
+    p += take;
+    n -= take;
+    if (c->buflen == 64) {
+      sha256_block(c, c->buf);
+      c->buflen = 0;
+    }
+  }
+}
+
+inline void sha256_final(sha256_t* c, uint8_t out[32]) noexcept {
+  const uint64_t bl = c->bitlen;  // message length, before padding
+  const uint8_t one = 0x80;
+  sha256_update(c, &one, 1);
+  c->bitlen = bl;
+  const uint8_t zero = 0;
+  while (c->buflen != 56) {
+    sha256_update(c, &zero, 1);
+    c->bitlen = bl;
+  }
+  uint8_t len[8];
+  for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i)
+    len[i] = uint8_t(bl >> (56 - 8 * i));
+  sha256_update(c, len, 8);
+  for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
+    out[i * 4 + 0] = uint8_t(c->h[i] >> 24);
+    out[i * 4 + 1] = uint8_t(c->h[i] >> 16);
+    out[i * 4 + 2] = uint8_t(c->h[i] >> 8);
+    out[i * 4 + 3] = uint8_t(c->h[i]);
+  }
+}
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// §2 the one named shared line set: the wcls_done_tsc publication slots.
+// One 64-B-padded slot per logit chunk. Orchestrator: release store, once per
+// chunk per cycle. Workers: relaxed load at consume entry. Outside every ring
+// region (§2 last row), so no ring region ever shares a line with them.
+// ===========================================================================
+struct alignas(64) wcls_slot_t {
+  std::atomic<uint64_t> v;
+  uint8_t pad[56];
+};
+static_assert(sizeof(wcls_slot_t) == 64, "one slot per cache line (§2)");
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// §3.1 cross-thread TSC coherence probe cells (one per worker region).
+// ===========================================================================
+struct alignas(64) probe_cell_t {
+  std::atomic<uint64_t> a;        // worker pre-stamp
+  std::atomic<uint64_t> b;        // orchestrator reply stamp
+  std::atomic<uint32_t> turn;     // 0 idle, 1 worker waiting, 2 orch replied
+  std::atomic<uint32_t> engaged;  // worker has arrived
+  std::atomic<uint32_t> done;     // exchange finished (either side)
+  uint8_t pad[28];
+};
+static_assert(sizeof(probe_cell_t) == 64, "probe cell is one line");
+
+// One cache line per concurrent lane. Every worker performs one fetch_or on
+// seen_mask. Only the worker that completes expected_mask reads TSC and stores
+// last_tsc. The existing workers_done latch publishes both atomics to the
+// orchestrator before it returns from wait().
+struct alignas(64) o1c_lane_shared_t {
+  std::atomic<uint64_t> seen_mask;
+  std::atomic<uint64_t> last_tsc;
+  uint8_t pad[48];
+};
+static_assert(sizeof(o1c_lane_shared_t) == 64, "one O1c lane per cache line");
+
+// Each helper writes only its own row. The existing workers_done latch
+// publishes all plain row stores to the orchestrator before wait() returns.
+// Epoch tagging avoids clearing 64 cache lines on every forward.
+inline constexpr uint32_t kO1cHelperCheckpointCount = 4;
+struct alignas(64) o1c_helper_checkpoint_row_t {
+  uint64_t epoch;
+  uint64_t returned_tsc[kO1cHelperCheckpointCount];
+  uint32_t seen_mask;
+  uint8_t pad[20];
+};
+static_assert(sizeof(o1c_helper_checkpoint_row_t) == 64,
+    "one private helper checkpoint row per cache line");
+
+struct alignas(64) o1c_shared_t {
+  std::atomic<uint64_t> active_epoch;   // release-published after CONFIG
+  std::atomic<uint32_t> async_anomaly;  // worker-set bits, merged at join/C
+  uint32_t expected_attention;
+  uint32_t expected_helpers;
+  uint32_t configured;
+  uint64_t generation;  // orchestrator-only; zero is reserved
+  uint64_t expected_attention_mask;
+  uint64_t expected_helper_mask;
+  uint32_t expected_helper_checkpoints;  // zero or kO1cHelperCheckpointCount
+  uint8_t pad[12];
+};
+static_assert(sizeof(o1c_shared_t) == 64, "O1c shared control is one cache line");
+
+inline constexpr uint32_t kProbeIters = 1000;              // §3.1 "1000 ping-pong"
+inline constexpr uint64_t kProbeDeadline = 3000000000ull;  // ~1 s at 3 GHz
+inline constexpr int32_t kSkewFailSentinel = 30000;        // > 100 ns => V7 fatal
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// Per-region hot control block. Cache-line separated, single writer, not part
+// of the dump. Static storage, first-touched at init so arm D pays exactly
+// arm E's page-fault cost (§8.1 "allocation performed and first-touched in D
+// exactly as in E").
+// ===========================================================================
+struct alignas(64) region_ctl_t {
+  uint8_t* base;      // region base inside the mapping (page aligned)
+  region_hdr_t* hdr;  // == (region_hdr_t*)base
+  uint8_t* payload;   // == base + kRegionHdrSize
+  uint32_t region_ix;
+  uint32_t write_index;  // §5.1 thread-local write index: not shared, not atomic
+  uint64_t seq;          // §5.1 per-region monotonic sequence
+  bool recording;        // §8.2 the one per-region bool read at entry
+  bool attached;         // tid recorded + skew probe done
+  bool rec_open;         // a staging record is open
+  bool rx_after_flag;    // classification of the block currently in flight
+  bool cpu_entry_ok;
+  uint32_t stride;  // §11 rx_stamp_stride
+
+  // worker staging + §7.1 chain cursors
+  rec_worker_t w;
+  uint64_t worker_cycle_seq;    // region-local invocation counter
+  uint64_t s0;                  // this invocation's entry stamp
+  uint64_t cursor;              // last boundary stamp of the open record
+  uint64_t rx_entry;            // current block's entry stamp (0 = not sampled)
+  uint64_t prev_block_exit;     // last stamped block exit in THIS stream
+  uint64_t cpu_entry_ns;        // CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID at the record's t_entry
+  uint32_t stream_blocks;       // blocks this listener's stream will deliver
+  uint32_t stream_blocks_seen;  // blocks delivered so far in this stream
+
+  // orchestrator staging + §7.2 chain cursors
+  rec_cycle_t c;
+  uint64_t prev_return;  // previous cycle's t_return (0 before the first)
+  uint64_t prev_cpu_ns;  // previous cycle's CLOCK_THREAD_CPUTIME_ID reading
+  bool have_prev;        // false only before the first committed REC_CYCLE
+
+  // Record-v4 O1a..O1e staging. These fields are owned by the orchestrator
+  // thread and are not part of the dump. They persist across zero-logit
+  // forwards, whose elapsed time belongs to the next recorded cycle's O1.
+  uint64_t o1_cursor;
+  uint64_t o1_before_run_forward;
+  uint64_t o1_setup;
+  uint64_t o1_model_forward;
+  uint64_t o1_post_model;
+  uint64_t o1_pre_run_logits;
+  uint16_t o1_flags;
+  uint8_t o1_phase;
+
+  // Record-v4 O1c lane state. Active stamps are one-forward values. The u64
+  // sums/counts persist across zero-logit forwards until the next REC_CYCLE.
+  uint64_t o1c_b;
+  uint64_t o1c_dispatch;
+  uint64_t o1c_main_prejoin;
+  uint64_t o1c_join_return;
+  uint64_t o1c_epoch;
+  uint64_t o1c_helper_blend_return[kO1cHelperCheckpointCount];
+  uint64_t o1c_dispatch_sum;
+  uint64_t o1c_main_prejoin_sum;
+  uint64_t o1c_attention_done_sum;
+  uint64_t o1c_helper_done_sum;
+  uint64_t o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[kO1cHelperCheckpointCount];
+  uint64_t o1c_critical_ready_sum;
+  uint64_t o1c_join_tail_sum;
+  uint64_t o1c_attention_tasks;
+  uint64_t o1c_helper_tasks;
+  uint64_t o1c_forwards;
+  uint64_t o1c_zero_logit_forwards;
+  uint64_t o1c_attention_forwards;
+  uint64_t o1c_helper_forwards;
+  uint64_t o1c_main_critical;
+  uint64_t o1c_attention_critical;
+  uint64_t o1c_helper_critical;
+  uint64_t o1c_critical_ambiguous;
+  uint32_t o1c_helper_checkpoint_mode;  // accumulation mode: zero or four
+  uint8_t o1c_phase;
+
+  // Record-v7 O1a partition state. These values are owned by the registered
+  // work-queue/scheduler/model thread and are not serialized directly. They
+  // persist across zero-logit forwards and are cleared only after a successful
+  // normal REC_CYCLE commit or the clean terminal-prefix discard.
+  uint64_t o1a_cursor;
+  uint64_t o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafCount];
+  uint64_t o1a_count[kO1aCountCount];
+  uint64_t o1a_hit[kO1aHitCount];
+  const void* o1a_executor_ptr;
+  uint8_t o1a_phase;
+  uint8_t o1a_saved_phase;
+  uint8_t o1a_executor_kind;
+  bool o1a_wait_open;
+  bool o1a_queue_loop_open;
+  bool o1a_executor_open;
+  bool o1a_empty_return_pending;
+};
+static_assert(alignof(region_ctl_t) == 64, "control blocks are line separated");
+static_assert(sizeof(region_ctl_t) % 64 == 0, "no two control blocks share a line");
+
+// Written by the one task that claims the listeners_notified final decrement,
+// then read by the orchestrator after the latch's release/acquire hand-off.
+// It is outside the ring mapping and on its own cache line.
+struct alignas(64) final_notifier_slot_t {
+  uint64_t cycle_id;
+  uint64_t tsc;
+  uint32_t notifier_ix;
+  uint8_t pad[44];
+};
+static_assert(
+    sizeof(final_notifier_slot_t) == 64, "final notifier slot is one cache line");
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// Process-wide state. Namespace-scope inline variable, so it is
+// zero-initialised in BSS before any thread exists.
+// ===========================================================================
+struct global_t {
+  uint8_t* map;  // the 5-region mapping (kRingBytes)
+  region_ctl_t ctl[kNRegions];
+  wcls_slot_t wcls_done_tsc[kMaxChks];
+  probe_cell_t probe[kNWorkers];
+  probe_cell_t pool_probe[kMaxPoolWorkers];
+  int32_t pool_probe_skew_ns[kMaxPoolWorkers];
+  o1c_shared_t o1c;
+  o1c_lane_shared_t o1c_attention;
+  o1c_lane_shared_t o1c_helper;
+  o1c_helper_checkpoint_row_t o1c_helper_checkpoint[kMaxPoolWorkers];
+  bool pool_probe_done;
+  final_notifier_slot_t final_notifier;
+  std::atomic<uint64_t> cycle_id;  // §4 row 1, published by the orchestrator
+  std::atomic<uint32_t> initialised;
+  std::atomic<uint32_t> dumped;
+  uint32_t stride;
+  uint32_t n_notifier_tasks;  // = logits_chunk_size at init
+  uint32_t tsc_flags;
+  uint32_t calib_residual_ppm;
+  bool recording;
+  uint64_t calib_load_tsc, calib_load_mono, calib_load_real;
+  uint64_t calib_dump_tsc, calib_dump_mono, calib_dump_real;
+  uint64_t calib_load_rate_q32;  // ticks/ns, unsigned Q32 local fit
+  uint64_t calib_dump_rate_q32;  // ticks/ns, unsigned Q32 local fit
+  uint8_t exe_sha256[32];
+  uint8_t zerofill_digest[32];
+  char dir[256];
+  char run_id[40];
+
+  // §5.6 dump-path strings and the completion message, formed ONCE at init.
+  // They exist so dump() itself needs no snprintf: everything dump() does to
+  // produce ring.bin is then open/write/fsync/close plus clock_gettime and
+  // rdtsc, which is the async-signal-safe subset, so the same function can be
+  // reached from the crash path (dump_from_crash()). Appended at the END of
+  // global_t so every pre-existing member keeps its offset.
+  char path_bin[288];   // "<dir>/ring.bin"        (dir is 256 B incl. NUL)
+  char path_meta[288];  // "<dir>/ring.meta.json"
+  char msg_wrote[384];  // the exact success line, pre-formatted
+  uint32_t msg_wrote_len;
+
+  // Record-v7 deliberately permits one instrumented scheduler/model-state
+  // pair. The owner thread is bound once, at the first target V0 hook. The TLS
+  // anchor comparison makes later valid hot-path checks syscall-free.
+  const void* o1a_scheduler_owner;
+  const void* o1a_state_owner;
+  const void* o1a_thread_anchor;
+  uint32_t o1a_owner_tid;
+  bool o1a_owner_registered;
+  bool o1a_registration_failed;
+};
+
+inline global_t g;
+
+// The per-thread handle. A plain pointer with a constant initialiser, so no
+// TLS guard variable and no lazy-init branch at the access sites. It is set
+// only on the worker path, and only in arm E: in arm D it stays null, which
+// is what makes every fill_logits_buffer hook a single null test.
+inline thread_local region_ctl_t* tls = nullptr;
+inline thread_local uint8_t o1a_thread_anchor_tls = 0;
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// Duration helpers. Every u32 duration field goes through these, so both
+// saturation (§5.4 saturated_durations) and negative deltas (clock_anomalies)
+// are COUNTED, never silently wrapped. Any nonzero counter is fatal to the
+// draw under V7 — these are fail-loud paths, not repair paths.
+// ===========================================================================
+inline uint32_t delta32(region_ctl_t* c,
+    uint64_t from,
+    uint64_t to,
+    uint8_t* wflags,
+    uint16_t* cflags) noexcept {
+  if (to < from) {
+    ++c->hdr->clock_anomalies;
+    if (wflags) *wflags |= kWFlagClockAnomaly;
+    if (cflags) *cflags |= kCFlagClockAnomaly;
+    return 0;
+  }
+  uint64_t d = to - from;
+  if (d > 0xFFFFFFFFull) {
+    ++c->hdr->saturated_durations;
+    if (wflags) *wflags |= kWFlagSaturated;
+    if (cflags) *cflags |= kCFlagSaturated;
+    return 0xFFFFFFFFu;
+  }
+  return uint32_t(d);
+}
+
+inline void accum32(region_ctl_t* c,
+    uint32_t* field,
+    uint64_t from,
+    uint64_t to,
+    uint8_t* wflags,
+    uint16_t* cflags) noexcept {
+  uint64_t sum = uint64_t(*field) + delta32(c, from, to, wflags, cflags);
+  if (sum > 0xFFFFFFFFull) {
+    ++c->hdr->saturated_durations;
+    if (wflags) *wflags |= kWFlagSaturated;
+    if (cflags) *cflags |= kCFlagSaturated;
+    sum = 0xFFFFFFFFull;
+  }
+  *field = uint32_t(sum);
+}
+
+inline uint32_t addsat32(uint32_t a, uint64_t b) noexcept {
+  uint64_t s = uint64_t(a) + b;
+  return (s > 0xFFFFFFFFull) ? 0xFFFFFFFFu : uint32_t(s);
+}
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// §5.5 publication: payload bytes [0,kCommitOffset) as plain stores, then commit
+// word as a release store, then published_count with a release store. The
+// __atomic_* builtins are used on the mapped plain-typed words so the mapped
+// structs stay standard-layout and byte-exact (pinned toolchain: clang-19,
+// §10.2; gcc supports the same builtins).
+// ===========================================================================
+inline bool commit_record(region_ctl_t* c, const void* staging) noexcept {
+  if (c->write_index >= kCapacity) {  // §5.4 NO WRAP: count and drop
+    ++c->hdr->records_dropped;
+    return false;
+  }
+  uint8_t* slot = c->payload + size_t(c->write_index) * kRecSize;
+  std::memcpy(slot, staging, kCommitOffset);
+  uint64_t sum = fnv1a64(slot, kCommitOffset);
+  __atomic_store_n(
+      reinterpret_cast<uint64_t*>(slot + kCommitOffset), sum, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
+  ++c->write_index;
+  ++c->hdr->records_written;
+  __atomic_store_n(
+      &c->hdr->published_count, uint64_t(c->write_index), __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
+  return true;
+}
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// Initialisation (§2 preallocated, §3.3 calibration; unconditional in D)
+// ===========================================================================
+inline void read_tsc_flags() noexcept {
+  g.tsc_flags |= 0x4u;  // bit2: lfence-serialized stamps (always true here)
+  FILE* f = std::fopen("/proc/cpuinfo", "r");
+  if (!f) return;
+  char line[4096];
+  while (std::fgets(line, sizeof(line), f)) {
+    if (std::strncmp(line, "flags", 5) != 0) continue;
+    if (std::strstr(line, "constant_tsc")) g.tsc_flags |= 0x1u;
+    if (std::strstr(line, "nonstop_tsc")) g.tsc_flags |= 0x2u;
+    break;
+  }
+  std::fclose(f);
+}
+
+// §3.3 rule 2: one calibration point = the median-round-trip sample of 1000
+// interleaved (rdtsc, CLOCK_MONOTONIC_RAW, CLOCK_REALTIME) reads. Selecting
+// the median-latency triple (rather than three independent medians) keeps the
+// three values one coherent simultaneous observation.
+inline bool sample_calib(
+    uint64_t* out_tsc, uint64_t* out_mono, uint64_t* out_real) noexcept {
+  static const int kN = 1000;
+  static uint64_t rt[kN], ts[kN], mo[kN], re[kN];  // init/dump only, not hot
+  int valid = 0;
+  for (int i = 0; i < kN; ++i) {
+    uint64_t t0 = tsc();
+    uint64_t m = 0, r = 0;
+    bool ok_m = clock_ns(CLOCK_MONOTONIC_RAW, &m);
+    bool ok_r = clock_ns(CLOCK_REALTIME, &r);
+    uint64_t t1 = tsc();
+    if (!ok_m || !ok_r || t1 < t0) continue;
+    rt[valid] = t1 - t0;
+    ts[valid] = t0 + rt[valid] / 2;
+    mo[valid] = m;
+    re[valid] = r;
+    ++valid;
+  }
+  if (valid == 0) {
+    *out_tsc = *out_mono = *out_real = 0;
+    return false;
+  }
+  // Index of the median round trip, by nearest rank on the total order
+  // (rt[j], j). Ranking on rt alone is not robust: round-trip tick counts tie
+  // heavily, and when they do no sample has exactly kN/2 strictly-smaller
+  // peers, so the loop would fall through and silently keep sample 0. Adding
+  // the index tie-break makes i -> rank a bijection onto [0, kN), so exactly
+  // one sample has rank kN/2 for any input. Allocation-free and deterministic;
+  // O(kN^2) is fine at init/dump only.
+  int idx = 0;
+  for (int i = 0; i < valid; ++i) {
+    int rank = 0;
+    for (int j = 0; j < valid; ++j)
+      rank += (rt[j] < rt[i] || (rt[j] == rt[i] && j < i)) ? 1 : 0;
+    if (rank == valid / 2) {
+      idx = i;
+      break;
+    }
+  }
+  *out_tsc = ts[idx];
+  *out_mono = mo[idx];
+  *out_real = re[idx];
+  return true;
+}
+
+// One endpoint for a local-rate sample: choose the smallest of 16
+// TSC-clock-TSC brackets and use its TSC midpoint.
+inline bool rate_endpoint(uint64_t* out_tsc, uint64_t* out_mono) noexcept {
+  uint64_t best = ~uint64_t(0), best_tsc = 0, best_mono = 0;
+  for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i) {
+    uint64_t t0 = tsc(), mono = 0;
+    bool ok = clock_ns(CLOCK_MONOTONIC_RAW, &mono);
+    uint64_t t1 = tsc();
+    if (!ok || t1 < t0 || t1 - t0 >= best) continue;
+    best = t1 - t0;
+    best_tsc = t0 + best / 2;
+    best_mono = mono;
+  }
+  if (best == ~uint64_t(0)) return false;
+  *out_tsc = best_tsc;
+  *out_mono = best_mono;
+  return true;
+}
+
+inline bool sleep_calib_window() noexcept {
+  struct timespec req;
+  req.tv_sec = 0;
+  req.tv_nsec = long(kCalibWindowMs) * 1000000l;
+  struct timespec rem;
+  while (nanosleep(&req, &rem) != 0) {
+    if (errno != EINTR) return false;
+    req = rem;
+  }
+  return true;
+}
+
+// Median of five 100 ms local fits. Q32 avoids writer/parser disagreement
+// from floating-point rounding. Any failed sample fails the measurement.
+inline uint64_t local_rate_q32() noexcept {
+  uint64_t rates[kCalibSamples];
+  for (uint16_t i = 0; i < kCalibSamples; ++i) {
+    uint64_t t0 = 0, m0 = 0, t1 = 0, m1 = 0;
+    if (!rate_endpoint(&t0, &m0) || !sleep_calib_window() || !rate_endpoint(&t1, &m1) ||
+        t1 <= t0 || m1 <= m0)
+      return 0;
+    unsigned __int128 num = static_cast<unsigned __int128>(t1 - t0) << 32;
+    unsigned __int128 q = num / (m1 - m0);
+    if (q == 0 || q > ~uint64_t(0)) return 0;
+    rates[i] = uint64_t(q);
+  }
+  for (uint16_t i = 1; i < kCalibSamples; ++i) {
+    uint64_t v = rates[i];
+    uint16_t j = i;
+    while (j > 0 && rates[j - 1] > v) {
+      rates[j] = rates[j - 1];
+      --j;
+    }
+    rates[j] = v;
+  }
+  return rates[kCalibSamples / 2];
+}
+
+inline uint32_t calibration_ppm(
+    uint64_t d_tsc, uint64_t d_mono, uint64_t rate_q32) noexcept {
+  if (d_tsc == 0 || d_mono == 0 || rate_q32 == 0) return 0xFFFFFFFFu;
+  unsigned __int128 a = static_cast<unsigned __int128>(d_tsc) << 32;
+  unsigned __int128 b = static_cast<unsigned __int128>(d_mono) * rate_q32;
+  if (b == 0) return 0xFFFFFFFFu;
+  unsigned __int128 diff = (a >= b) ? (a - b) : (b - a);
+  unsigned __int128 ppm = (diff * 1000000u + b - 1) / b;
+  return ppm > 0xFFFFFFFFu ? 0xFFFFFFFFu : uint32_t(ppm);
+}
+
+inline double q32_to_double(uint64_t q) noexcept {
+  return double(q) / 4294967296.0;
+}
+
+// V4 input. ~0.5 s for the 520 MB executable; model load only, never hot.
+inline void hash_self_exe() noexcept {
+  std::memset(g.exe_sha256, 0, sizeof(g.exe_sha256));
+  int fd = open("/proc/self/exe", O_RDONLY);
+  if (fd < 0) return;
+  static uint8_t buf[1 << 16];
+  sha256_t c;
+  sha256_init(&c);
+  for (;;) {
+    ssize_t n = read(fd, buf, sizeof(buf));
+    if (n < 0) {
+      if (errno == EINTR) continue;
+      close(fd);
+      std::memset(g.exe_sha256, 0, sizeof(g.exe_sha256));
+      return;
+    }
+    if (n == 0) break;
+    sha256_update(&c, buf, size_t(n));
+  }
+  close(fd);
+  sha256_final(&c, g.exe_sha256);
+}
+
+// Forward declarations for the atexit hookup. The attributes are repeated on
+// the definitions below; they are stated here because this is the first
+// declaration clang sees.
+inline __attribute__((noinline)) void dump() noexcept;
+inline __attribute__((noinline)) void dump_from_crash() noexcept;
+
+// write(2) of a NUL-terminated literal or of an already-formed buffer. Used
+// for every §5.6 dump diagnostic instead of fprintf, because stdio takes a
+// lock and may allocate, and dump() must remain callable from the crash path
+// (§5.6 "residual limits"). strlen/write are both async-signal-safe.
+inline void emit(const char* s) noexcept {
+  size_t n = 0;
+  while (s[n] != '\0')
+    ++n;
+  ssize_t w = write(2, s, n);
+  (void)w;  // a lost diagnostic must never change control flow
+}
+
+inline void env_copy(
+    char* dst, size_t cap, const char* name, const char* dflt) noexcept {
+  const char* v = std::getenv(name);
+  if (!v) v = dflt;
+  std::memset(dst, 0, cap);
+  std::strncpy(dst, v, cap - 1);
+}
+
+// One call site: the model `state` constructor (§8.2 "model load, once").
+// Shared allocation and framing run in both arms. Recording fields and O1c
+// ambiguity metadata are populated only in arm E; arm D leaves them zero.
+inline void init(uint32_t notifier_tasks) noexcept {
+  uint32_t expected = 0;
+  if (!g.initialised.compare_exchange_strong(expected, 1, std::memory_order_acq_rel))
+    return;  // a second model instance in one process: keep the first ring
+
+  const char* rec = std::getenv("TRON_DIAG_RING_RECORD");
+  g.recording = (rec != nullptr && std::strcmp(rec, "1") == 0);  // §8.2 exact "1"
+  const char* st = std::getenv("TRON_DIAG_RING_STRIDE");
+  long sv = st ? std::atol(st) : 1;
+  g.stride = (sv >= 1 && sv <= 1000000) ? uint32_t(sv) : 1;
+  env_copy(g.dir, sizeof(g.dir), "TRON_DIAG_RING_DIR", ".");
+  env_copy(g.run_id, sizeof(g.run_id), "TRON_DIAG_RING_RUN_ID", "");
+  g.n_notifier_tasks = notifier_tasks;
+
+  // §5.6: form the output paths and the success line HERE, so dump() carries
+  // no snprintf. Both file names and all three interpolated values (path,
+  // kFileSize, arm letter) are already known at model load.
+  std::snprintf(g.path_bin, sizeof(g.path_bin), "%s/ring.bin", g.dir);
+  std::snprintf(g.path_meta, sizeof(g.path_meta), "%s/ring.meta.json", g.dir);
+  {
+    int ml = std::snprintf(g.msg_wrote, sizeof(g.msg_wrote),
+        "[g2ring] wrote %s (%llu B, arm %c)\n", g.path_bin,
+        (unsigned long long)kFileSize, g.recording ? 'E' : 'D');
+    g.msg_wrote_len = (ml > 0 && size_t(ml) < sizeof(g.msg_wrote)) ? uint32_t(ml) : 0u;
+  }
+
+  // §2: one MAP_POPULATE anonymous mapping, zero filled, at model load and
+  // before the first forward. Regions are page aligned by construction
+  // (stride = 1537 pages), so no two regions share a page, hence no two share
+  // a cache line. The 256-B file header and 16-B trailer are file-only
+  // framing written at dump time; they are not part of the mapping.
+  void* p = mmap(nullptr, size_t(kRingBytes), PROT_READ | PROT_WRITE,
+      MAP_PRIVATE | MAP_ANONYMOUS | MAP_POPULATE, -1, 0);
+  if (p == MAP_FAILED) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "[g2ring] mmap of %llu B failed; ring disabled\n",
+        (unsigned long long)kRingBytes);
+    g.map = nullptr;
+    g.recording = false;
+    return;
+  }
+  g.map = static_cast<uint8_t*>(p);
+  std::memset(g.map, 0, size_t(kRingBytes));  // explicit first touch
+
+  for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kNRegions; ++i) {
+    region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[i];
+    std::memset(c, 0, sizeof(*c));  // first touch of the control block
+    c->base = g.map + size_t(i) * size_t(kRegionStride);
+    c->hdr = reinterpret_cast<region_hdr_t*>(c->base);
+    c->payload = c->base + kRegionHdrSize;
+    c->region_ix = i;
+    c->recording = g.recording;
+    c->stride = g.stride;
+    c->hdr->worker_ix = i;  // 4 for the orchestrator region
+    c->hdr->region_kind = (i == kOrchRegion) ? kKindOrch : kKindWorker;
+    c->hdr->recording = g.recording ? 1u : 0u;
+  }
+  for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kMaxChks; ++i)
+    g.wcls_done_tsc[i].v.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kMaxPoolWorkers; ++i) {
+    g.pool_probe[i].a.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    g.pool_probe[i].b.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    g.pool_probe[i].turn.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    g.pool_probe[i].engaged.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    g.pool_probe[i].done.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    g.pool_probe_skew_ns[i] = kSkewFailSentinel;
+    std::memset(&g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[i], 0, sizeof(g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[i]));
+  }
+  g.o1c.active_epoch.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  g.o1c.async_anomaly.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  g.o1c.expected_helper_checkpoints = 0;
+  __atomic_store_n(&g.o1c.configured, 0u, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
+  g.o1c_attention.seen_mask.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  g.o1c_attention.last_tsc.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  g.o1c_helper.seen_mask.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  g.o1c_helper.last_tsc.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  g.cycle_id.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+
+  read_tsc_flags();
+  if (g.stride > 1) g.tsc_flags |= 0x8u;  // header bit3: stride active
+
+  // §8.3 D-proof input: SHA-256 of one region's zero payload, computed here
+  // over the freshly zeroed bytes and over exactly kPayloadBytes.
+  {
+    sha256_t sc;
+    sha256_init(&sc);
+    sha256_update(&sc, g.ctl[0].payload, size_t(kPayloadBytes));
+    sha256_final(&sc, g.zerofill_digest);
+  }
+
+  hash_self_exe();
+  g.calib_load_rate_q32 = local_rate_q32();
+  (void)sample_calib(&g.calib_load_tsc, &g.calib_load_mono, &g.calib_load_real);
+  if (g.recording) {
+    unsigned __int128 scaled =
+        static_cast<unsigned __int128>(g.calib_load_rate_q32) * kCriticalAmbiguityNs;
+    uint64_t ticks = uint64_t((scaled + 0xFFFFFFFFull) >> 32);
+    region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+    if (ticks == 0 || ticks > 0xFFFFFFFFull) {
+      ++c->hdr->lane_anomalies;
+      c->o1_flags |= kCFlagO1cLaneAnomaly;
+      ticks = ticks == 0 ? 1 : 0xFFFFFFFFull;
+    }
+    c->hdr->o1c_ambiguity_ticks = uint32_t(ticks);
+  }
+
+  // §5.6 trigger: this atexit registration is the BACKSTOP, not the primary
+  // trigger. The primary trigger is the explicit tron_diag_ring_dump() call in
+  // runtron's main() after eval() returns. Both are safe because dump() is
+  // idempotent (the g.dumped compare-exchange), and the backstop is kept so a
+  // caller other than runtron (a test binary, rinzler) still produces a dump.
+  std::atexit(&dump);
+
+  std::fprintf(stderr,
+      "[g2ring] arm=%s stride=%u detailed_workers=%u notifier_tasks=%u "
+      "dir=%s ring=%llu B rate=%.6f t/ns\n",
+      g.recording ? "E" : "D", g.stride, kNWorkers, g.n_notifier_tasks, g.dir,
+      (unsigned long long)kRingBytes, q32_to_double(g.calib_load_rate_q32));
+  g.initialised.store(2, std::memory_order_release);
+}
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// §3.1 TSC coherence probe. It runs once, at the first recorded cycle,
+// because the pulled source set contains no model-load hook that executes
+// after the app-pool threads exist (design notes DEV-3). Bounded and
+// fail-closed: a partner that never arrives leaves the sentinel skew, which
+// trips V7 and invalidates the draw. It runs in arm D as well as arm E, and
+// it completes before this cycle's first stamp, so it perturbs no interval.
+// ===========================================================================
+inline int32_t ticks_to_ns(uint64_t ticks) noexcept {
+  double measured = q32_to_double(g.calib_load_rate_q32);
+  double r = (measured > 0.0) ? measured : 3.0;
+  double ns = double(ticks) / r;
+  if (ns > 2000000000.0) ns = 2000000000.0;
+  return int32_t(ns);
+}
+
+inline void probe_worker_side(uint32_t k) noexcept {
+  probe_cell_t& pc = g.probe[k];
+  uint64_t worst = 0;
+  bool orch_ahead = false;
+  uint64_t start = tsc();
+  pc.engaged.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
+  for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kProbeIters; ++i) {
+    uint64_t A = tsc();
+    pc.a.store(A, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    pc.turn.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
+    while (pc.turn.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != 2) {
+      if (tsc() - start > kProbeDeadline) {
+        g.ctl[k].hdr->tsc_cross_thread_skew_ns = kSkewFailSentinel;
+        pc.done.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
+        return;
+      }
+      __builtin_ia32_pause();
+    }
+    uint64_t C = tsc();
+    uint64_t B = pc.b.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    // The orchestrator's stamp B must satisfy A <= B <= C if the two clocks
+    // agree; any excursion is a lower bound on |skew| (§3.1 "implied minimum
+    // cross-thread skew").
+    if (B > C && (B - C) > worst) {
+      worst = B - C;
+      orch_ahead = true;
+    }
+    if (B < A && (A - B) > worst) {
+      worst = A - B;
+      orch_ahead = false;
+    }
+    pc.turn.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
+  }
+  pc.done.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
+  int32_t ns = ticks_to_ns(worst);
+  g.ctl[k].hdr->tsc_cross_thread_skew_ns = orch_ahead ? ns : -ns;
+}
+
+inline void probe_orch_side() noexcept {
+  for (uint32_t k = 0; k < kNWorkers; ++k) {
+    probe_cell_t& pc = g.probe[k];
+    uint64_t start = tsc();
+    bool arrived = true;
+    while (pc.engaged.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == 0) {
+      if (tsc() - start > kProbeDeadline) {
+        g.ctl[k].hdr->tsc_cross_thread_skew_ns = kSkewFailSentinel;
+        pc.done.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
+        arrived = false;
+        break;
+      }
+      __builtin_ia32_pause();
+    }
+    if (!arrived) continue;
+    while (pc.done.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == 0) {
+      if (pc.turn.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == 1) {
+        pc.b.store(tsc(), std::memory_order_relaxed);
+        pc.turn.store(2, std::memory_order_release);
+      } else {
+        __builtin_ia32_pause();
+      }
+      if (tsc() - start > 2 * kProbeDeadline) {
+        g.ctl[k].hdr->tsc_cross_thread_skew_ns = kSkewFailSentinel;
+        break;
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  g.ctl[kOrchRegion].hdr->tsc_cross_thread_skew_ns = 0;  // the reference clock
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(region_ctl_t* c, bool clock, bool saturated) noexcept;
+inline uint64_t o1c_mask(uint32_t n) noexcept;
+inline bool o1a_state_owner_gate(const void* state_owner, bool require_thread) noexcept;
+
+// The O1c endpoints may be written by every app-pool worker, not only the four
+// detailed logits workers. Probe the complete pool once, during the first
+// recording-on O1c forward, before any real plugin task is enqueued.
+inline bool o1c_pool_probe_needed(const void* state_owner) noexcept {
+  return o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, true) && !g.pool_probe_done;
+}
+
+inline void o1c_pool_probe_worker(const void* state_owner, uint32_t k) noexcept {
+  if (!g.recording) return;  // arm D: guard the worker entry itself
+  if (!o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, false)) return;
+  if (k >= kMaxPoolWorkers) return;
+  probe_cell_t& pc = g.pool_probe[k];
+  uint64_t worst = 0;
+  bool orch_ahead = false;
+  uint64_t start = tsc();
+  pc.engaged.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
+  for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kPoolProbeIters; ++i) {
+    uint64_t A = tsc();
+    pc.a.store(A, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    pc.turn.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
+    while (pc.turn.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != 2) {
+      if (tsc() - start > kProbeDeadline) {
+        g.pool_probe_skew_ns[k] = kSkewFailSentinel;
+        pc.done.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
+        return;
+      }
+      __builtin_ia32_pause();
+    }
+    uint64_t C = tsc();
+    uint64_t B = pc.b.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    if (B > C && B - C > worst) {
+      worst = B - C;
+      orch_ahead = true;
+    }
+    if (B < A && A - B > worst) {
+      worst = A - B;
+      orch_ahead = false;
+    }
+    pc.turn.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
+  }
+  int32_t ns = ticks_to_ns(worst);
+  g.pool_probe_skew_ns[k] = orch_ahead ? ns : -ns;
+  pc.done.store(1, std::memory_order_release);
+}
+
+inline void o1c_pool_probe_orchestrator(
+    const void* state_owner, uint32_t n_workers) noexcept {
+  if (g.map == nullptr || !g.recording || g.pool_probe_done) return;
+  if (!o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, true)) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  region_hdr_t* h = c->hdr;
+  h->pool_workers = n_workers;
+  h->pool_probe_covered = 0;
+  h->pool_probe_failures = 0;
+  h->max_abs_pool_skew_ns = 0;
+  h->covered_mask = 0;
+  if (n_workers == 0 || n_workers > kMaxPoolWorkers) {
+    ++h->pool_probe_failures;
+    orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c, false, false);
+    g.pool_probe_done = true;
+    return;
+  }
+
+  for (uint32_t k = 0; k < n_workers; ++k) {
+    probe_cell_t& pc = g.pool_probe[k];
+    uint64_t start = tsc();
+    bool arrived = true;
+    while (pc.engaged.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == 0) {
+      if (tsc() - start > kProbeDeadline) {
+        arrived = false;
+        break;
+      }
+      __builtin_ia32_pause();
+    }
+    if (!arrived) {
+      ++h->pool_probe_failures;
+      continue;
+    }
+    while (pc.done.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == 0) {
+      if (pc.turn.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == 1) {
+        pc.b.store(tsc(), std::memory_order_relaxed);
+        pc.turn.store(2, std::memory_order_release);
+      } else {
+        __builtin_ia32_pause();
+      }
+      if (tsc() - start > 2 * kProbeDeadline) break;
+    }
+    if (pc.done.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == 0) {
+      ++h->pool_probe_failures;
+      continue;
+    }
+    int32_t skew = g.pool_probe_skew_ns[k];
+    if (skew == kSkewFailSentinel) {
+      ++h->pool_probe_failures;
+      continue;
+    }
+    uint32_t abs_skew = skew < 0 ? uint32_t(-int64_t(skew)) : uint32_t(skew);
+    ++h->pool_probe_covered;
+    h->covered_mask |= uint64_t(1) << k;
+    if (abs_skew > uint32_t(h->max_abs_pool_skew_ns))
+      h->max_abs_pool_skew_ns = int32_t(abs_skew);
+    if (abs_skew > kPoolSkewLimitNs) ++h->pool_probe_failures;
+  }
+  if (h->pool_probe_covered != n_workers || h->covered_mask != o1c_mask(n_workers))
+    ++h->pool_probe_failures;
+  if (h->pool_probe_failures != 0) orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c, false, false);
+  g.pool_probe_done = true;
+}
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// Record-v7 parent O1, O1a, O1c-lane, and helper-checkpoint hooks.
+//
+// The staged durations persist across zero-logit forwards. Each boundary
+// advances one cursor, so O1a..O1e telescope exactly from the previous
+// recorded t_return to the next recorded t_entry. A phase mismatch is saved
+// as a fatal record flag; it is never repaired or silently zero-filled.
+// ===========================================================================
+
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1AwaitA = 0;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1AwaitB = 1;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1AwaitC = 2;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1AwaitD = 3;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1AwaitEntry = 4;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1AwaitZeroReturn = 5;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1AwaitRecordedReturn = 6;
+
+// Record-v7 O1a serialized phases 1..10 match the region-header terminal
+// phase contract. Values above 10 are internal-only closure states.
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aNoSeed = 0;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aRecordTail = 1;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aQbatchTeardown = 2;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aPreviousExit = 3;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aQueueHandoff = 4;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aCvWait = 5;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aRequestApply = 6;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aDispatchDelay = 7;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aForwardArgs = 8;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aForwardSetup = 9;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aStateEntry = 10;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aAwaitEntry = 11;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aAwaitRecordedReturn = 12;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aZeroLogitsBody = 13;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aTerminalIdle = 14;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aInvalid = 15;
+
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aLeafRecordTail = 0;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aLeafQbatchTeardown = 1;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aLeafPreviousExit = 2;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aLeafQueueHandoff = 3;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aLeafCvWait = 4;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aLeafRequestApply = 5;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aLeafDispatchDelay = 6;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aLeafForwardArgs = 7;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aLeafForwardSetup = 8;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aLeafStateEntry = 9;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aLeafZeroLogitsBody = 10;
+
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aCountWaits = 0;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aCountEmptySweeps = 1;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aCountQueueLoops = 2;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aCountOtherExecutors = 3;
+
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aHitP = 0;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aHitK = 1;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aHitQ = 2;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aHitU = 3;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aHitQ0 = 4;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aHitW0 = 5;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aHitW1 = 6;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aHitD = 7;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aHitX = 8;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aHitV0 = 9;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aHitV1 = 10;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aHitF = 11;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aHitG = 12;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aHitZ = 13;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aHitH = 14;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1aHitA = 15;
+
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1cIdle = 0;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1cAwaitConfig = 1;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1cConfigured = 2;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1cDispatched = 3;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1cMainAtJoin = 4;
+inline constexpr uint8_t kO1cJoined = 5;
+
+inline constexpr uint32_t kO1cAsyncEpoch = 0x01;
+inline constexpr uint32_t kO1cAsyncIndex = 0x02;
+inline constexpr uint32_t kO1cAsyncDuplicate = 0x04;
+inline constexpr uint32_t kO1cAsyncCheckpointOrder = 0x08;
+inline constexpr uint32_t kO1cAsyncCheckpointClock = 0x10;
+
+// o1c_phase and configured are plain fixed-layout fields, but the crash dump
+// may inspect them from a signal on another thread. Use the compiler atomics
+// at every access so the dump-time open-forward check never races a plain
+// load/store. They remain outside the serialized ring format.
+inline uint8_t o1c_phase_load(const region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  return __atomic_load_n(&c->o1c_phase, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
+}
+
+inline void o1c_phase_store(region_ctl_t* c, uint8_t phase) noexcept {
+  __atomic_store_n(&c->o1c_phase, phase, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
+}
+
+inline uint8_t o1a_phase_load(const region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  return __atomic_load_n(&c->o1a_phase, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
+}
+
+inline void o1a_phase_store(region_ctl_t* c, uint8_t phase) noexcept {
+  __atomic_store_n(&c->o1a_phase, phase, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
+}
+
+inline uint32_t o1c_configured_load() noexcept {
+  return __atomic_load_n(&g.o1c.configured, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
+}
+
+inline void o1c_configured_store(uint32_t configured) noexcept {
+  __atomic_store_n(&g.o1c.configured, configured, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1_mark_anomaly(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  ++c->hdr->clock_anomalies;
+  c->o1_flags |= kCFlagClockAnomaly | kCFlagO1SubstageAnomaly;
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(
+    region_ctl_t* c, bool clock = false, bool saturated = false) noexcept {
+  ++c->hdr->lane_anomalies;
+  c->o1_flags |= kCFlagO1cLaneAnomaly;
+  if (clock) {
+    ++c->hdr->clock_anomalies;
+    c->o1_flags |= kCFlagClockAnomaly;
+  }
+  if (saturated) {
+    ++c->hdr->saturated_durations;
+    c->o1_flags |= kCFlagSaturated;
+  }
+}
+
+// O1a abnormal paths may be reached by a wrong asynchronous owner or by a
+// crash-time closure check. Saturating compiler atomics avoid data races there;
+// valid marker paths use only plain single-writer state.
+inline void o1a_sat_inc(uint32_t* field) noexcept {
+  uint32_t old = __atomic_load_n(field, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
+  while (old != 0xFFFFFFFFu && !__atomic_compare_exchange_n(field, &old, old + 1, false,
+                                   __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE)) {
+  }
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_set_flags(region_ctl_t* c, uint16_t flags) noexcept {
+  __atomic_fetch_or(&c->o1_flags, flags, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL);
+  if (c->rec_open) __atomic_fetch_or(&c->c.flags, flags, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->recording) return;
+  o1a_sat_inc(&c->hdr->o1a_anomalies);
+  orch_o1a_set_flags(c, kCFlagO1aPartitionAnomaly);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_mark_owner_mismatch(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->recording) return;
+  o1a_sat_inc(&c->hdr->o1a_owner_mismatches);
+  orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_mark_cross_thread(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->recording) return;
+  o1a_sat_inc(&c->hdr->o1a_cross_thread_hooks);
+  orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_mark_count_overflow(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->recording) return;
+  o1a_sat_inc(&c->hdr->o1a_count_overflows);
+  orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_mark_clock(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->recording) return;
+  o1a_sat_inc(&c->hdr->clock_anomalies);
+  orch_o1a_set_flags(c, kCFlagClockAnomaly | kCFlagO1aPartitionAnomaly);
+  o1a_sat_inc(&c->hdr->o1a_anomalies);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_mark_saturated(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->recording) return;
+  o1a_sat_inc(&c->hdr->saturated_durations);
+  orch_o1a_set_flags(c, kCFlagSaturated | kCFlagO1aPartitionAnomaly);
+  o1a_sat_inc(&c->hdr->o1a_anomalies);
+}
+
+inline region_ctl_t* o1a_orch_ctl() noexcept {
+  return g.map == nullptr ? nullptr : &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+}
+
+inline void o1a_register_owner(
+    const void* scheduler_owner, const void* state_owner) noexcept {
+  region_ctl_t* c = o1a_orch_ctl();
+  if (scheduler_owner == nullptr || state_owner == nullptr) {
+    if (c && c->recording) orch_o1a_mark_owner_mismatch(c);
+    return;
+  }
+  if (!g.o1a_owner_registered) {
+    g.o1a_scheduler_owner = scheduler_owner;
+    g.o1a_state_owner = state_owner;
+    g.o1a_owner_registered = true;
+    return;
+  }
+  // Exactly one registration is permitted, including an identical duplicate.
+  g.o1a_registration_failed = true;
+  if (c && c->recording) orch_o1a_mark_owner_mismatch(c);
+}
+
+inline bool o1a_state_owner_gate(
