+    const void* state_owner, bool require_thread) noexcept {
+  region_ctl_t* c = o1a_orch_ctl();
+  if (!c || !c->recording) return false;
+  if (!g.o1a_owner_registered || g.o1a_registration_failed || state_owner == nullptr ||
+      state_owner != g.o1a_state_owner) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_owner_mismatch(c);
+    return false;
+  }
+  if (require_thread &&
+      (g.o1a_thread_anchor == nullptr ||
+          g.o1a_thread_anchor != static_cast<const void*>(&o1a_thread_anchor_tls))) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_cross_thread(c);
+    return false;
+  }
+  return true;
+}
+
+inline bool o1a_scheduler_owner_gate(
+    const void* scheduler_owner, bool require_thread = true) noexcept {
+  region_ctl_t* c = o1a_orch_ctl();
+  if (!c || !c->recording) return false;
+  if (!g.o1a_owner_registered || g.o1a_registration_failed ||
+      scheduler_owner == nullptr || scheduler_owner != g.o1a_scheduler_owner) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_owner_mismatch(c);
+    return false;
+  }
+  if (require_thread &&
+      (g.o1a_thread_anchor == nullptr ||
+          g.o1a_thread_anchor != static_cast<const void*>(&o1a_thread_anchor_tls))) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_cross_thread(c);
+    return false;
+  }
+  return true;
+}
+
+inline bool o1a_owner_pair_gate(
+    const void* scheduler_owner, const void* state_owner) noexcept {
+  return o1a_scheduler_owner_gate(scheduler_owner) &&
+         o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, true);
+}
+
+inline bool o1a_thread_gate() noexcept {
+  region_ctl_t* c = o1a_orch_ctl();
+  if (!c || !c->recording) return false;
+  if (!g.o1a_owner_registered || g.o1a_registration_failed) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_owner_mismatch(c);
+    return false;
+  }
+  if (g.o1a_thread_anchor == nullptr ||
+      g.o1a_thread_anchor != static_cast<const void*>(&o1a_thread_anchor_tls)) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_cross_thread(c);
+    return false;
+  }
+  return true;
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1c_add64(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t* field, uint64_t value) noexcept {
+  if (*field > ~uint64_t(0) - value) {
+    *field = ~uint64_t(0);
+    orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c, false, true);
+  } else {
+    *field += value;
+  }
+}
+
+inline uint32_t orch_o1c_narrow(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t value) noexcept {
+  if (value <= 0xFFFFFFFFull) return uint32_t(value);
+  orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c, false, true);
+  return 0xFFFFFFFFu;
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1c_stage_u64(uint32_t* low, uint32_t* high, uint64_t value) noexcept {
+  *low = uint32_t(value);
+  *high = uint32_t(value >> 32);
+}
+
+inline bool o1c_add_overflows(uint64_t a, uint64_t b) noexcept {
+  return a > ~uint64_t(0) - b;
+}
+
+inline uint64_t o1c_mask(uint32_t n) noexcept {
+  if (n == 0) return 0;
+  if (n == 64) return ~uint64_t(0);
+  return (uint64_t(1) << n) - 1;
+}
+
+inline bool orch_o1a_leaf_for_phase(uint8_t phase, uint8_t* leaf) noexcept {
+  switch (phase) {
+  case kO1aRecordTail:
+    *leaf = kO1aLeafRecordTail;
+    return true;
+  case kO1aQbatchTeardown:
+    *leaf = kO1aLeafQbatchTeardown;
+    return true;
+  case kO1aPreviousExit:
+    *leaf = kO1aLeafPreviousExit;
+    return true;
+  case kO1aQueueHandoff:
+    *leaf = kO1aLeafQueueHandoff;
+    return true;
+  case kO1aCvWait:
+    *leaf = kO1aLeafCvWait;
+    return true;
+  case kO1aRequestApply:
+    *leaf = kO1aLeafRequestApply;
+    return true;
+  case kO1aDispatchDelay:
+    *leaf = kO1aLeafDispatchDelay;
+    return true;
+  case kO1aForwardArgs:
+    *leaf = kO1aLeafForwardArgs;
+    return true;
+  case kO1aForwardSetup:
+    *leaf = kO1aLeafForwardSetup;
+    return true;
+  case kO1aStateEntry:
+    *leaf = kO1aLeafStateEntry;
+    return true;
+  default:
+    return false;
+  }
+}
+
+inline bool orch_o1a_add64(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t* field, uint64_t value) noexcept {
+  if (*field > ~uint64_t(0) - value) {
+    *field = ~uint64_t(0);
+    orch_o1a_mark_saturated(c);
+    return false;
+  }
+  *field += value;
+  return true;
+}
+
+inline bool orch_o1a_inc(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t* field) noexcept {
+  if (*field == ~uint64_t(0)) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_count_overflow(c);
+    return false;
+  }
+  ++*field;
+  return true;
+}
+
+inline bool orch_o1a_flush_at(region_ctl_t* c, uint8_t phase, uint64_t t) noexcept {
+  uint8_t leaf = 0;
+  if (!orch_o1a_leaf_for_phase(phase, &leaf)) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return false;
+  }
+  if (t < c->o1a_cursor) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_clock(c);
+    return false;
+  }
+  bool ok = orch_o1a_add64(c, &c->o1a_leaf[leaf], t - c->o1a_cursor);
+  c->o1a_cursor = t;
+  return ok;
+}
+
+inline bool orch_o1a_transition_at(
+    region_ctl_t* c, uint8_t expected, uint8_t next, uint64_t t) noexcept {
+  if (o1a_phase_load(c) != expected) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return false;
+  }
+  if (!orch_o1a_flush_at(c, expected, t)) return false;
+  o1a_phase_store(c, next);
+  return true;
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_clear_aggregate(
+    region_ctl_t* c, bool preserve_executor = false) noexcept {
+  const void* executor_ptr = c->o1a_executor_ptr;
+  uint8_t executor_kind = c->o1a_executor_kind;
+  bool executor_open = c->o1a_executor_open;
+  std::memset(c->o1a_leaf, 0, sizeof(c->o1a_leaf));
+  std::memset(c->o1a_count, 0, sizeof(c->o1a_count));
+  std::memset(c->o1a_hit, 0, sizeof(c->o1a_hit));
+  c->o1a_cursor = 0;
+  c->o1a_saved_phase = kO1aNoSeed;
+  c->o1a_executor_ptr = nullptr;
+  c->o1a_executor_kind = 0;
+  c->o1a_wait_open = false;
+  c->o1a_queue_loop_open = false;
+  c->o1a_executor_open = false;
+  c->o1a_empty_return_pending = false;
+  if (preserve_executor && executor_open) {
+    c->o1a_executor_ptr = executor_ptr;
+    c->o1a_executor_kind = executor_kind;
+    c->o1a_executor_open = true;
+  }
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_seed_at(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t r) noexcept {
+  c->o1a_cursor = r;
+  o1a_phase_store(c, kO1aRecordTail);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_after_return_at(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t p) noexcept {
+  if (orch_o1a_transition_at(c, kO1aRecordTail, kO1aPreviousExit, p))
+    (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitP]);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_zero_entry_at(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t e) noexcept {
+  if (!c->have_prev && o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aNoSeed) return;
+  if (o1a_phase_load(c) != kO1aAwaitEntry) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return;
+  }
+  c->o1a_cursor = e;
+  o1a_phase_store(c, kO1aZeroLogitsBody);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_zero_return_at(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t r) noexcept {
+  if (!c->have_prev && o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aNoSeed) return;
+  if (o1a_phase_load(c) != kO1aZeroLogitsBody) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return;
+  }
+  if (r < c->o1a_cursor) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_clock(c);
+    return;
+  }
+  (void)orch_o1a_add64(c, &c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafZeroLogitsBody], r - c->o1a_cursor);
+  orch_o1a_seed_at(c, r);
+}
+
+inline uint32_t orch_o1a_narrow_duration(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t value) noexcept {
+  if (value <= 0xFFFFFFFFull) return uint32_t(value);
+  orch_o1a_mark_saturated(c);
+  return 0xFFFFFFFFu;
+}
+
+inline uint8_t orch_o1a_narrow_count(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t value) noexcept {
+  if (value <= 0xFFu) return uint8_t(value);
+  orch_o1a_mark_count_overflow(c);
+  return 0xFFu;
+}
+
+inline bool orch_o1a_identity(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t parent) noexcept {
+  uint64_t sum = 0;
+  for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kO1aLeafCount; ++i) {
+    if (sum > ~uint64_t(0) - c->o1a_leaf[i]) {
+      orch_o1a_mark_saturated(c);
+      return false;
+    }
+    sum += c->o1a_leaf[i];
+  }
+  if (sum != parent) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return false;
+  }
+  return true;
+}
+
+inline bool orch_o1a_counts_reconcile(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t forwards) noexcept {
+  uint64_t waits = c->o1a_count[kO1aCountWaits];
+  uint64_t empty = c->o1a_count[kO1aCountEmptySweeps];
+  uint64_t loops = c->o1a_count[kO1aCountQueueLoops];
+  uint64_t other = c->o1a_count[kO1aCountOtherExecutors];
+  if (forwards > ~uint64_t(0) - empty || forwards + empty > ~uint64_t(0) - other) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_count_overflow(c);
+    return false;
+  }
+  uint64_t attempts = forwards + empty;
+  uint64_t executor_calls = attempts + other;
+  bool ok = c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitP] == forwards && c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitK] == forwards &&
+            c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitQ] == forwards && c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitU] == forwards &&
+            c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitH] == forwards && c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitA] == forwards &&
+            c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitF] == attempts && c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitG] == attempts &&
+            c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitZ] == empty && c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitQ0] == loops &&
+            c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitD] == loops && c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitX] == loops &&
+            c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitW0] == waits && c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitW1] == waits &&
+            c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitV0] == executor_calls &&
+            c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitV1] == executor_calls;
+  if (!ok) orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+  return ok;
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_stage_cycle_fields(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c->have_prev) {
+    if (o1a_phase_load(c) != kO1aNoSeed) orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return;  // first-record sentinel: memset record already supplies all zeros
+  }
+  bool target_executor_open = c->o1a_executor_open && c->o1a_executor_kind == 1 &&
+                              c->o1a_executor_ptr == g.o1a_scheduler_owner;
+  if (o1a_phase_load(c) != kO1aAwaitEntry || c->o1a_wait_open ||
+      c->o1a_queue_loop_open || !target_executor_open || c->o1a_empty_return_pending) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+  }
+  (void)orch_o1a_identity(c, c->o1_before_run_forward);
+  (void)orch_o1a_counts_reconcile(c, c->o1c_forwards);
+
+  c->c.d_o1a_record_tail = orch_o1a_narrow_duration(c, c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafRecordTail]);
+  c->c.d_o1a_qbatch_teardown =
+      orch_o1a_narrow_duration(c, c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafQbatchTeardown]);
+  c->c.d_o1a_previous_exit =
+      orch_o1a_narrow_duration(c, c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafPreviousExit]);
+  c->c.d_o1a_queue_handoff =
+      orch_o1a_narrow_duration(c, c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafQueueHandoff]);
+  c->c.d_o1a_cv_wait = orch_o1a_narrow_duration(c, c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafCvWait]);
+  c->c.d_o1a_request_apply =
+      orch_o1a_narrow_duration(c, c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafRequestApply]);
+  c->c.d_o1a_dispatch_delay =
+      orch_o1a_narrow_duration(c, c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafDispatchDelay]);
+  c->c.d_o1a_forward_args =
+      orch_o1a_narrow_duration(c, c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafForwardArgs]);
+  c->c.d_o1a_forward_setup =
+      orch_o1a_narrow_duration(c, c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafForwardSetup]);
+  c->c.d_o1a_state_entry = orch_o1a_narrow_duration(c, c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafStateEntry]);
+  c->c.d_o1a_zero_logits_body =
+      orch_o1a_narrow_duration(c, c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafZeroLogitsBody]);
+  c->c.n_o1a_cv_waits = orch_o1a_narrow_count(c, c->o1a_count[kO1aCountWaits]);
+  c->c.n_o1a_empty_sweeps =
+      orch_o1a_narrow_count(c, c->o1a_count[kO1aCountEmptySweeps]);
+  c->c.n_o1a_queue_loops = orch_o1a_narrow_count(c, c->o1a_count[kO1aCountQueueLoops]);
+  c->c.n_o1a_other_executor_forwards =
+      orch_o1a_narrow_count(c, c->o1a_count[kO1aCountOtherExecutors]);
+  c->c.flags |= c->o1_flags;
+  o1a_phase_store(c, kO1aAwaitRecordedReturn);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_qbatch_clear_begin(const void* state_owner) noexcept {
+  if (!o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, true)) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  if (o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aNoSeed) return;
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  if (orch_o1a_transition_at(c, kO1aPreviousExit, kO1aQbatchTeardown, t))
+    (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitK]);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_qbatch_cleared(const void* state_owner) noexcept {
+  if (!o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, true)) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  if (o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aNoSeed) return;
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  if (orch_o1a_transition_at(c, kO1aQbatchTeardown, kO1aPreviousExit, t))
+    (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitQ]);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_scheduler_exit(const void* scheduler_owner) noexcept {
+  if (!o1a_scheduler_owner_gate(scheduler_owner)) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  if (o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aNoSeed) return;
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  if (orch_o1a_transition_at(c, kO1aPreviousExit, kO1aQueueHandoff, t))
+    (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitU]);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_queue_loop() noexcept {
+  region_ctl_t* c = o1a_orch_ctl();
+  if (!c || !c->recording) return;
+  uint8_t phase = o1a_phase_load(c);
+  if (phase == kO1aNoSeed || phase == kO1aTerminalIdle) return;
+  if (!o1a_thread_gate()) return;
+  if ((phase != kO1aQueueHandoff && phase != kO1aDispatchDelay) ||
+      c->o1a_queue_loop_open || c->o1a_wait_open || c->o1a_executor_open) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return;
+  }
+  c->o1a_queue_loop_open = true;
+  (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitQ0]);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_wait_begin() noexcept {
+  region_ctl_t* c = o1a_orch_ctl();
+  if (!c || !c->recording) return;
+  uint8_t phase = o1a_phase_load(c);
+  if (phase == kO1aNoSeed || phase == kO1aTerminalIdle) return;
+  if (!o1a_thread_gate()) return;
+  if (!c->o1a_queue_loop_open || c->o1a_wait_open ||
+      (phase != kO1aQueueHandoff && phase != kO1aDispatchDelay)) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return;
+  }
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  if (!orch_o1a_flush_at(c, phase, t)) return;
+  c->o1a_saved_phase = phase;
+  c->o1a_wait_open = true;
+  o1a_phase_store(c, kO1aCvWait);
+  (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitW0]);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_wait_end() noexcept {
+  region_ctl_t* c = o1a_orch_ctl();
+  if (!c || !c->recording) return;
+  if (o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aNoSeed || o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aTerminalIdle) return;
+  if (!o1a_thread_gate()) return;
+  if (!c->o1a_wait_open || o1a_phase_load(c) != kO1aCvWait ||
+      (c->o1a_saved_phase != kO1aQueueHandoff &&
+          c->o1a_saved_phase != kO1aDispatchDelay)) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return;
+  }
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  if (!orch_o1a_flush_at(c, kO1aCvWait, t)) return;
+  c->o1a_wait_open = false;
+  o1a_phase_store(c, c->o1a_saved_phase);
+  c->o1a_saved_phase = kO1aNoSeed;
+  (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_count[kO1aCountWaits]);
+  (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitW1]);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_queue_drained() noexcept {
+  region_ctl_t* c = o1a_orch_ctl();
+  if (!c || !c->recording) return;
+  uint8_t phase = o1a_phase_load(c);
+  if (phase == kO1aNoSeed || phase == kO1aTerminalIdle) return;
+  if (!o1a_thread_gate()) return;
+  if (!c->o1a_queue_loop_open || c->o1a_wait_open ||
+      (phase != kO1aQueueHandoff && phase != kO1aDispatchDelay)) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return;
+  }
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  if (!orch_o1a_flush_at(c, phase, t)) return;
+  o1a_phase_store(c, kO1aRequestApply);
+  (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_count[kO1aCountQueueLoops]);
+  (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitD]);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_requests_applied() noexcept {
+  region_ctl_t* c = o1a_orch_ctl();
+  if (!c || !c->recording) return;
+  uint8_t phase = o1a_phase_load(c);
+  if (phase == kO1aNoSeed || phase == kO1aTerminalIdle) return;
+  if (!o1a_thread_gate()) return;
+  if (!c->o1a_queue_loop_open) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return;
+  }
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  if (!orch_o1a_transition_at(c, kO1aRequestApply, kO1aDispatchDelay, t)) return;
+  c->o1a_queue_loop_open = false;
+  (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitX]);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_executor_begin(const void* executor) noexcept {
+  region_ctl_t* c = o1a_orch_ctl();
+  if (!c || !c->recording) return;
+  if (!g.o1a_owner_registered || g.o1a_registration_failed || executor == nullptr) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_owner_mismatch(c);
+    return;
+  }
+  const void* this_anchor = static_cast<const void*>(&o1a_thread_anchor_tls);
+  if (g.o1a_thread_anchor == nullptr) {
+    g.o1a_thread_anchor = this_anchor;
+  } else if (g.o1a_thread_anchor != this_anchor) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_cross_thread(c);
+    return;
+  }
+  if (c->o1a_executor_open) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return;
+  }
+  c->o1a_executor_open = true;
+  c->o1a_executor_ptr = executor;
+  if (o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aNoSeed) {
+    c->o1a_executor_kind = 3;  // pre-sentinel call: bind only, do not count
+    return;
+  }
+  if (o1a_phase_load(c) != kO1aDispatchDelay) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    c->o1a_executor_kind = 0;
+    return;
+  }
+  c->o1a_executor_kind = executor == g.o1a_scheduler_owner ? 1 : 2;
+  if (c->o1a_executor_kind == 2)
+    (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_count[kO1aCountOtherExecutors]);
+  (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitV0]);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_executor_end(const void* executor) noexcept {
+  if (!o1a_thread_gate()) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  if (!c->o1a_executor_open || executor == nullptr || executor != c->o1a_executor_ptr) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return;
+  }
+  uint8_t kind = c->o1a_executor_kind;
+  c->o1a_executor_open = false;
+  c->o1a_executor_ptr = nullptr;
+  c->o1a_executor_kind = 0;
+  if (kind == 3) {
+    // The first target V0 predates the sentinel R. If that call reached R,
+    // its V1 is the first V1 inside the newly seeded R-to-A aggregate.
+    uint8_t phase = o1a_phase_load(c);
+    if (phase != kO1aNoSeed && phase != kO1aTerminalIdle)
+      (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitV1]);
+    return;
+  }
+  if (kind != 1 && kind != 2) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return;
+  }
+  (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitV1]);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_forward_entry(const void* scheduler_owner) noexcept {
+  if (!o1a_scheduler_owner_gate(scheduler_owner)) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  if (o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aNoSeed) return;
+  if (!c->o1a_executor_open || c->o1a_executor_kind != 1 ||
+      c->o1a_executor_ptr != scheduler_owner) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return;
+  }
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  if (orch_o1a_transition_at(c, kO1aDispatchDelay, kO1aForwardArgs, t))
+    (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitF]);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_forward_args(const void* scheduler_owner, bool empty) noexcept {
+  if (!o1a_scheduler_owner_gate(scheduler_owner)) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  if (o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aNoSeed) return;
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  uint8_t next = empty ? kO1aDispatchDelay : kO1aForwardSetup;
+  if (!orch_o1a_transition_at(c, kO1aForwardArgs, next, t)) return;
+  (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitG]);
+  if (empty) {
+    (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_count[kO1aCountEmptySweeps]);
+    c->o1a_empty_return_pending = true;
+  }
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_empty_return(const void* scheduler_owner) noexcept {
+  if (!o1a_scheduler_owner_gate(scheduler_owner)) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  if (o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aNoSeed) return;
+  if (o1a_phase_load(c) != kO1aDispatchDelay || !c->o1a_empty_return_pending) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return;
+  }
+  c->o1a_empty_return_pending = false;
+  (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitZ]);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_state_call(
+    const void* scheduler_owner, const void* state_owner) noexcept {
+  if (!o1a_owner_pair_gate(scheduler_owner, state_owner)) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  if (o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aNoSeed) return;
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  if (orch_o1a_transition_at(c, kO1aForwardSetup, kO1aStateEntry, t))
+    (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitH]);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_run_forward_begin_at(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t a) noexcept {
+  if (o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aNoSeed) return;
+  if (orch_o1a_transition_at(c, kO1aStateEntry, kO1aAwaitEntry, a))
+    (void)orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitA]);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1a_clean_termination() noexcept {
+  region_ctl_t* c = o1a_orch_ctl();
+  if (!c || !c->recording) return;
+  uint8_t phase = o1a_phase_load(c);
+  if (phase == kO1aTerminalIdle) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return;
+  }
+  if (phase == kO1aNoSeed) {
+    o1a_phase_store(c, kO1aTerminalIdle);
+    return;
+  }
+  if (!o1a_thread_gate()) return;
+  if (phase < kO1aRecordTail || phase > kO1aStateEntry || c->o1a_executor_open ||
+      c->o1a_queue_loop_open || c->o1a_empty_return_pending || c->o1a_wait_open) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+  }
+  c->hdr->o1a_terminal_prefix_phase =
+      (phase >= kO1aRecordTail && phase <= kO1aStateEntry) ? phase : 0;
+  if (c->hdr->o1a_terminal_prefix_discards != 0xFFFFFFFFu)
+    ++c->hdr->o1a_terminal_prefix_discards;
+  else
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+  orch_o1a_clear_aggregate(c);
+  o1a_phase_store(c, kO1aTerminalIdle);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1_accum64(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t* field, uint64_t t) noexcept {
+  if (t < c->o1_cursor) {
+    orch_o1_mark_anomaly(c);
+  } else {
+    uint64_t d = t - c->o1_cursor;
+    if (*field > 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFull - d) {
+      ++c->hdr->saturated_durations;
+      c->o1_flags |= kCFlagSaturated | kCFlagO1SubstageAnomaly;
+      *field = 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFull;
+    } else {
+      *field += d;
+    }
+  }
+  c->o1_cursor = t;
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1_boundary_at(region_ctl_t* c,
+    uint8_t expected_phase,
+    uint8_t next_phase,
+    uint64_t* field,
+    uint64_t t) noexcept {
+  if (c->o1_phase != expected_phase) orch_o1_mark_anomaly(c);
+  if (!c->have_prev && expected_phase == kO1AwaitA) {
+    // Seed first-record order checking at A without measuring a duration from
+    // the process's zero-initialized cursor. Its legacy previous-return O1
+    // timing remains zero; its complete B-to-C O1c identity is retained.
+    c->o1_cursor = t;
+    *field = 0;
+  } else {
+    orch_o1_accum64(c, field, t);
+  }
+  c->o1_phase = next_phase;
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1_run_forward_begin(const void* state_owner) noexcept {
+  if (!o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, true)) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  orch_o1a_run_forward_begin_at(c, t);
+  orch_o1_boundary_at(c, kO1AwaitA, kO1AwaitB, &c->o1_before_run_forward, t);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1c_begin_at(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t t) noexcept {
+  if (o1c_phase_load(c) != kO1cIdle ||
+      g.o1c.active_epoch.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != 0 ||
+      o1c_configured_load() != 0)
+    orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c);
+  // Publish non-idle first. A crash at any later instruction must make the
+  // dump fail closed even before CONFIG publishes active_epoch.
+  o1c_phase_store(c, kO1cAwaitConfig);
+  uint64_t generation = g.o1c.generation + 1;
+  if (generation == 0) {
+    generation = 1;
+    orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c);
+  }
+  g.o1c.generation = generation;
+  g.o1c.active_epoch.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  g.o1c.async_anomaly.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  g.o1c.expected_attention = 0;
+  g.o1c.expected_helpers = 0;
+  g.o1c.expected_helper_checkpoints = 0;
+  g.o1c.expected_attention_mask = 0;
+  g.o1c.expected_helper_mask = 0;
+  o1c_configured_store(0);
+  g.o1c_attention.seen_mask.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  g.o1c_attention.last_tsc.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  g.o1c_helper.seen_mask.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  g.o1c_helper.last_tsc.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  c->o1c_b = t;
+  c->o1c_dispatch = 0;
+  c->o1c_main_prejoin = 0;
+  c->o1c_join_return = 0;
+  c->o1c_epoch = generation;
+  std::memset(c->o1c_helper_blend_return, 0, sizeof(c->o1c_helper_blend_return));
+}
+
+// Stamp B is shared exactly by the O1a-e state and the O1c lane state.
+inline void orch_o1_model_begin(const void* state_owner) noexcept {
+  if (!o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, true)) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  orch_o1_boundary_at(c, kO1AwaitB, kO1AwaitC, &c->o1_setup, t);
+  orch_o1c_begin_at(c, t);
+}
+
+inline uint64_t orch_o1c_config(const void* state_owner,
+    uint32_t attention_workers,
+    uint32_t helper_workers,
+    uint32_t helper_checkpoints) noexcept {
+  if (g.map == nullptr) return 0;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  if (!c->recording) return 0;
+  if (!o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, true)) return 0;
+  if (o1c_phase_load(c) != kO1cAwaitConfig) orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c);
+  if (attention_workers == 0 || attention_workers > kMaxPoolWorkers ||
+      helper_workers > kMaxPoolWorkers ||
+      uint64_t(attention_workers) + helper_workers > kMaxPoolWorkers) {
+    orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return 0;
+  }
+  if (helper_checkpoints != 0 && helper_checkpoints != kO1cHelperCheckpointCount) {
+    orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return 0;
+  }
+
+  region_hdr_t* h = c->hdr;
+  uint32_t total = attention_workers + helper_workers;
+  uint64_t want_pool_mask = o1c_mask(total);
+  if (!g.pool_probe_done || h->pool_workers != total ||
+      h->pool_probe_covered != total || h->pool_probe_failures != 0 ||
+      h->max_abs_pool_skew_ns < 0 ||
+      uint32_t(h->max_abs_pool_skew_ns) > kPoolSkewLimitNs ||
+      h->covered_mask != want_pool_mask)
+    orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c);
+
+  g.o1c.expected_attention = attention_workers;
+  g.o1c.expected_helpers = helper_workers;
+  if (c->o1c_forwards != 0 && c->o1c_helper_checkpoint_mode != helper_checkpoints) {
+    orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c);
+    return 0;
+  }
+  if (c->o1c_forwards == 0) c->o1c_helper_checkpoint_mode = helper_checkpoints;
+  g.o1c.expected_helper_checkpoints = helper_checkpoints;
+  g.o1c.expected_attention_mask = o1c_mask(attention_workers);
+  g.o1c.expected_helper_mask = o1c_mask(helper_workers);
+  o1c_configured_store(1);
+  o1c_phase_store(c, kO1cConfigured);
+  g.o1c.active_epoch.store(c->o1c_epoch, std::memory_order_release);
+  return c->o1c_epoch;
+}
+
+inline void o1c_lane_done(const void* state_owner,
+    uint64_t epoch,
+    uint32_t worker_ix,
+    o1c_lane_shared_t* lane,
+    bool helper_lane) noexcept {
+  if (!o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, false)) return;
+  if (epoch == 0) return;
+  if (g.o1c.active_epoch.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != epoch) {
+    g.o1c.async_anomaly.fetch_or(kO1cAsyncEpoch, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    return;
+  }
+  // CONFIG writes expected counts/masks before its active_epoch release.
+  // Read them only after the matching acquire above; passing them as function
+  // arguments would allow evaluation before that acquire.
+  uint32_t expected = helper_lane ? g.o1c.expected_helpers : g.o1c.expected_attention;
+  uint64_t expected_mask =
+      helper_lane ? g.o1c.expected_helper_mask : g.o1c.expected_attention_mask;
+  if (worker_ix >= expected || worker_ix >= kMaxPoolWorkers) {
+    g.o1c.async_anomaly.fetch_or(kO1cAsyncIndex, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    return;
+  }
+  uint64_t bit = uint64_t(1) << worker_ix;
+  uint64_t old = lane->seen_mask.fetch_or(bit, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
+  if ((old & bit) != 0) {
+    g.o1c.async_anomaly.fetch_or(kO1cAsyncDuplicate, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    return;
+  }
+  if ((old | bit) == expected_mask)
+    lane->last_tsc.store(tsc(), std::memory_order_release);
+}
+
+inline void o1c_helper_blend_returned(const void* state_owner,
+    uint64_t epoch,
+    uint32_t helper_ix,
+    uint32_t checkpoint_ix) noexcept {
+  if (!o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, false)) return;
+  if (epoch == 0) return;
+  if (g.o1c.active_epoch.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != epoch) {
+    g.o1c.async_anomaly.fetch_or(kO1cAsyncEpoch, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    return;
+  }
+  uint32_t expected = g.o1c.expected_helper_checkpoints;
+  if (expected != kO1cHelperCheckpointCount || checkpoint_ix >= expected ||
+      helper_ix >= g.o1c.expected_helpers || helper_ix >= kMaxPoolWorkers) {
+    g.o1c.async_anomaly.fetch_or(kO1cAsyncIndex, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    return;
+  }
+
+  o1c_helper_checkpoint_row_t& row = g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[helper_ix];
+  if (checkpoint_ix == 0) {
+    if (row.epoch == epoch) {
+      g.o1c.async_anomaly.fetch_or(kO1cAsyncDuplicate, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+      return;
+    }
+    row.epoch = epoch;
+    row.seen_mask = 0;
+  } else if (row.epoch != epoch) {
+    g.o1c.async_anomaly.fetch_or(kO1cAsyncEpoch, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    return;
+  }
+
+  uint32_t want_mask = (uint32_t(1) << checkpoint_ix) - 1;
+  if (row.seen_mask != want_mask) {
+    uint32_t bit = uint32_t(1) << checkpoint_ix;
+    uint32_t anomaly =
+        (row.seen_mask & bit) != 0 ? kO1cAsyncDuplicate : kO1cAsyncCheckpointOrder;
+    g.o1c.async_anomaly.fetch_or(anomaly, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+    return;
+  }
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  if (checkpoint_ix != 0 && t < row.returned_tsc[checkpoint_ix - 1])
+    g.o1c.async_anomaly.fetch_or(kO1cAsyncCheckpointClock, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  row.returned_tsc[checkpoint_ix] = t;
+  row.seen_mask = want_mask | (uint32_t(1) << checkpoint_ix);
+}
+
+inline void o1c_attention_done(
+    const void* state_owner, uint64_t epoch, uint32_t worker_ix) noexcept {
+  o1c_lane_done(state_owner, epoch, worker_ix, &g.o1c_attention, false);
+}
+
+inline void o1c_helper_done(
+    const void* state_owner, uint64_t epoch, uint32_t worker_ix) noexcept {
+  o1c_lane_done(state_owner, epoch, worker_ix, &g.o1c_helper, true);
+}
+
+inline bool orch_o1c_epoch_phase(
+    region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t epoch, uint8_t phase) noexcept {
+  bool ok = epoch != 0 && c->o1c_epoch == epoch && o1c_phase_load(c) == phase &&
+            g.o1c.active_epoch.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == epoch;
+  if (!ok) orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c);
+  return ok;
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1c_dispatch_done(const void* state_owner, uint64_t epoch) noexcept {
+  if (epoch == 0 || g.map == nullptr) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  if (!c->recording) return;
+  if (!o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, true)) return;
+  (void)orch_o1c_epoch_phase(c, epoch, kO1cConfigured);
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  if (t < c->o1c_b) orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c, true);
+  c->o1c_dispatch = t;
+  o1c_phase_store(c, kO1cDispatched);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1c_main_prejoin(const void* state_owner, uint64_t epoch) noexcept {
+  if (epoch == 0 || g.map == nullptr) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  if (!c->recording) return;
+  if (!o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, true)) return;
+  (void)orch_o1c_epoch_phase(c, epoch, kO1cDispatched);
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  if (t < c->o1c_dispatch) orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c, true);
+  c->o1c_main_prejoin = t;
+  o1c_phase_store(c, kO1cMainAtJoin);
+}
+
+inline bool orch_o1c_reduce_helper_checkpoints(
+    region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t epoch, uint64_t join_tsc, bool* clock_anomaly) noexcept {
+  std::memset(c->o1c_helper_blend_return, 0, sizeof(c->o1c_helper_blend_return));
+  uint32_t expected = g.o1c.expected_helper_checkpoints;
+  if (expected == 0) return true;
+  if (expected != kO1cHelperCheckpointCount) return false;
+  if (g.o1c.expected_helpers == 0) return true;
+
+  bool valid = true;
+  uint32_t full_mask = (uint32_t(1) << expected) - 1;
+  for (uint32_t helper_ix = 0; helper_ix < g.o1c.expected_helpers; ++helper_ix) {
+    const o1c_helper_checkpoint_row_t& row = g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[helper_ix];
+    if (row.epoch != epoch || row.seen_mask != full_mask) {
+      valid = false;
+      continue;
+    }
+    uint64_t previous = c->o1c_b;
+    for (uint32_t checkpoint_ix = 0; checkpoint_ix < expected; ++checkpoint_ix) {
+      uint64_t stamp = row.returned_tsc[checkpoint_ix];
+      if (stamp < previous || stamp > join_tsc) {
+        *clock_anomaly = true;
+        valid = false;
+      }
+      previous = stamp;
+      if (stamp > c->o1c_helper_blend_return[checkpoint_ix])
+        c->o1c_helper_blend_return[checkpoint_ix] = stamp;
+    }
+  }
+  return valid;
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1c_join_return(const void* state_owner, uint64_t epoch) noexcept {
+  if (epoch == 0 || g.map == nullptr) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  if (!c->recording) return;
+  if (!o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, true)) return;
+  (void)orch_o1c_epoch_phase(c, epoch, kO1cMainAtJoin);
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  if (t < c->o1c_main_prejoin) orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c, true);
+  c->o1c_join_return = t;
+  // Fix the join-return timestamp before scanning private rows. The scan is
+  // still inside run(), so its cost is included in the later B-to-C/O1c stamp.
+  bool checkpoint_clock = false;
+  bool checkpoints_ok =
+      orch_o1c_reduce_helper_checkpoints(c, epoch, t, &checkpoint_clock);
+  uint64_t attn_seen = g.o1c_attention.seen_mask.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
+  uint64_t helper_seen = g.o1c_helper.seen_mask.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
+  uint32_t async = g.o1c.async_anomaly.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
+  if (attn_seen != g.o1c.expected_attention_mask ||
+      helper_seen != g.o1c.expected_helper_mask || async != 0 || !checkpoints_ok)
+    orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(
+        c, checkpoint_clock || (async & kO1cAsyncCheckpointClock) != 0);
+  o1c_phase_store(c, kO1cJoined);
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1c_finalize_at(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t t) noexcept {
+  bool valid = o1c_phase_load(c) == kO1cJoined && o1c_configured_load() != 0 &&
+               c->o1c_epoch != 0 &&
+               g.o1c.active_epoch.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == c->o1c_epoch;
+  if (!valid) orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c);
+
+  uint64_t attn = g.o1c_attention.last_tsc.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
+  uint64_t helper = g.o1c_helper.last_tsc.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
+  uint32_t n_attn = g.o1c.expected_attention;
+  uint32_t n_helper = g.o1c.expected_helpers;
+  uint64_t attn_seen = g.o1c_attention.seen_mask.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
+  uint64_t helper_seen = g.o1c_helper.seen_mask.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
+  if (attn_seen != g.o1c.expected_attention_mask ||
+      helper_seen != g.o1c.expected_helper_mask ||
+      g.o1c.async_anomaly.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != 0 || n_attn == 0 ||
+      attn == 0 || (n_helper != 0 && helper == 0) || (n_helper == 0 && helper != 0)) {
+    orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c);
+    valid = false;
+  }
+
+  bool checkpoints_valid = true;
+  bool checkpoint_clock = false;
+  uint32_t n_checkpoints = g.o1c.expected_helper_checkpoints;
+  if (n_checkpoints == 0) {
+    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kO1cHelperCheckpointCount; ++i)
+      if (c->o1c_helper_blend_return[i] != 0) checkpoints_valid = false;
+  } else if (n_checkpoints != kO1cHelperCheckpointCount) {
+    checkpoints_valid = false;
+  } else if (n_helper != 0) {
+    uint64_t previous = c->o1c_b;
+    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kO1cHelperCheckpointCount; ++i) {
+      uint64_t checkpoint = c->o1c_helper_blend_return[i];
+      if (checkpoint < previous || checkpoint > helper) {
+        checkpoints_valid = false;
+        checkpoint_clock = true;
+      }
+      previous = checkpoint;
+    }
+  }
+  if (!checkpoints_valid) {
+    orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c, checkpoint_clock);
+    valid = false;
+  }
+  if (c->o1c_b > c->o1c_dispatch || c->o1c_dispatch > c->o1c_main_prejoin ||
+      c->o1c_main_prejoin > c->o1c_join_return || c->o1c_join_return > t ||
+      attn < c->o1c_b || attn > c->o1c_join_return ||
+      (n_helper != 0 && (helper < c->o1c_b || helper > c->o1c_join_return))) {
+    orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c, true);
+    valid = false;
+  }
+
+  orch_o1c_add64(c, &c->o1c_attention_tasks, n_attn);
+  orch_o1c_add64(c, &c->o1c_helper_tasks, n_helper);
+  orch_o1c_add64(c, &c->o1c_forwards, 1);
+  if (n_attn != 0) orch_o1c_add64(c, &c->o1c_attention_forwards, 1);
+  if (n_helper != 0) orch_o1c_add64(c, &c->o1c_helper_forwards, 1);
+
+  if (valid) {
+    uint64_t critical = c->o1c_main_prejoin;
+    uint64_t second = 0;
+    uint8_t winner = 0;  // 0 main, 1 attention, 2 helper
+    auto consider = [&](uint64_t endpoint, uint8_t lane) noexcept {
+      if (endpoint > critical) {
+        second = critical;
+        critical = endpoint;
+        winner = lane;
+      } else if (endpoint > second) {
+        second = endpoint;
+      }
+    };
+    consider(attn, 1);
+    if (n_helper != 0) consider(helper, 2);
+
+    uint64_t ambiguity = c->hdr->o1c_ambiguity_ticks;
+    if (critical - second <= ambiguity) {
+      orch_o1c_add64(c, &c->o1c_critical_ambiguous, 1);
+    } else if (winner == 0) {
+      orch_o1c_add64(c, &c->o1c_main_critical, 1);
+    } else if (winner == 1) {
+      orch_o1c_add64(c, &c->o1c_attention_critical, 1);
+    } else {
+      orch_o1c_add64(c, &c->o1c_helper_critical, 1);
+    }
+
+    uint64_t critical_delta = critical - c->o1c_b;
+    uint64_t tail_delta = t - critical;
+    orch_o1c_add64(c, &c->o1c_dispatch_sum, c->o1c_dispatch - c->o1c_b);
+    orch_o1c_add64(c, &c->o1c_main_prejoin_sum, c->o1c_main_prejoin - c->o1c_b);
+    orch_o1c_add64(c, &c->o1c_attention_done_sum, attn - c->o1c_b);
+    if (n_helper != 0) {
+      orch_o1c_add64(c, &c->o1c_helper_done_sum, helper - c->o1c_b);
+      if (n_checkpoints == kO1cHelperCheckpointCount)
+        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kO1cHelperCheckpointCount; ++i)
+          orch_o1c_add64(c, &c->o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[i],
+              c->o1c_helper_blend_return[i] - c->o1c_b);
+    }
+    orch_o1c_add64(c, &c->o1c_critical_ready_sum, critical_delta);
+    orch_o1c_add64(c, &c->o1c_join_tail_sum, tail_delta);
+    if (critical_delta + tail_delta != t - c->o1c_b) orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c);
+  }
+
+  // Close in this order and publish Idle last. A racing crash dump therefore
+  // sees either a non-idle phase or fully closed state, never a false Idle.
+  o1c_configured_store(0);
+  g.o1c.active_epoch.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
+  o1c_phase_store(c, kO1cIdle);
+}
+
+// Stamp C is shared exactly by O1c and the existing O1a-e state.
+inline void orch_o1_model_end(const void* state_owner) noexcept {
+  if (!o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, true)) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  orch_o1c_finalize_at(c, t);
+  orch_o1_boundary_at(c, kO1AwaitC, kO1AwaitD, &c->o1_model_forward, t);
+}
+inline void orch_o1_run_logits_call(const void* state_owner) noexcept {
+  if (!o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, true)) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  orch_o1_boundary_at(c, kO1AwaitD, kO1AwaitEntry, &c->o1_post_model, tsc());
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1_run_logits_entry(
+    region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t t, bool zero_logit) noexcept {
+  if (c->o1_phase != kO1AwaitEntry) orch_o1_mark_anomaly(c);
+  orch_o1_accum64(c, &c->o1_pre_run_logits, t);
+  if (zero_logit) orch_o1c_add64(c, &c->o1c_zero_logit_forwards, 1);
+  if (zero_logit) orch_o1a_zero_entry_at(c, t);
+  c->o1_phase = zero_logit ? kO1AwaitZeroReturn : kO1AwaitRecordedReturn;
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1_zero_return_at(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t t) noexcept {
+  if (c->o1_phase != kO1AwaitZeroReturn) orch_o1_mark_anomaly(c);
+  orch_o1_accum64(c, &c->o1_before_run_forward, t);
+  c->o1_phase = kO1AwaitA;
+}
+
+inline uint32_t orch_o1_narrow(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t value) noexcept {
+  if (value <= 0xFFFFFFFFull) return uint32_t(value);
+  ++c->hdr->saturated_durations;
+  c->o1_flags |= kCFlagSaturated | kCFlagO1SubstageAnomaly;
+  return 0xFFFFFFFFu;
+}
+
+inline void orch_o1_reset_after_record(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t t_return) noexcept {
+  c->o1_cursor = t_return;
+  c->o1_before_run_forward = 0;
+  c->o1_setup = 0;
+  c->o1_model_forward = 0;
+  c->o1_post_model = 0;
+  c->o1_pre_run_logits = 0;
+  c->o1_flags = 0;
+  c->o1_phase = kO1AwaitA;
+  c->o1c_dispatch_sum = 0;
+  c->o1c_main_prejoin_sum = 0;
+  c->o1c_attention_done_sum = 0;
+  c->o1c_helper_done_sum = 0;
+  for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kO1cHelperCheckpointCount; ++i)
+    c->o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[i] = 0;
+  c->o1c_helper_checkpoint_mode = 0;
+  c->o1c_critical_ready_sum = 0;
+  c->o1c_join_tail_sum = 0;
+  c->o1c_attention_tasks = 0;
+  c->o1c_helper_tasks = 0;
+  c->o1c_forwards = 0;
+  c->o1c_zero_logit_forwards = 0;
+  c->o1c_attention_forwards = 0;
+  c->o1c_helper_forwards = 0;
+  c->o1c_main_critical = 0;
+  c->o1c_attention_critical = 0;
+  c->o1c_helper_critical = 0;
+  c->o1c_critical_ambiguous = 0;
+}
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// Orchestrator logits hooks (region 4). O1..O10, O1a..O1e, and the O1c
+// critical-ready + join-tail split each retain an exact 0-tick identity.
+// ===========================================================================
+
+inline void orch_stage_o1c_cycle_fields(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  bool critical_overflow =
+      o1c_add_overflows(c->o1c_main_critical, c->o1c_attention_critical) ||
+      o1c_add_overflows(
+          c->o1c_main_critical + c->o1c_attention_critical, c->o1c_helper_critical) ||
+      o1c_add_overflows(
+          c->o1c_main_critical + c->o1c_attention_critical + c->o1c_helper_critical,
+          c->o1c_critical_ambiguous);
+  uint64_t critical_counts =
+      critical_overflow ? 0
+                        : c->o1c_main_critical + c->o1c_attention_critical +
+                              c->o1c_helper_critical + c->o1c_critical_ambiguous;
+  bool identity_overflow =
+      o1c_add_overflows(c->o1c_critical_ready_sum, c->o1c_join_tail_sum);
+  bool checkpoints_enabled = c->o1c_helper_checkpoint_mode == kO1cHelperCheckpointCount;
+  bool checkpoints_valid = c->o1c_helper_checkpoint_mode == 0 || checkpoints_enabled;
+  if (c->o1c_helper_forwards == 0) {
+    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kO1cHelperCheckpointCount; ++i)
+      checkpoints_valid = checkpoints_valid && c->o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[i] == 0;
+  } else if (checkpoints_enabled) {
+    checkpoints_valid =
+        c->o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[0] <= c->o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[1] &&
+        c->o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[1] <= c->o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[2] &&
+        c->o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[2] <= c->o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[3] &&
+        c->o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[3] <= c->o1c_helper_done_sum;
+  } else {
+    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kO1cHelperCheckpointCount; ++i)
+      checkpoints_valid = checkpoints_valid && c->o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[i] == 0;
+  }
+  if (c->o1c_forwards == 0 || critical_overflow || identity_overflow ||
+      !checkpoints_valid || c->o1c_zero_logit_forwards == ~uint64_t(0) ||
+      c->o1c_zero_logit_forwards + 1 != c->o1c_forwards ||
+      c->o1c_attention_forwards != c->o1c_forwards ||
+      c->o1c_helper_forwards > c->o1c_forwards || critical_counts != c->o1c_forwards ||
+      c->o1c_attention_tasks < c->o1c_attention_forwards ||
+      c->o1c_helper_tasks < c->o1c_helper_forwards ||
+      c->o1c_critical_ready_sum + c->o1c_join_tail_sum != c->o1_model_forward)
+    orch_o1c_mark_anomaly(c);
+  orch_o1c_stage_u64(&c->c.dispatch_sum, &c->c.dispatch_sum_hi, c->o1c_dispatch_sum);
+  orch_o1c_stage_u64(
+      &c->c.main_prejoin_sum, &c->c.main_prejoin_sum_hi, c->o1c_main_prejoin_sum);
+  orch_o1c_stage_u64(
+      &c->c.attention_done_sum, &c->c.attention_done_sum_hi, c->o1c_attention_done_sum);
+  orch_o1c_stage_u64(
+      &c->c.helper_done_sum, &c->c.helper_done_sum_hi, c->o1c_helper_done_sum);
+  orch_o1c_stage_u64(&c->c.helper_blend8_return_sum, &c->c.helper_blend8_return_sum_hi,
+      c->o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[0]);
+  orch_o1c_stage_u64(&c->c.helper_blend17_return_sum,
+      &c->c.helper_blend17_return_sum_hi, c->o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[1]);
+  orch_o1c_stage_u64(&c->c.helper_blend26_return_sum,
+      &c->c.helper_blend26_return_sum_hi, c->o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[2]);
+  orch_o1c_stage_u64(&c->c.helper_blend35_return_sum,
+      &c->c.helper_blend35_return_sum_hi, c->o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[3]);
+  orch_o1c_stage_u64(
+      &c->c.critical_ready_sum, &c->c.critical_ready_sum_hi, c->o1c_critical_ready_sum);
+  orch_o1c_stage_u64(&c->c.join_tail_sum, &c->c.join_tail_sum_hi, c->o1c_join_tail_sum);
+  c->c.attention_tasks = orch_o1c_narrow(c, c->o1c_attention_tasks);
+  c->c.helper_tasks = orch_o1c_narrow(c, c->o1c_helper_tasks);
+  c->c.forwards = orch_o1c_narrow(c, c->o1c_forwards);
+  c->c.zero_logit_forwards = orch_o1c_narrow(c, c->o1c_zero_logit_forwards);
+  c->c.attention_forwards = orch_o1c_narrow(c, c->o1c_attention_forwards);
+  c->c.helper_forwards = orch_o1c_narrow(c, c->o1c_helper_forwards);
+  c->c.main_critical = orch_o1c_narrow(c, c->o1c_main_critical);
+  c->c.attention_critical = orch_o1c_narrow(c, c->o1c_attention_critical);
+  c->c.helper_critical = orch_o1c_narrow(c, c->o1c_helper_critical);
+  c->c.critical_ambiguous = orch_o1c_narrow(c, c->o1c_critical_ambiguous);
+}
+
+inline void orch_stage_cycle_prefix(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t t) noexcept {
+  // O1c starts at Stamp B, so its first cycle has a complete measured
+  // identity even though the legacy previous-return timing has no baseline.
+  orch_stage_o1c_cycle_fields(c);
+  orch_o1a_stage_cycle_fields(c);
+  if (c->have_prev) {
+    c->c.t_prev_return = c->prev_return;
+    c->c.d_o1_before_run_forward = orch_o1_narrow(c, c->o1_before_run_forward);
+    c->c.d_o1_setup = orch_o1_narrow(c, c->o1_setup);
+    c->c.d_o1_model_forward = orch_o1_narrow(c, c->o1_model_forward);
+    c->c.d_o1_post_model = orch_o1_narrow(c, c->o1_post_model);
+    c->c.d_o1_pre_run_logits = orch_o1_narrow(c, c->o1_pre_run_logits);
+    c->c.flags |= c->o1_flags;
+    accum32(c, &c->c.d_upstream, c->prev_return, t, nullptr, &c->c.flags);
+    uint64_t o1_parts = uint64_t(c->c.d_o1_before_run_forward) + c->c.d_o1_setup +
+                        c->c.d_o1_model_forward + c->c.d_o1_post_model +
+                        c->c.d_o1_pre_run_logits;
+    if (o1_parts != c->c.d_upstream) c->c.flags |= kCFlagO1SubstageAnomaly;
+  } else {
+    // First-record sentinel: the legacy previous-return O1 fields remain zero.
+    // O1c sums/counts above are complete and retained.
+    c->c.t_prev_return = 0;
+    c->c.d_upstream = 0;
+    c->c.flags |= c->o1_flags | kCFlagFirstRecord;
+  }
+}
+
+// model.hpp run_logits entry (frozen-tree anchor :2084).
+inline region_ctl_t* orch_cycle_begin(
+    const void* state_owner, size_t n_logit_chks) noexcept {
+  if (g.map == nullptr) return nullptr;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  if (c->recording && !o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, true)) return nullptr;
+  if (!c->attached) {
+    uint32_t tid = gettid_u32();
+    if (c->recording && g.o1a_owner_tid != 0 && tid != g.o1a_owner_tid) {
+      orch_o1a_mark_cross_thread(c);
+      return nullptr;
+    }
+    if (c->recording) g.o1a_owner_tid = tid;
+    c->hdr->tid = tid;
+    probe_orch_side();
+    c->attached = true;
+  }
+  if (!c->recording) return c;  // arm D: not one store past this point
+  // Forwards that carry no logit listeners produce no records at all; their
+  // wall time and substage sums are carried by the next recorded cycle's O1.
+  // Take the entry boundary before returning so the next Stamp A can continue
+  // the same exact partition without emitting an extra record.
+  if (n_logit_chks == 0) {
+    orch_o1_run_logits_entry(c, tsc(), true);
+    return c;
+  }
+  std::memset(&g.final_notifier, 0, sizeof(g.final_notifier));
+  uint64_t id = g.cycle_id.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) + 1;
+  g.cycle_id.store(id, std::memory_order_release);  // §4 row 1 publication
+  std::memset(&c->c, 0, sizeof(c->c));
+  c->c.rec_type = kRecCycle;
+  c->c.rec_version = uint8_t(kRecordVersion);
+  c->c.cycle_id = id;
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  orch_o1_run_logits_entry(c, t, false);
+  c->c.t_entry = t;
+  orch_stage_cycle_prefix(c, t);
+  c->cursor = t;
+  c->rec_open = true;
+  return c;
+}
+
+inline void orch_step(region_ctl_t* c, uint32_t* field) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  accum32(c, field, c->cursor, t, nullptr, &c->c.flags);
+  c->cursor = t;
+}
+
+inline void orch_prep_done(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  orch_step(c, c ? &c->c.d_logits_prep : nullptr);
+}
+inline void orch_free_wait_done(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  orch_step(c, c ? &c->c.d_free_wait : nullptr);
+}
+inline void orch_stream_alloc_done(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  orch_step(c, c ? &c->c.d_stream_alloc : nullptr);
+}
+inline void orch_prepared_wait_done(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  orch_step(c, c ? &c->c.d_prepared_wait : nullptr);
+}
+inline void orch_launch_done(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  orch_step(c, c ? &c->c.d_wcls_launch : nullptr);
+}
+inline void orch_eagle_done(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  orch_step(c, c ? &c->c.d_eagle : nullptr);
+}
+
+// model.hpp :2126 — return of logits_done[chk].wait(). Field 3's producer:
+// stamps t_wcls_done and publishes it to the workers (§2 shared slot; the
+// worker-side classification is §4.1 substitute S-2).
+inline void orch_wcls_done(region_ctl_t* c, size_t chk) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  accum32(c, &c->c.d_wcls_done_wait, c->cursor, t, nullptr, &c->c.flags);
+  c->cursor = t;
+  c->c.t_wcls_done = t;
+  if (chk < kMaxChks) g.wcls_done_tsc[chk].v.store(t, std::memory_order_release);
+}
+
+// model.hpp :2129 — return of listeners_notified.wait().
+inline void orch_notified_wait_done(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  accum32(c, &c->c.d_notified_wait, c->cursor, t, nullptr, &c->c.flags);
+  c->cursor = t;
+  c->c.t_listeners_notified = t;
+  c->c.final_notifier_ix = g.final_notifier.notifier_ix;
+  c->c.t_final_notifier_pre_release = g.final_notifier.tsc;
+  c->c.final_notifier_cycle_id = g.final_notifier.cycle_id;
+  if (g.final_notifier.cycle_id == c->c.cycle_id)
+    c->c.final_notifier_src = kFinalNotifierExact;
+}
+
+// model.hpp :2143 — run_logits return.
+inline void orch_cycle_end(region_ctl_t* c,
+    const void* state_owner,
+    uint32_t n_logit_chks,
+    uint32_t n_listeners,
+    uint32_t n_empty_listeners,
+    uint32_t logits_chunk_size,
+    uint32_t max_top_k,
+    uint32_t vocab_size) noexcept {
+  if (!c) return;
+  if (c->recording && !o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, true)) return;
+  if (!c->rec_open) {
+    // A zero-logit run_logits has no REC_CYCLE, but its E->R body still
+    // belongs to the next recorded O1a. Preserve it in the staged sum.
+    if (c->recording && n_logit_chks == 0) {
+      uint64_t r = tsc();
+      orch_o1_zero_return_at(c, r);
+      orch_o1a_zero_return_at(c, r);
+      if (o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aRecordTail) orch_o1a_after_return_at(c, tsc());
+    } else if (c->recording) {
+      // A normal run_logits return without an open record is an unmatched or
+      // duplicate R hook. Keep the failure sticky so the next record exposes
+      // it; recording-off arm D must remain free of timestamps and stores.
+      orch_o1_mark_anomaly(c);
+    }
+    return;
+  }
+  if (c->o1_phase != kO1AwaitRecordedReturn) {
+    orch_o1_mark_anomaly(c);
+    // This anomaly happens after the staged flags were copied at E, so copy
+    // it again before committing the current record.
+    c->c.flags |= c->o1_flags;
+  }
+  if (c->have_prev && o1a_phase_load(c) != kO1aAwaitRecordedReturn)
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  accum32(c, &c->c.d_epilogue, c->cursor, t, nullptr, &c->c.flags);
+  c->c.t_return = t;
+  bool ok = false;
+  uint64_t cpu = cpu_ns_now(&ok);  // §11: exactly one syscall per cycle
+  if (!ok) ++c->hdr->cpu_clock_failures;
+  if (c->c.flags & kCFlagFirstRecord) {
+    c->c.cpu_ns = 0;  // §6.3 sentinel
+  } else if (ok && cpu >= c->prev_cpu_ns) {
+    c->c.cpu_ns = cpu - c->prev_cpu_ns;  // §3.2: reported separately, never
+                                         // added to any wall-clock interval
+  } else {
+    c->c.cpu_ns = 0;
+    if (ok) ++c->hdr->clock_anomalies;
+  }
+  c->c.n_logit_chks = n_logit_chks;
+  c->c.n_listeners = n_listeners;
+  c->c.n_empty_listeners = n_empty_listeners;
+  c->c.logits_chunk_size = logits_chunk_size;
+  c->c.max_top_k = max_top_k;
+  c->c.vocab_size = vocab_size;
+  c->c.seq = c->seq;
+  if (c->hdr->records_written == 0) c->hdr->first_cycle_id = c->c.cycle_id;
+  c->hdr->last_cycle_id = c->c.cycle_id;
+  bool committed = commit_record(c, &c->c);
+  ++c->seq;
+  c->prev_return = t;
+  if (ok) c->prev_cpu_ns = cpu;
+  c->have_prev = true;
+  c->rec_open = false;
+  if (!committed) {
+    orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+    o1a_phase_store(c, kO1aInvalid);
+    return;
+  }
+  orch_o1_reset_after_record(c, t);
+  orch_o1a_clear_aggregate(c, true);
+  orch_o1a_seed_at(c, t);
+  orch_o1a_after_return_at(c, tsc());
+}
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// Worker hooks (regions 0..3). The P1..P9 chain telescopes exactly, so
+// identity W is a 0-tick identity by construction (§7.1). Under stride > 1
+// the parser checks the extent variant instead of P4a+P4b (§11).
+// ===========================================================================
+
+// model.hpp :2145 — run_listener_notifications entry.
+inline region_ctl_t* worker_entry(
+    const void* state_owner, size_t worker_ix, size_t n_logit_chks) noexcept {
+  if (g.map == nullptr || worker_ix >= kNWorkers) return nullptr;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[worker_ix];
+  if (c->recording && !o1a_state_owner_gate(state_owner, false)) return nullptr;
+  if (!c->attached) {
+    c->hdr->tid = gettid_u32();
+    probe_worker_side(uint32_t(worker_ix));
+    c->attached = true;
+  }
+  if (!c->recording) return c;      // arm D: not one store past this point
+  if (n_logit_chks == 0) return c;  // DEV-6, mirrors the orchestrator
+  tls = c;
+  c->s0 = tsc();                                   // S0
+  c->cpu_entry_ns = cpu_ns_now(&c->cpu_entry_ok);  // §3.2 first read of the pair
+  if (!c->cpu_entry_ok) ++c->hdr->cpu_clock_failures;
+  ++c->worker_cycle_seq;
+  return c;
+}
+
+// model.hpp :2162 — top of the per-logit-chunk loop; opens this chunk's record.
+inline void worker_chunk_begin(region_ctl_t* c, size_t chk, size_t n_chks) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->recording || tls != c) return;
+  if (chk >= kMaxChks || n_chks > 255) {
+    ++c->hdr->records_dropped;  // §5.4: fail loud, never misclassify
+    c->rec_open = false;
+    return;
+  }
+  std::memset(&c->w, 0, sizeof(c->w));
+  c->w.rec_type = kRecWorker;
+  c->w.rec_version = uint8_t(kRecordVersion);
+  c->w.worker_ix = uint8_t(c->region_ix);
+  c->w.chk_ix = uint8_t(chk);
+  c->w.n_chks = uint8_t(n_chks);
+  c->w.wcls_src = kWclsUnavailable;
+  if (c->stride > 1) c->w.flags |= kWFlagStrideActive;
+  // t_entry is S0 for chunk 0 and the previous chunk's S9 otherwise, so the
+  // per-chunk records tile the invocation with no gap and no overlap.
+  c->w.t_entry = (chk == 0) ? c->s0 : c->cursor;
+  c->cursor = c->w.t_entry;
+  c->prev_block_exit = 0;
+  c->rx_entry = 0;
+  c->stream_blocks = 0;
+  c->stream_blocks_seen = 0;
+  c->rec_open = true;
+}
+
+// model.hpp :2166 — return of logits_allocated[chk].wait(). S1 / P1.
+// The cycle id is acquire-loaded HERE, not at :2145: the pool lambdas are
+// enqueued before run_logits is called (model.hpp:1220-1223), so an
+// entry-time load can race the orchestrator's publication (design notes
+// DEV-2). This wait is downstream of the orchestrator's count_down at :2116,
+// hence downstream of the publication at :2084.
+inline void worker_alloc_wait_done(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  accum32(c, &c->w.d_pre_wcls_wait, c->cursor, t, &c->w.flags, nullptr);
+  c->cursor = t;
+  c->w.cycle_id = g.cycle_id.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
+  c->w.wcls_src = kWclsOrchPublished;  // §4.1 substitute S-2 default
+}
+
+// model.hpp :2171 — logits_prepared[chk].count_down() (field 8, bit0).
+inline void worker_cd_prepared(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;
+  c->w.releaser_mask |= kRelPrepared;
+}
+
+// model.hpp :2172 — end of the stream-prepare loop. S2 / P2.
+inline void worker_prepare_done(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  accum32(c, &c->w.d_stream_prepare, c->cursor, t, &c->w.flags, nullptr);
+  c->cursor = t;
+}
+
+// model.hpp :2174 — non-streamed path: return of logits_done[chk].wait().
+// S2 / P2 plus the worker's own WCLS observation (§4.1 S-2 fallback).
+inline void worker_done_wait(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  accum32(c, &c->w.d_stream_prepare, c->cursor, t, &c->w.flags, nullptr);
+  c->cursor = t;
+  c->w.cycle_id = g.cycle_id.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
+  c->w.t_wcls_seen = t;
+  c->w.wcls_src = kWclsWorkerWait;
+}
+
+// model.hpp :2226 — stream.start(). blocks_expected (§4.1 row 3): the block
+// count this stream will deliver.
+//
+// TWO DISTINCT STREAM TYPES (build failure 2026-08-03, design-notes risk R-4
+// materialising; see DEV-7 and DEV-13):
+//   * ptensor.hpp:195 — the TP fan-in struct, several result_tiles each with
+//     their own `blocks`. The patch adds g2_total_blocks() there (:223) to sum
+//     them, because no single member holds the total.
+//   * libpos.hpp:162 tron::hardware::stream_result_t — ONE tile, whose
+//     `uint32_t blocks` (:166) IS the total. Reached via the alias at
+//     tensor.hpp:327; four of the 48 model TUs stream through it. DEV-7 keeps
+//     libpos.hpp unmodified, so it has no accessor and never will.
+// Dispatch at compile time on which member exists. Both branches yield the same
+// quantity — the number of blocks this stream will deliver — so blocks_expected
+// stays comparable across model configurations, which §4.1 row 3 requires.
+// Zero runtime cost: the discarded branch is not instantiated.
+template <class Stream>
+inline void stream_start(region_ctl_t* c, Stream& s) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;  // arm D never even sums the tiles
+  uint32_t n;
+  if constexpr (requires { s.g2_total_blocks(); }) {
+    n = uint32_t(s.g2_total_blocks());  // TP fan-in: sum over tiles
+  } else {
+    static_assert(
+        requires { s.blocks; },
+        "g2ring::stream_start: stream type exposes neither "
+        "g2_total_blocks() nor a `blocks` member; blocks_expected "
+        "(schema field 6/7 denominator) cannot be established. Add a "
+        "gated accessor for this stream type rather than guessing.");
+    n = uint32_t(s.blocks);  // single tile: `blocks` is the total
+  }
+  c->stream_blocks = n;
+  c->stream_blocks_seen = 0;
+  c->prev_block_exit = 0;
+  c->rx_entry = 0;
+  c->w.blocks_expected = addsat32(c->w.blocks_expected, n);
+}
+
+// model.hpp :2230 — stream.try_consume(...) returned false (libpos.hpp:192).
+inline void poll_false(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;
+  if (c->w.poll_false_returns != 0xFFFFFFFFu) ++c->w.poll_false_returns;
+}
+
+// model.hpp :2231 — consume-lambda entry. Fields 4, 6, 7, 9.
+inline void rx_begin(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;
+  ++c->stream_blocks_seen;
+  // §4.1 substitute S-2 with the cross-cycle guard of design notes DEV-4:
+  // a block is "after" iff the published slot value belongs to THIS cycle,
+  // i.e. is at least the record's t_entry. A stale value left by the previous
+  // cycle is strictly below t_entry, so it classifies as "before", as it must.
+  // This is a per-block relaxed load and a compare — never a stamp — so the
+  // split stays exact at any stride (§11).
+  uint64_t v = g.wcls_done_tsc[c->w.chk_ix].v.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  bool after = (v != 0) && (v >= c->w.t_entry);
+  if (after && c->w.t_wcls_seen == 0) c->w.t_wcls_seen = v;
+  c->rx_after_flag = after;
+  bool first_of_stream = (c->prev_block_exit == 0);
+  bool last_of_stream =
+      (c->stream_blocks != 0 && c->stream_blocks_seen >= c->stream_blocks);
+  bool sample = (c->stride <= 1) || (c->stream_blocks == 0) || first_of_stream ||
+                last_of_stream || (((c->stream_blocks_seen - 1) % c->stride) == 0);
+  if (!sample) {
+    c->rx_entry = 0;
+    return;
+  }
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  c->rx_entry = t;
+  if (first_of_stream) {
+    // P3: the wait for this listener's first block. For listener j+1 this
+    // interval begins at listener j's S8, which is where cursor sits (§7.1).
+    accum32(c, &c->w.d_pre_first_rx, c->cursor, t, &c->w.flags, nullptr);
+    c->cursor = t;
+    if (c->w.t_first_rx == 0) c->w.t_first_rx = t;  // S3, first across listeners
+  } else {
+    uint64_t gap = (t >= c->prev_block_exit) ? (t - c->prev_block_exit) : 0;
+    accum32(c, &c->w.d_rx_gap, c->prev_block_exit, t, &c->w.flags, nullptr);
+    if (gap > uint64_t(c->w.d_max_rx_gap))
+      c->w.d_max_rx_gap = (gap > 0xFFFFFFFFull) ? 0xFFFFFFFFu : uint32_t(gap);
+  }
+}
+
+// model.hpp :2232-2233 — consume-lambda exit. Field 5 and the entry counts.
+// The last block of every stream is always stamped, so t_last_rx_end (S4) and
+// therefore the P4 extent stay exact at any stride (§11).
+inline void rx_end(region_ctl_t* c, size_t n_entries) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;
+  uint32_t ne = (n_entries > 0xFFFFFFFFull) ? 0xFFFFFFFFu : uint32_t(n_entries);
+  if (c->rx_after_flag) {
+    ++c->w.blocks_after;
+    c->w.entries_after = addsat32(c->w.entries_after, ne);
+  } else {
+    ++c->w.blocks_before;
+    c->w.entries_before = addsat32(c->w.entries_before, ne);
+  }
+  bool last_of_stream =
+      (c->stream_blocks != 0 && c->stream_blocks_seen >= c->stream_blocks);
+  if (c->rx_entry == 0 && !last_of_stream) return;  // strided-out block
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  if (c->rx_entry != 0)
+    accum32(c, &c->w.d_rx_insert, c->rx_entry, t, &c->w.flags, nullptr);
+  c->prev_block_exit = t;
+  c->w.t_last_rx_end = t;  // S4
+  c->cursor = t;
+  c->rx_entry = 0;
+}
+
+// model.hpp :2237 — the bare accum.sort() call. S5 / P5 starts immediately
+// before that statement; there is no surrounding TRACE_EVENT pair at this
+// anchor (§4 row 12).
+inline void sort_begin(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  accum32(c, &c->w.d_post_last_rx, c->cursor, t, &c->w.flags, nullptr);
+  c->cursor = t;
+}
+
+// model.hpp :2237 — accum.sort() return. S6 / P6, plus fields 10 and 11
+// harvested from the accumulator (top_k_accum.hpp:33-35 and :36-39).
+template <class Accum>
+inline void sort_end(region_ctl_t* c, const Accum& a) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  accum32(c, &c->w.d_sort, c->cursor, t, &c->w.flags, nullptr);
+  c->cursor = t;
+  c->w.heap_pushes = addsat32(c->w.heap_pushes, a.g2_pushes);
+  c->w.heap_replacements = addsat32(c->w.heap_replacements, a.g2_replacements);
+}
+
+inline void set_flag(region_ctl_t* c, uint8_t bit) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open || bit == 0) return;
+  c->w.flags |= bit;
+}
+
+// model.hpp :2194 — the callback call. S7 / P7.
+inline void cb_begin(region_ctl_t* c, size_t listener) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  accum32(c, &c->w.d_post_sort, c->cursor, t, &c->w.flags, nullptr);
+  c->cursor = t;
+  if (c->w.n_listeners_served == 0) c->w.listener_id = uint16_t(listener);
+}
+
+// model.hpp :2194 — the callback return. S8 / P8.
+inline void cb_end(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  accum32(c, &c->w.d_callback, c->cursor, t, &c->w.flags, nullptr);
+  c->cursor = t;
+  if (c->w.n_listeners_served != 0xFFFFu) ++c->w.n_listeners_served;
+}
+
+// Shared record close: S9 / P9, cpu_ns, commit.
+inline void worker_close(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t t_exit) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;
+  accum32(c, &c->w.d_countdown, c->cursor, t_exit, &c->w.flags, nullptr);
+  c->cursor = t_exit;
+  c->w.t_exit = t_exit;
+  // Non-final chunk records have no listeners_notified count-down of their
+  // own. Pin the field to t_exit so V8 has a defined, in-range sentinel.
+  // Exact final-notifier identity is stored in REC_CYCLE, never inferred from
+  // these detailed-worker stamps.
+  if (c->w.t_countdown_notified == 0) c->w.t_countdown_notified = t_exit;
+  bool ok = false;
+  uint64_t cpu = cpu_ns_now(&ok);  // §3.2 second read of the pair
+  if (!ok) {
+    ++c->hdr->cpu_clock_failures;
+    c->w.cpu_ns = 0;
+  } else if (c->cpu_entry_ok && cpu >= c->cpu_entry_ns) {
+    c->w.cpu_ns = cpu - c->cpu_entry_ns;
+  } else {
+    c->w.cpu_ns = 0;
+  }
+  c->cpu_entry_ns = ok ? cpu : c->cpu_entry_ns;  // chunk c+1's entry reading
+  c->cpu_entry_ok = c->cpu_entry_ok && ok;
+  c->w.seq = c->seq;
+  c->w.worker_cycle_seq = c->worker_cycle_seq;
+  if (c->w.cycle_id == 0) ++c->hdr->cycle_id_mismatches;  // never observed the id
+  if (c->hdr->records_written == 0) c->hdr->first_cycle_id = c->w.cycle_id;
+  c->hdr->last_cycle_id = c->w.cycle_id;
+  commit_record(c, &c->w);
+  ++c->seq;
+  c->rec_open = false;
+}
+
+// model.hpp :2196 — logits_free[chk].count_down() (field 8, bit1). Also the
+// close point for every chunk record except the last.
+inline void worker_cd_free(region_ctl_t* c, size_t chk, size_t n_chks) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;
+  c->w.releaser_mask |= kRelFree;
+  if (chk + 1 < n_chks) worker_close(c, tsc());
+}
+
+// Immediately before the notifier countdown. This keeps a per-detailed-worker
+// input stamp. Exact final-notifier identity comes from worker_final_notifier.
+inline void worker_cd_notified(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c || !c->rec_open) return;
+  c->w.t_countdown_notified = tsc();
+}
+
+// Called only by latch_ref::count_down_with_finalizer after it has claimed
+// 1 -> FINALIZING and before it release-stores zero. The latch hand-off makes
+// these slot writes visible before the orchestrator returns from wait().
+inline void worker_final_notifier(size_t notifier_ix) noexcept {
+  uint64_t t = tsc();
+  uint64_t cycle_id = g.cycle_id.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
+  g.final_notifier.cycle_id = cycle_id;
+  g.final_notifier.tsc = t;
+  g.final_notifier.notifier_ix =
+      notifier_ix > 0xFFFFFFFFull ? 0xFFFFFFFFu : uint32_t(notifier_ix);
+}
+
+// model.hpp immediately before listeners_notified countdown. S9. Committing
+// here guarantees all four detailed records are published before the final
+// notifier can release the orchestrator.
+inline void worker_exit(region_ctl_t* c) noexcept {
+  if (!c) return;
+  if (c->rec_open) worker_close(c, tsc());
+  tls = nullptr;
+}
+
+// ===========================================================================
+// §5.6 dump. One record-v7 file of exactly kFileSize bytes:
+//   [0]                    256 B file header (built here; file-only framing)
+//   [256]                  5 x 6,295,552 B regions, straight from the mapping
+//   [256 + 5*6,295,552]    16 B trailer "ENDRING\0" + total_bytes(u64)
+//
+// THREE TRIGGERS, one dump (§5.6):
+//   1. the explicit tron_diag_ring_dump() call in runtron's main(), after
+//      eval() has returned — the normal path, and the only one §5.6 names;
+//   2. atexit(&dump), registered at init as the backstop for any other
+//      caller of the library (test binaries, rinzler);
+//   3. dump_from_crash(), called from tron's ALREADY-INSTALLED std::terminate
+//      and SIGSEGV handlers (src/common/backtrace.cpp) before their abort().
+// dump_impl() is idempotent through the g.dumped compare-exchange, so the
+// first trigger to arrive writes the file and the rest return immediately.
+//
+// Async-signal safety of trigger 3, stated exactly. ring.bin is produced with
+// open/write/fsync/close, clock_gettime, nanosleep, rdtsc, memcpy/memset and a
+// lock-free atomic loads/compare-exchanges — all async-signal-safe. The paths and the
+// success line are pre-formed at init, so no snprintf runs here. The ONE
+// non-safe step, ring.meta.json (fopen/fprintf/fclose), is skipped when
+// signal_context is true; §9.1 makes the binary header authoritative and the
+// meta file convenience, and ring_parse.py reads only ring.bin.
+// Residual limits, not worked around: `_exit()`, SIGKILL, SIGTERM, SIGINT and
+// a raw abort()/raise(SIGABRT) (a glibc assert, for instance) still produce no
+// ring.bin, because no handler exists for them and §5.6 forbids installing
+// one. A record being written by another thread at fault time may be captured
+// torn, which is exactly the case §5.5's release-store commit was specified
+// for: the parser either sees a complete record or does not count it.
+// ===========================================================================
+inline bool write_all(int fd, const void* p, size_t n) noexcept {
+  const uint8_t* b = static_cast<const uint8_t*>(p);
+  while (n > 0) {
+    ssize_t w = write(fd, b, n);
+    if (w < 0) {
+      if (errno == EINTR) continue;
+      return false;
+    }
+    b += size_t(w);
+    n -= size_t(w);
+  }
+  return true;
+}
+
+inline void o1c_dump_fail_if_open() noexcept {
+  if (!g.recording) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  uint8_t phase = o1c_phase_load(c);
+  uint64_t active_epoch = g.o1c.active_epoch.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
+  uint32_t configured = o1c_configured_load();
+  if (phase == kO1cIdle && active_epoch == 0 && configured == 0) return;
+
+  // lane_anomalies is serialized in the region header and V7 treats any
+  // non-zero value as fatal. Saturate instead of wrapping, and use compiler
+  // atomics because the crash trigger may run on a different thread. The
+  // dump CAS calls this exactly once, so repeated explicit/atexit/crash
+  // triggers cannot increment it again. No committed record is edited.
+  uint32_t old = __atomic_load_n(&c->hdr->lane_anomalies, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
+  while (
+      old != 0xFFFFFFFFu && !__atomic_compare_exchange_n(&c->hdr->lane_anomalies, &old,
+                                old + 1, false, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE)) {
+  }
+}
+
+inline void o1a_dump_fail_if_open() noexcept {
+  if (!g.recording) return;
+  region_ctl_t* c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+  uint8_t phase = o1a_phase_load(c);
+  if (phase == kO1aTerminalIdle) return;
+  if (phase == kO1aNoSeed && !c->have_prev) return;
+  // A normal run must execute T after the work-queue loop. A crash or a
+  // missing T leaves an active unpublished prefix; preserve fatal header
+  // evidence without editing any committed record.
+  orch_o1a_mark_anomaly(c);
+}
+
+inline void dump_impl(bool signal_context) noexcept {
+  if (g.map == nullptr) return;
+  // Idempotence gate. A single CAS 0 -> 1: exactly one caller can ever proceed
+  // past this point, whichever of the three triggers arrives first, and a
+  // second call from the same or another thread returns here. This is what
+  // makes the explicit exit-path call and the atexit backstop composable.
+  uint32_t expected = 0;
+  if (!g.dumped.compare_exchange_strong(expected, 1, std::memory_order_acq_rel))
+    return;  // idempotent: §5.6 allows exactly one dump per process
+
+  // A B/CONFIG/dispatch/join sequence without its matching C must not let
+  // older committed records look valid. Record fatal header evidence before
+  // any bytes are serialized. Arm D retains its all-zero O1c extension.
+  o1c_dump_fail_if_open();
+  o1a_dump_fail_if_open();
+
+  g.calib_dump_rate_q32 = local_rate_q32();
+  (void)sample_calib(&g.calib_dump_tsc, &g.calib_dump_mono, &g.calib_dump_real);
+
+  // Recompute both local-vs-long-span residuals with integer arithmetic and
+  // round upward. A missing rate or invalid span fails closed.
+  g.calib_residual_ppm = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
+  if (g.calib_dump_mono > g.calib_load_mono && g.calib_dump_tsc > g.calib_load_tsc) {
+    uint64_t d_tsc = g.calib_dump_tsc - g.calib_load_tsc;
+    uint64_t d_mono = g.calib_dump_mono - g.calib_load_mono;
+    uint32_t load_ppm = calibration_ppm(d_tsc, d_mono, g.calib_load_rate_q32);
+    uint32_t dump_ppm = calibration_ppm(d_tsc, d_mono, g.calib_dump_rate_q32);
+    g.calib_residual_ppm = load_ppm > dump_ppm ? load_ppm : dump_ppm;
+  }
+
+  file_hdr_t h;
+  std::memset(&h, 0, sizeof(h));
+  std::memcpy(h.magic, "TRONRING", 8);
+  h.format_version = kFormatVersion;
+  h.record_version = kRecordVersion;
+  h.endian_marker = kEndianMarker;
+  h.record_size = kRecSize;
+  h.n_regions = kNRegions;
+  h.n_detailed_workers = kNWorkers;
+  h.capacity_records = kCapacity;
+  h.header_size = kFileHdrSize;
+  h.region_stride = kRegionStride;
+  h.calib_load_tsc = g.calib_load_tsc;
+  h.calib_load_mono_raw_ns = g.calib_load_mono;
+  h.calib_load_realtime_ns = g.calib_load_real;
+  h.calib_dump_tsc = g.calib_dump_tsc;
+  h.calib_dump_mono_raw_ns = g.calib_dump_mono;
+  h.calib_dump_realtime_ns = g.calib_dump_real;
+  h.calib_residual_ppm = g.calib_residual_ppm;
+  h.tsc_flags = g.tsc_flags;
+  h.rx_stamp_stride = g.stride;
+  h.recording_enabled = g.recording ? 1u : 0u;
+  h.arm_label[0] = uint8_t(g.recording ? 'E' : 'D');  // §9.1: "B" is never valid
+  std::memcpy(h.run_id, g.run_id, sizeof(h.run_id));
+  std::memcpy(h.exe_sha256, g.exe_sha256, 32);
+  std::memcpy(h.zerofill_digest, g.zerofill_digest, 32);
+  h.calib_load_rate_q32 = g.calib_load_rate_q32;
+  h.calib_dump_rate_q32 = g.calib_dump_rate_q32;
+  h.n_notifier_tasks = g.n_notifier_tasks;
+  h.calib_window_ms = kCalibWindowMs;
+  h.calib_samples = kCalibSamples;
+
+  const char* path = g.path_bin;  // pre-formed at init: no snprintf here
+  int fd = open(path, O_WRONLY | O_CREAT | O_TRUNC, 0644);
+  if (fd < 0) {
+    emit("[g2ring] cannot open ");
+    emit(path);
+    emit("; no dump (failed draw)\n");
+    return;
+  }
+  bool ok = write_all(fd, &h, sizeof(h));
+  if (ok) ok = write_all(fd, g.map, size_t(kRingBytes));
+  uint8_t trailer[16];
+  std::memcpy(trailer, "ENDRING", 7);
+  trailer[7] = 0;
+  uint64_t total = kFileSize;
+  std::memcpy(trailer + 8, &total, 8);
+  if (ok) ok = write_all(fd, trailer, sizeof(trailer));
+  if (fsync(fd) != 0) ok = false;
+  if (close(fd) != 0) ok = false;
+  if (!ok) {
+    emit("[g2ring] short write to ");
+    emit(path);
+    emit(" (failed draw)\n");
+    return;
+  }
+
+  // ring.meta.json is the only non-async-signal-safe step (stdio takes a lock
+  // and may allocate), so the crash path skips it. §9.1: the binary header is
+  // authoritative, the meta file is convenience, and ring_parse.py reads only
+  // ring.bin. A crash dump therefore has ring.bin and no ring.meta.json.
+  if (signal_context) {
+    emit("[g2ring] crash-path dump: ring.meta.json skipped (9.1 binary header"
+         " is authoritative)\n");
+    if (g.msg_wrote_len) {
+      ssize_t w = write(2, g.msg_wrote, size_t(g.msg_wrote_len));
+      (void)w;
+    }
+    return;
+  }
+
+  const char* mpath = g.path_meta;  // pre-formed at init
+  FILE* mf = std::fopen(mpath, "w");
+  if (mf) {
+    std::fprintf(mf,
+        "{\n"
+        "  \"schema\": \"%s\",\n"
+        "  \"format_version\": %u,\n  \"record_version\": %u,\n"
+        "  \"record_size\": %u,\n  \"n_regions\": %u,\n"
+        "  \"n_detailed_workers\": %u,\n  \"n_notifier_tasks\": %u,\n"
+        "  \"capacity_records\": %u,\n  \"header_size\": %u,\n"
+        "  \"region_stride\": %llu,\n  \"total_bytes\": %llu,\n"
+        "  \"arm_label\": \"%c\",\n  \"recording_enabled\": %u,\n"
+        "  \"rx_stamp_stride\": %u,\n  \"tsc_flags\": %u,\n"
+        "  \"calib_residual_ppm\": %u,\n"
+        "  \"calib_load_rate_q32\": %llu,\n"
+        "  \"calib_dump_rate_q32\": %llu,\n"
+        "  \"calib_window_ms\": %u,\n  \"calib_samples\": %u,\n"
+        "  \"calib_load\": {\"tsc\": %llu, \"mono_raw_ns\": %llu, "
+        "\"realtime_ns\": %llu},\n"
+        "  \"calib_dump\": {\"tsc\": %llu, \"mono_raw_ns\": %llu, "
+        "\"realtime_ns\": %llu},\n"
+        "  \"run_id\": \"%s\",\n"
+        "  \"note\": \"the binary header is authoritative (9.1)\"\n}\n",
+        kMetaSchema, h.format_version, h.record_version, h.record_size, h.n_regions,
+        h.n_detailed_workers, h.n_notifier_tasks, h.capacity_records, h.header_size,
+        (unsigned long long)h.region_stride, (unsigned long long)kFileSize,
+        char(h.arm_label[0]), h.recording_enabled, h.rx_stamp_stride, h.tsc_flags,
+        h.calib_residual_ppm, (unsigned long long)h.calib_load_rate_q32,
+        (unsigned long long)h.calib_dump_rate_q32, h.calib_window_ms, h.calib_samples,
+        (unsigned long long)h.calib_load_tsc,
+        (unsigned long long)h.calib_load_mono_raw_ns,
+        (unsigned long long)h.calib_load_realtime_ns,
+        (unsigned long long)h.calib_dump_tsc,
+        (unsigned long long)h.calib_dump_mono_raw_ns,
+        (unsigned long long)h.calib_dump_realtime_ns, g.run_id);
+    std::fclose(mf);
+  }
+  if (g.msg_wrote_len) {  // the exact same line as before, pre-formatted
+    ssize_t w = write(2, g.msg_wrote, size_t(g.msg_wrote_len));
+    (void)w;
+  }
+}
+
+// The two public entry points. Both are noinline so each keeps a symbol of its
+// own in the binary and the §5.6 call sites are visible as direct calls in
+// .text — the disassembly evidence the schema asks for.
+inline __attribute__((noinline)) void dump() noexcept {
+  dump_impl(false);
+}
+inline __attribute__((noinline)) void dump_from_crash() noexcept {
+  dump_impl(true);
+}
+
+}  // namespace g2ring
+}  // namespace tron
+
+// The name §5.6 of the schema and design-notes DEV-12 use for the explicit
+// dump entry point. tron::g2ring::dump() is idempotent (a compare-exchange on
+// g.dumped), so calling this in addition to the atexit registration is safe.
+//
+// extern "C" so the symbol in the binary is literally `tron_diag_ring_dump`,
+// the name §5.6 uses; `used` so it survives -O3 GlobalDCE even in translation
+// units that only include the header; `noinline` so the call from runtron's
+// main() is a real call instruction that `nm` and objdump can both find. The
+// §8.2 disassembly audit checks for exactly this symbol and this call.
+extern "C" __attribute__((used, noinline)) inline void tron_diag_ring_dump() noexcept {
+  tron::g2ring::dump();
+}
+

+#pragma once
+
+#include <algorithm>
+#include <array>

+#include <charconv>

+#include <cstdint>

+#include <cstring>
+#include <exception>

+#include <memory>

+#include <stdexcept>
