+namespace tron::expert_stats_capture {
+
+inline constexpr char control_dir_env[] = "TRON_EXPERT_STATS_CONTROL_DIR";
+inline constexpr char control_token_env[] = "TRON_EXPERT_STATS_CONTROL_TOKEN";
+inline constexpr char barrier_timeout_env[] = "TRON_EXPERT_STATS_BARRIER_TIMEOUT_MS";
+
+enum class phase { before, after };
+
+struct config {
+  bool enabled = false;
+  std::string control_dir;
+  std::string token;
+  std::chrono::milliseconds timeout{0};
+};
+
+namespace detail {
+
+inline bool is_hex(char c) {
+  return (c >= '0' && c <= '9') || (c >= 'a' && c <= 'f') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'F');
+}
+
+inline std::runtime_error error(std::string_view message) {
+  return std::runtime_error("expert-stats capture barrier: " + std::string(message));
+}
+
+inline std::runtime_error errno_error(std::string_view operation) {
+  return error(std::string(operation) + ": " + std::strerror(errno));
+}
+
+inline void validate_config(config const& cfg) {
+  if (!cfg.enabled) return;
+  std::filesystem::path const path(cfg.control_dir);
+  if (cfg.control_dir.empty() || !path.is_absolute() ||
+      !cfg.control_dir.starts_with("/var/tmp/") || path.lexically_normal() != path) {
+    throw error("control directory must be a normalized path below /var/tmp");
+  }
+  if (cfg.token.size() != 32 ||
+      !std::all_of(cfg.token.begin(), cfg.token.end(), is_hex)) {
+    throw error("control token must contain exactly 32 hexadecimal characters");
+  }
+  if (cfg.timeout.count() <= 0 || cfg.timeout.count() > 600'000) {
+    throw error("barrier timeout must be in the range 1..600000 ms");
+  }
+}
+
+inline std::chrono::milliseconds parse_timeout(std::string_view text) {
+  uint64_t value = 0;
+  auto const [end, ec] = std::from_chars(text.data(), text.data() + text.size(), value);
+  if (ec != std::errc{} || end != text.data() + text.size() || value == 0 ||
+      value > 600'000) {
+    throw error("barrier timeout must be a decimal value in the range 1..600000 ms");
+  }
+  return std::chrono::milliseconds(value);
+}
+
+inline void validate_protocol_text(std::string_view value, std::string_view label) {
+  if (value.empty() || value.size() > 4096 ||
+      value.find_first_of("\t\r\n") != std::string_view::npos) {
+    throw error(std::string(label) + " is empty, too long, or contains a delimiter");
+  }
+}
+
+class fd {
+public:
+  fd() = default;
+  explicit fd(int value)
+  : value_(value) {}
+  ~fd() {
+    if (value_ >= 0) ::close(value_);
+  }
+  fd(fd const&) = delete;
+  fd& operator=(fd const&) = delete;
+  fd(fd&& other) noexcept
+  : value_(std::exchange(other.value_, -1)) {}
+  fd& operator=(fd&& other) noexcept {
+    if (this != &other) {
+      if (value_ >= 0) ::close(value_);
+      value_ = std::exchange(other.value_, -1);
+    }
+    return *this;
+  }
+  int get() const {
+    return value_;
+  }
+
+private:
+  int value_ = -1;
+};
+
+inline void validate_owner_and_mode(
+    struct stat const& st, mode_t type, mode_t mode, std::string_view label) {
+  if ((st.st_mode & S_IFMT) != type) {
+    throw error(std::string(label) + " has the wrong file type");
+  }
+  if (st.st_uid != geteuid()) {
+    throw error(std::string(label) + " is not owned by the current user");
+  }
+  if ((st.st_mode & 07777) != mode) {
+    throw error(std::string(label) + " has the wrong permissions");
+  }
+}
+
+inline fd open_control_directory(std::string const& path_text) {
+  std::filesystem::path const path(path_text);
+  fd current(::open("/", O_RDONLY | O_DIRECTORY | O_CLOEXEC));
+  if (current.get() < 0) throw errno_error("open filesystem root");
+
+  auto const relative = path.relative_path();
+  dev_t var_tmp_device = 0;
+  size_t component_index = 0;
+  for (auto part = relative.begin(); part != relative.end(); ++part) {
+    auto next = part;
+    bool const final = ++next == relative.end();
+    std::string const name = part->string();
+    struct stat before{};
+    if (::fstatat(current.get(), name.c_str(), &before, AT_SYMLINK_NOFOLLOW) != 0) {
+      throw errno_error("fstatat control directory component");
+    }
+    if ((before.st_mode & S_IFMT) != S_IFDIR) {
+      throw error("control directory contains a non-directory or symlink component");
+    }
+
+    fd opened(::openat(
+        current.get(), name.c_str(), O_RDONLY | O_DIRECTORY | O_CLOEXEC | O_NOFOLLOW));
+    if (opened.get() < 0) throw errno_error("open control directory component");
+    struct stat after{};
+    if (::fstat(opened.get(), &after) != 0) {
+      throw errno_error("fstat control directory component");
+    }
+    if ((after.st_mode & S_IFMT) != S_IFDIR || before.st_dev != after.st_dev ||
+        before.st_ino != after.st_ino) {
+      throw error("control directory component changed while it was opened");
+    }
+    if (component_index == 1) {
+      struct statfs filesystem{};
+      if (::fstatfs(opened.get(), &filesystem) != 0) {
+        throw errno_error("fstatfs /var/tmp");
+      }
+      if (filesystem.f_type == NFS_SUPER_MAGIC) {
+        throw error("control directory must not be on NFS");
+      }
+      var_tmp_device = after.st_dev;
+    }
+    if (final) {
+      validate_owner_and_mode(after, S_IFDIR, 0700, "control directory");
+      if (after.st_dev != var_tmp_device) {
+        throw error("control directory must use the /var/tmp filesystem");
+      }
+    }
+    current = std::move(opened);
+    ++component_index;
+  }
+  return current;
+}
+
+inline fd open_fifo(int directory, char const* name) {
+  struct stat before{};
+  if (::fstatat(directory, name, &before, AT_SYMLINK_NOFOLLOW) != 0) {
+    throw errno_error(std::string("fstatat ") + name);
+  }
+  validate_owner_and_mode(before, S_IFIFO, 0600, name);
+  if (before.st_nlink != 1) {
+    throw error(std::string(name) + " must have exactly one hard link");
+  }
+
+  fd result(::openat(directory, name, O_RDWR | O_NONBLOCK | O_CLOEXEC | O_NOFOLLOW));
+  if (result.get() < 0) throw errno_error(std::string("open ") + name);
+  struct stat after{};
+  if (::fstat(result.get(), &after) != 0) {
+    throw errno_error(std::string("fstat ") + name);
+  }
+  validate_owner_and_mode(after, S_IFIFO, 0600, name);
+  if (after.st_nlink != 1) {
+    throw error(std::string(name) + " must have exactly one hard link");
+  }
+  if (before.st_dev != after.st_dev || before.st_ino != after.st_ino) {
+    throw error(std::string(name) + " changed while it was opened");
+  }
+  return result;
+}
+
+inline void require_empty(int descriptor, std::string_view label) {
+  char byte = 0;
+  while (true) {
+    ssize_t const count = ::read(descriptor, &byte, 1);
+    if (count > 0) throw error(std::string(label) + " contains stale or extra data");
+    if (count == 0) throw error(std::string(label) + " unexpectedly reached EOF");
+    if (errno == EINTR) continue;
+    if (errno == EAGAIN || errno == EWOULDBLOCK) return;
+    throw errno_error(std::string("read ") + std::string(label));
+  }
+}
+
+inline std::string nonce() {
+  std::array<unsigned char, 16> bytes{};
+  size_t filled = 0;
+  while (filled < bytes.size()) {
+    ssize_t const count = ::getrandom(bytes.data() + filled, bytes.size() - filled, 0);
+    if (count > 0) {
+      filled += static_cast<size_t>(count);
+      continue;
+    }
+    if (count < 0 && errno == EINTR) continue;
+    if (count < 0) throw errno_error("getrandom");
+    throw error("getrandom returned no data");
+  }
+
+  constexpr char digits[] = "0123456789abcdef";
+  std::string result(bytes.size() * 2, '0');
+  for (size_t i = 0; i < bytes.size(); ++i) {
+    result[2 * i] = digits[bytes[i] >> 4];
+    result[2 * i + 1] = digits[bytes[i] & 0xf];
+  }
+  return result;
+}
+
+inline std::string_view phase_name(phase value) {
+  return value == phase::before ? "before" : "after";
+}
+
+inline void send_ready(int descriptor, std::string const& line) {
+  long const pipe_buf = ::fpathconf(descriptor, _PC_PIPE_BUF);
+  if (pipe_buf <= 0 || line.size() > static_cast<size_t>(pipe_buf)) {
+    throw error("READY message does not fit in one atomic FIFO write");
+  }
+  ssize_t count = -1;
+  do {
+    count = ::write(descriptor, line.data(), line.size());
+  } while (count < 0 && errno == EINTR);
+  if (count < 0) throw errno_error("write READY");
+  if (static_cast<size_t>(count) != line.size()) {
+    throw error("READY message was only partially written");
+  }
+}
+
+inline std::string read_command(
+    int descriptor, std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point deadline) {
+  std::string data;
+  data.reserve(256);
+  while (true) {
+    auto const now = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+    if (now >= deadline) throw error("timed out waiting for RELEASE or ABORT");
+    auto remaining =
+        std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(deadline - now);
+    int timeout = static_cast<int>(std::max<int64_t>(1, remaining.count()));
+    struct pollfd item{.fd = descriptor, .events = POLLIN, .revents = 0};
+    int const ready = ::poll(&item, 1, timeout);
+    if (ready < 0 && errno == EINTR) continue;
+    if (ready < 0) throw errno_error("poll commands.fifo");
+    if (ready == 0) continue;
+    if (item.revents & (POLLERR | POLLHUP | POLLNVAL)) {
+      throw error("commands.fifo reported an error or hangup");
+    }
+    if (!(item.revents & POLLIN)) continue;
+
+    std::array<char, 256> buffer{};
+    while (true) {
+      ssize_t const count = ::read(descriptor, buffer.data(), buffer.size());
+      if (count > 0) {
+        data.append(buffer.data(), static_cast<size_t>(count));
+        if (data.size() > 1024) throw error("command exceeds 1024 bytes");
+        size_t const newline = data.find('\n');
+        if (newline != std::string::npos) {
+          if (newline + 1 != data.size()) {
+            throw error("commands.fifo contains more than one command");
+          }
+          require_empty(descriptor, "commands.fifo");
+          return data;
+        }
+        continue;
+      }
+      if (count == 0) throw error("commands.fifo unexpectedly reached EOF");
+      if (errno == EINTR) continue;
+      if (errno == EAGAIN || errno == EWOULDBLOCK) break;
+      throw errno_error("read commands.fifo");
+    }
+  }
+}
+
+// Once BEFORE has been released, scheduler shutdown must succeed before the
+// matching AFTER barrier can label the counters stable. Either failure masks
+// the workload failure and makes the capture fail closed.
+template <class Shutdown, class After>
+inline void shutdown_then_after(Shutdown&& shutdown, After&& after) {
+  std::forward<Shutdown>(shutdown)();
+  std::forward<After>(after)();
+}
+
+template <class Shutdown, class After>
+inline void finish_after_release(
+    std::exception_ptr workload_failure, Shutdown&& shutdown, After&& after) {
+  shutdown_then_after(std::forward<Shutdown>(shutdown), std::forward<After>(after));
+  if (workload_failure) std::rethrow_exception(workload_failure);
+}
+
+}  // namespace detail
+
+inline config config_from_values(
+    char const* directory, char const* token, char const* timeout) {
+  unsigned const present =
+      (directory ? 1U : 0U) + (token ? 1U : 0U) + (timeout ? 1U : 0U);
+  if (present == 0) return {};
+  if (present != 3) {
+    throw detail::error("all three control environment variables are required");
+  }
+  config result{.enabled = true,
+      .control_dir = directory,
+      .token = token,
+      .timeout = detail::parse_timeout(timeout)};
+  detail::validate_config(result);
+  return result;
+}
+
+inline config config_from_environment() {
+  return config_from_values(std::getenv(control_dir_env),
+      std::getenv(control_token_env), std::getenv(barrier_timeout_env));
+}
+
+class controller {
+public:
+  explicit controller(config cfg)
+  : cfg_(std::move(cfg)) {
+    detail::validate_config(cfg_);
+    state_ = cfg_.enabled ? state::initial : state::disabled;
+  }
+
+  bool enabled() const {
+    return cfg_.enabled;
+  }
+
+  void barrier(phase requested, std::string_view model, std::string_view mountpoint) {
+    if (!cfg_.enabled) return;
+    state const expected =
+        requested == phase::before ? state::initial : state::before_released;
+    state const ready =
+        requested == phase::before ? state::before_ready : state::after_ready;
+    state const released =
+        requested == phase::before ? state::before_released : state::after_released;
+    if (state_ != expected) {
+      state_ = state::failed;
+      throw detail::error("duplicate or out-of-order barrier phase");
+    }
+    state_ = ready;
+
+    try {
+      detail::validate_protocol_text(model, "model id");
+      detail::validate_protocol_text(mountpoint, "FUSE mountpoint");
+      auto const deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + cfg_.timeout;
+      detail::fd directory = detail::open_control_directory(cfg_.control_dir);
+      detail::fd events = detail::open_fifo(directory.get(), "events.fifo");
+      detail::fd commands = detail::open_fifo(directory.get(), "commands.fifo");
+      detail::require_empty(events.get(), "events.fifo");
+      detail::require_empty(commands.get(), "commands.fifo");
+
+      std::string const nonce = detail::nonce();
+      std::string const phase_text(detail::phase_name(requested));
+      std::string const ready_line = "READY\t1\t" + phase_text + "\t" + cfg_.token +
+                                     "\t" + nonce + "\t" + std::to_string(::getpid()) +
+                                     "\t" + std::string(model) + "\t" +
+                                     std::string(mountpoint) + "\n";
+
+      spdlog::apply_all(
+          [](std::shared_ptr<spdlog::logger> const& logger) { logger->flush(); });
+      detail::send_ready(events.get(), ready_line);
+
+      std::string const command = detail::read_command(commands.get(), deadline);
+      std::string const release =
+          "RELEASE\t1\t" + phase_text + "\t" + cfg_.token + "\t" + nonce + "\n";
+      std::string const abort =
+          "ABORT\t1\t" + phase_text + "\t" + cfg_.token + "\t" + nonce + "\n";
+      if (command == abort) throw detail::error("ABORT received from supervisor");
+      if (command != release) {
+        throw detail::error("malformed or mismatched supervisor command");
+      }
+      state_ = released;
+    } catch (...) {
+      state_ = state::failed;
+      throw;
+    }
+  }
+
+private:
+  enum class state {
+    disabled,
+    initial,
+    before_ready,
+    before_released,
+    after_ready,
+    after_released,
+    failed,
+  };
+
+  config cfg_;
+  state state_ = state::disabled;
+};
+
+inline controller& process_controller() {
+  static controller instance(config_from_environment());
+  return instance;
+}
+
+inline void validate_configuration() {
+  (void)process_controller();
+}
+
+inline void validate_capture_shape(controller const& capture,
+    size_t isolated_iterations,
+    size_t batches,
+    bool has_draft_model) {
+  if (!capture.enabled()) return;
+  if (isolated_iterations != 1 || batches != 1 || has_draft_model) {
+    throw detail::error(
+        "capture mode requires one isolated iteration, one batch, and no draft model");
+  }
+}
+
+inline void validate_capture_shape(
+    size_t isolated_iterations, size_t batches, bool has_draft_model) {
+  validate_capture_shape(
+      process_controller(), isolated_iterations, batches, has_draft_model);
+}
+
+inline void barrier(phase requested, std::string_view model) {
+  controller& capture = process_controller();
+  if (!capture.enabled()) return;
+  capture.barrier(requested, model, fuse::stats::get_mountpoint());
+}
+
+}  // namespace tron::expert_stats_capture

index 114a1295bd45546422d660863daf8a6614b9f290..34dcc216f5dd9a2a70eefa34ad9602e467cb296b 100644

@@ -143,6 +143,32 @@ struct latch_ref {
         trigger->full_name(), amt);
   }
 
+#if TRON_DIAG_RING
+  // Claim the final decrement without exposing zero to waiters. The callback
+  // publishes diagnostic evidence, then the release store wakes the waiter.
+  template <class F>
+  bool count_down_with_finalizer(F&& finalizer) const noexcept {
+    constexpr uint64_t finalizing = ~uint64_t{0};
+    auto& n = left();
+    uint64_t old = n.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
+    for (;;) {
+      TRON_ASSERT(old != 0 && old != finalizing, "Over-counted down latch {}",
+          trigger->full_name());
+      if (old == 1) {
+        if (n.compare_exchange_weak(old, finalizing, std::memory_order_acq_rel,
+                std::memory_order_acquire)) {
+          finalizer();
+          n.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
+          return true;
+        }
+      } else if (n.compare_exchange_weak(old, old - 1, std::memory_order_acq_rel,
+                     std::memory_order_acquire)) {
+        return false;
+      }
+    }
+  }
+#endif
+
   void operator++() noexcept {
     left()++;
   }

+-- | Target-only helper checkpoints. MoeBlend numbers are operation ordinals
+-- in first-appearance order, not a generic promise about layer identity.
+-- Fail generation if the frozen gpt-oss-120b graph no longer has the exact
+-- 36-operation shape on which the 8/17/26/35 mapping was validated.
+type MoeBlendCheckpoints = M.Map MoeBlendKey Int
+
+moeBlendCheckpoints :: String -> [Tron.Statement] -> MoeBlendCheckpoints
+moeBlendCheckpoints implName stmts
+  | implName /= "ingested_gpt_oss_120b_impl" = M.empty
+  | length blends /= expectedBlendCount =
+      error $
+        "TronCpp helper checkpoints for ingested_gpt_oss_120b_impl require exactly "
+          ++ show expectedBlendCount
+          ++ " unique MoeBlend operations; found "
+          ++ show (length blends)
+  | otherwise =
+      M.fromList
+        [ (fst (blends !! ordinal), checkpointIx)
+        | (checkpointIx, ordinal) <- zip [0 ..] checkpointOrdinals
+        ]
+ where
+  blends = collectMoeBlends stmts
+  expectedBlendCount = 36
+  checkpointOrdinals = [8, 17, 26, 35]
+

+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+set -euo pipefail
+
+if [[ $# -lt 1 ]]; then
+  echo "usage: $0 generated-ingested-gpt-oss-120b.hpp [generic-header ...]" >&2
+  exit 2
+fi
+
+target=$1
+shift
+[[ -f $target ]]
+
+line_of_once() {
+  local text=$1
+  local count
+  count=$(grep -Fc -- "$text" "$target")
+  if [[ $count -ne 1 ]]; then
+    echo "expected exactly one '$text'; found $count" >&2
+    exit 1
+  fi
+  grep -nF -- "$text" "$target" | cut -d: -f1
+}
+
+expect_line() {
+  local line=$1
+  local expected=$2
+  local label=$3
+  local actual
+  actual=$(sed -n "${line}p" "$target")
+  actual=${actual#"${actual%%[![:space:]]*}"}
+  if [[ $actual != "$expected" ]]; then
+    echo "$label: expected '$expected' at line $line; found '$actual'" >&2
+    exit 1
+  fi
+}
+
+grep -Fq 'struct ingested_gpt_oss_120b_impl' "$target"
+line_of_once \
+  'G2_O1C_CONFIG_HELPER_CHECKPOINTS(&outer_state, n_attn_workers, n_main_helpers, 4)' \
+  > /dev/null
+line_of_once \
+  'run_main_help(q_batches, 1 + i, n_workers, g2_o1c_epoch, i)' \
+  > /dev/null
+
+helper_begin=$(line_of_once 'void run_main_help(')
+helper_end=$(line_of_once 'size_t available_main_parallelism(')
+if ((helper_begin >= helper_end)); then
+  echo 'run_main_help bounds are reversed' >&2
+  exit 1
+fi
+
+total=$(grep -Fc -- 'G2_O1C_HELPER_BLEND_RETURNED(' "$target")
+if [[ $total -ne 4 ]]; then
+  echo "expected four helper checkpoint hooks; found $total" >&2
+  exit 1
+fi
+
+blends=(8 17 26 35)
+for slot in 0 1 2 3; do
+  blend=${blends[$slot]}
+  call="moe_blend_${blend}(q_batch, worker_ix, n_workers);"
+  main_call="moe_blend_${blend}(q_batch, 0, n_workers);"
+  hook="G2_O1C_HELPER_BLEND_RETURNED(&outer_state, g2_o1c_epoch, g2_o1c_helper_ix, ${slot});"
+  hook_line=$(line_of_once "$hook")
+  if ((hook_line <= helper_begin || hook_line >= helper_end)); then
+    echo "checkpoint slot $slot is outside run_main_help" >&2
+    exit 1
+  fi
+  # These layer-specific routing and SwiGLU anchors are generated from the
+  # model graph independently of the lifted moe_blend_N method names. Checking
+  # the whole call neighborhood prevents a 36-blend graph with shifted
+  # ordinals from passing merely because the hook follows moe_blend_N.
+  expect_line "$((hook_line - 9))" \
+    "q_batch->routing_channel_${blend}.start_reading(worker_ix);" \
+    "checkpoint slot $slot routing anchor"
+  expect_line "$((hook_line - 6))" \
+    "kernel_add_swiglu_${blend}(q_batch, worker_ix, n_workers);" \
+    "checkpoint slot $slot SwiGLU anchor"
+  expect_line "$((hook_line - 3))" \
+    'q_batch->matmul_channel.start_reading(worker_ix);' \
+    "checkpoint slot $slot pre-call channel"
+  expect_line "$((hook_line - 2))" "$call" \
+    "checkpoint slot $slot blend call"
+  expect_line "$((hook_line - 1))" '#if TRON_DIAG_RING' \
+    "checkpoint slot $slot diagnostic gate"
+  expect_line "$((hook_line + 1))" '#endif' \
+    "checkpoint slot $slot diagnostic gate end"
+  expect_line "$((hook_line + 2))" \
+    'q_batch->matmul_channel.finish_reading(worker_ix);' \
+    "checkpoint slot $slot post-call channel"
+  expect_line "$((hook_line + 3))" \
+    "q_batch->routing_channel_${blend}.finish_reading(worker_ix);" \
+    "checkpoint slot $slot routing release"
+  main_line=$(line_of_once "$main_call")
+  if ((main_line >= helper_begin)); then
+    echo "main call for blend $blend is not before run_main_help" >&2
+    exit 1
+  fi
+done
+
+while IFS=: read -r hook_line _; do
+  if ((hook_line <= helper_begin || hook_line >= helper_end)); then
+    echo "checkpoint hook outside helper method at line $hook_line" >&2
+    exit 1
+  fi
+done < <(grep -nF -- 'G2_O1C_HELPER_BLEND_RETURNED(' "$target")
+
+for generic in "$@"; do
+  [[ -f $generic ]]
+  if grep -Fq -- 'G2_O1C_HELPER_BLEND_RETURNED(' "$generic"; then
+    echo "target-only checkpoint leaked into generic header $generic" >&2
+    exit 1
+  fi
+  if grep -Fq -- 'G2_O1C_CONFIG_HELPER_CHECKPOINTS(' "$generic"; then
+    echo "target-only config leaked into generic header $generic" >&2
+    exit 1
+  fi
+done
+
+target_sha=$(sha256sum "$target" | awk '{print $1}')
+printf 'generated_helper_target_selftest_pass=true checkpoints=4 target_sha256=%s target=%s\n' \
+  "$target_sha" "$target"

+#include <atomic>
+#include <cstdint>
+#include <x86intrin.h>
+
+// Count every use of the real header's sanctioned TSC primitive.  This is
+// intentionally local to this test translation unit: production code and the
+// production header remain unchanged.
+static std::atomic<uint64_t> g_observed_rdtsc_calls{0};
+
+static inline uint64_t observed_rdtsc() noexcept {
+  g_observed_rdtsc_calls.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  return __rdtsc();
+}
+
+#define __rdtsc() observed_rdtsc()
+#define TRON_DIAG_RING 1
+#include "common/g2_diag_ring.hpp"
+#undef __rdtsc
+
+#include <array>
+#include <cstdio>
+#include <cstring>
+#include <limits>
+#include <thread>
+#include <unistd.h>
+
+#include <sys/wait.h>
+
+namespace {
+
+using namespace tron::g2ring;
+
+const char* g_case_name = nullptr;
+
+[[noreturn]] void fail(const char* expression, int line) {
+  std::fprintf(stderr, "FAIL %s line %d: %s\n", g_case_name, line, expression);
+  _exit(1);
+}
+
+#define CHECK(expression)                                                              \
+  do {                                                                                 \
+    if (!(expression)) fail(#expression, __LINE__);                                    \
+  } while (false)
+
+struct alignas(64) ring_storage_t {
+  std::array<uint8_t, kRegionHdrSize + 4 * kRecSize> bytes{};
+};
+
+struct fixture_t {
+  ring_storage_t storage;
+  int scheduler = 1;
+  int state = 2;
+  int other_executor = 3;
+  int wrong_scheduler = 4;
+  int wrong_state = 5;
+  region_ctl_t* c = nullptr;
+
+  explicit fixture_t(bool recording = true) {
+    // Every case runs in a fresh child process, so the inline global and TLS
+    // objects are still in their zero-initialized process-start state here.
+    g.map = storage.bytes.data();
+    g.recording = recording;
+    c = &g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+    c->base = storage.bytes.data();
+    c->hdr = reinterpret_cast<region_hdr_t*>(storage.bytes.data());
+    c->payload = storage.bytes.data() + kRegionHdrSize;
+    c->region_ix = kOrchRegion;
+    c->recording = recording;
+    c->attached = true;
+    c->stride = 1;
+    c->o1_phase = kO1AwaitA;
+    o1c_phase_store(c, kO1cIdle);
+    c->hdr->worker_ix = kOrchRegion;
+    c->hdr->region_kind = kKindOrch;
+    c->hdr->recording = recording ? 1 : 0;
+  }
+
+  void register_owner() {
+    o1a_register_owner(&scheduler, &state);
+  }
+
+  // Bind the owner thread through the real V0/V1 entry points.  Keeping the
+  // kind-3 executor open until after R is how the first post-sentinel segment
+  // receives the preceding target V1 in production.
+  void bind_pre_sentinel() {
+    register_owner();
+    orch_o1a_executor_begin(&scheduler);
+    CHECK(c->o1a_executor_open);
+    CHECK(c->o1a_executor_kind == 3);
+  }
+};
+
+uint64_t leaf_sum(const region_ctl_t* c) {
+  uint64_t result = 0;
+  for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kO1aLeafCount; ++i)
+    result += c->o1a_leaf[i];
+  return result;
+}
+
+bool aggregate_is_zero(const region_ctl_t* c) {
+  for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kO1aLeafCount; ++i)
+    if (c->o1a_leaf[i] != 0) return false;
+  for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kO1aCountCount; ++i)
+    if (c->o1a_count[i] != 0) return false;
+  for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kO1aHitCount; ++i)
+    if (c->o1a_hit[i] != 0) return false;
+  return c->o1a_cursor == 0 && !c->o1a_wait_open && !c->o1a_queue_loop_open &&
+         !c->o1a_executor_open && !c->o1a_empty_return_pending;
+}
+
+uint64_t staged_leaf_sum(const rec_cycle_t& r) {
+  return uint64_t(r.d_o1a_record_tail) + r.d_o1a_qbatch_teardown +
+         r.d_o1a_previous_exit + r.d_o1a_queue_handoff + r.d_o1a_cv_wait +
+         r.d_o1a_request_apply + r.d_o1a_dispatch_delay + r.d_o1a_forward_args +
+         r.d_o1a_forward_setup + r.d_o1a_state_entry + r.d_o1a_zero_logits_body;
+}
+
+struct segment_builder_t {
+  fixture_t& f;
+  region_ctl_t* c;
+  uint64_t now;
+  uint64_t forwards = 0;
+  uint64_t zero_forwards = 0;
+
+  explicit segment_builder_t(fixture_t& fixture, uint64_t r = 100)
+  : f(fixture)
+  , c(fixture.c)
+  , now(r) {
+    c->have_prev = true;
+    f.bind_pre_sentinel();
+    orch_o1a_seed_at(c, now);
+    orch_o1a_after_return_at(c, advance(1));
+    orch_o1a_executor_end(&f.scheduler);
+    CHECK(!c->o1a_executor_open);
+    CHECK(c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitV1] == 1);
+  }
+
+  uint64_t advance(uint64_t ticks) {
+    now += ticks;
+    return now;
+  }
+
+  void prefix(uint64_t p_to_k = 2, uint64_t k_to_q = 3, uint64_t q_to_u = 5) {
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_transition_at(
+        c, kO1aPreviousExit, kO1aQbatchTeardown, advance(p_to_k)));
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitK]));
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_transition_at(
+        c, kO1aQbatchTeardown, kO1aPreviousExit, advance(k_to_q)));
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitQ]));
+    CHECK(
+        orch_o1a_transition_at(c, kO1aPreviousExit, kO1aQueueHandoff, advance(q_to_u)));
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitU]));
+  }
+
+  void queue_loop(uint64_t before_wait,
+      bool wait,
+      uint64_t wait_ticks,
+      uint64_t after_wait,
+      uint64_t request_apply_ticks) {
+    uint8_t suspended = o1a_phase_load(c);
+    CHECK(suspended == kO1aQueueHandoff || suspended == kO1aDispatchDelay);
+    CHECK(!c->o1a_queue_loop_open);
+    c->o1a_queue_loop_open = true;
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitQ0]));
+
+    if (wait) {
+      CHECK(orch_o1a_flush_at(c, suspended, advance(before_wait)));
+      c->o1a_saved_phase = suspended;
+      c->o1a_wait_open = true;
+      o1a_phase_store(c, kO1aCvWait);
+      CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitW0]));
+
+      CHECK(orch_o1a_flush_at(c, kO1aCvWait, advance(wait_ticks)));
+      c->o1a_wait_open = false;
+      o1a_phase_store(c, suspended);
+      c->o1a_saved_phase = kO1aNoSeed;
+      CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_count[kO1aCountWaits]));
+      CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitW1]));
+    } else {
+      CHECK(before_wait == 0);
+    }
+
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_flush_at(c, suspended, advance(after_wait)));
+    o1a_phase_store(c, kO1aRequestApply);
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_count[kO1aCountQueueLoops]));
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitD]));
+
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_transition_at(
+        c, kO1aRequestApply, kO1aDispatchDelay, advance(request_apply_ticks)));
+    c->o1a_queue_loop_open = false;
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitX]));
+  }
+
+  void other_executor(uint64_t elapsed_dispatch_ticks = 0) {
+    orch_o1a_executor_begin(&f.other_executor);
+    CHECK(c->o1a_executor_open && c->o1a_executor_kind == 2);
+    advance(elapsed_dispatch_ticks);
+    orch_o1a_executor_end(&f.other_executor);
+    CHECK(!c->o1a_executor_open);
+  }
+
+  void empty_sweep(uint64_t f_ticks = 2, uint64_t g_ticks = 3) {
+    orch_o1a_executor_begin(&f.scheduler);
+    CHECK(c->o1a_executor_open && c->o1a_executor_kind == 1);
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_transition_at(
+        c, kO1aDispatchDelay, kO1aForwardArgs, advance(f_ticks)));
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitF]));
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_transition_at(
+        c, kO1aForwardArgs, kO1aDispatchDelay, advance(g_ticks)));
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitG]));
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_count[kO1aCountEmptySweeps]));
+    c->o1a_empty_return_pending = true;
+    orch_o1a_empty_return(&f.scheduler);
+    CHECK(!c->o1a_empty_return_pending);
+    orch_o1a_executor_end(&f.scheduler);
+    CHECK(!c->o1a_executor_open);
+  }
+
+  // Finish one target forward.  A zero forward closes at R and starts the
+  // next P segment.  A recorded forward remains open at E for staging.
+  void finish_forward(bool zero,
+      uint64_t f_ticks = 2,
+      uint64_t g_ticks = 3,
+      uint64_t h_ticks = 4,
+      uint64_t a_ticks = 5,
+      uint64_t entry_gap = 7,
+      uint64_t zero_body_ticks = 11,
+      uint64_t r_to_p_ticks = 1) {
+    orch_o1a_executor_begin(&f.scheduler);
+    CHECK(c->o1a_executor_open && c->o1a_executor_kind == 1);
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_transition_at(
+        c, kO1aDispatchDelay, kO1aForwardArgs, advance(f_ticks)));
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitF]));
+    CHECK(
+        orch_o1a_transition_at(c, kO1aForwardArgs, kO1aForwardSetup, advance(g_ticks)));
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitG]));
+    CHECK(
+        orch_o1a_transition_at(c, kO1aForwardSetup, kO1aStateEntry, advance(h_ticks)));
+    CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(c, &c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitH]));
+    orch_o1a_run_forward_begin_at(c, advance(a_ticks));
+    CHECK(o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aAwaitEntry);
+    ++forwards;
+
+    if (!zero) return;
+    ++zero_forwards;
+    orch_o1a_zero_entry_at(c, advance(entry_gap));
+    CHECK(o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aZeroLogitsBody);
+    orch_o1a_zero_return_at(c, advance(zero_body_ticks));
+    CHECK(o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aRecordTail);
+    orch_o1a_after_return_at(c, advance(r_to_p_ticks));
+    CHECK(o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aPreviousExit);
+    orch_o1a_executor_end(&f.scheduler);
+    CHECK(!c->o1a_executor_open);
+  }
+
+  void stage() {
+    CHECK(o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aAwaitEntry);
+    c->o1_before_run_forward = leaf_sum(c);
+    c->o1c_forwards = forwards;
+    c->o1c_zero_logit_forwards = zero_forwards;
+    uint32_t before = c->hdr->o1a_anomalies;
+    orch_o1a_stage_cycle_fields(c);
+    CHECK(c->hdr->o1a_anomalies == before);
+    CHECK(o1a_phase_load(c) == kO1aAwaitRecordedReturn);
+    CHECK(staged_leaf_sum(c->c) == c->o1_before_run_forward);
+  }
+};
+
+void test_ordinary() {
+  fixture_t f;
+  segment_builder_t b(f);
+  b.prefix();
+  b.queue_loop(2, true, 7, 3, 4);
+  b.finish_forward(true);
+  b.prefix();
+  b.queue_loop(1, true, 5, 2, 3);
+  b.finish_forward(false);
+  b.stage();
+
+  for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kO1aLeafCount; ++i)
+    CHECK(f.c->o1a_leaf[i] > 0);
+  CHECK(f.c->c.n_o1a_cv_waits == 2);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1c_forwards == 2);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_anomalies == 0);
+}
+
+void test_k_split() {
+  fixture_t f;
+  segment_builder_t b(f);
+  b.prefix(2, 5, 7);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafPreviousExit] == 9);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafQbatchTeardown] == 5);
+  b.queue_loop(0, false, 0, 2, 3);
+  b.finish_forward(false);
+  b.stage();
+  CHECK(f.c->c.d_o1a_previous_exit == 9);
+  CHECK(f.c->c.d_o1a_qbatch_teardown == 5);
+  CHECK(staged_leaf_sum(f.c->c) == f.c->o1_before_run_forward);
+}
+
+void test_no_wait() {
+  fixture_t f;
+  segment_builder_t b(f);
+  b.prefix();
+  b.queue_loop(0, false, 0, 6, 4);
+  b.finish_forward(false);
+  b.stage();
+  CHECK(f.c->c.n_o1a_cv_waits == 0);
+  CHECK(f.c->c.d_o1a_cv_wait == 0);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_anomalies == 0);
+}
+
+void test_zero_duration_wait() {
+  fixture_t f;
+  segment_builder_t b(f);
+  b.prefix();
+  b.queue_loop(2, true, 0, 3, 4);
+  b.finish_forward(false);
+  b.stage();
+  CHECK(f.c->c.n_o1a_cv_waits == 1);
+  CHECK(f.c->c.d_o1a_cv_wait == 0);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_anomalies == 0);
+}
+
+void test_real_wait_fixture() {
+  fixture_t f;
+  segment_builder_t b(f);
+  b.prefix();
+  uint64_t queue_before = f.c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafQueueHandoff];
+  b.queue_loop(3, true, 11, 5, 7);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafCvWait] == 11);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafQueueHandoff] - queue_before == 8);
+  b.finish_forward(false);
+  b.stage();
+  CHECK(f.c->c.d_o1a_cv_wait == 11);
+  CHECK(f.c->c.d_o1a_queue_handoff == 8);
+}
+
+void test_repeated_queue_loop() {
+  fixture_t f;
+  segment_builder_t b(f);
+  b.prefix();
+  b.queue_loop(0, false, 0, 2, 3);
+  b.queue_loop(0, false, 0, 4, 5);
+  b.queue_loop(1, true, 2, 3, 4);
+  b.finish_forward(false);
+  b.stage();
+  CHECK(f.c->c.n_o1a_queue_loops == 3);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitQ0] == 3);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitD] == 3);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitX] == 3);
+}
+
+void test_empty_sweep() {
+  fixture_t f;
+  segment_builder_t b(f);
+  b.prefix();
+  b.queue_loop(0, false, 0, 2, 3);
+  b.empty_sweep();
+  b.queue_loop(0, false, 0, 4, 2);
+  b.finish_forward(false);
+  b.stage();
+  CHECK(f.c->c.n_o1a_empty_sweeps == 1);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitF] == 2);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitG] == 2);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitZ] == 1);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitV0] == 2);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitV1] == 2);
+}
+
+void test_other_executor() {
+  fixture_t f;
+  segment_builder_t b(f);
+  b.prefix();
+  b.queue_loop(0, false, 0, 2, 3);
+  uint64_t dispatch_before = f.c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafDispatchDelay];
+  b.other_executor(17);
+  b.other_executor(19);
+  b.finish_forward(false, 5);
+  b.stage();
+  CHECK(f.c->c.n_o1a_other_executor_forwards == 2);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitV0] == 3);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitV1] == 3);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitF] == 1);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafDispatchDelay] - dispatch_before == 41);
+}
+
+void test_zero_carryover() {
+  fixture_t f;
+  segment_builder_t b(f);
+  for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
+    b.prefix();
+    b.queue_loop(0, false, 0, 2, 3);
+    b.finish_forward(true, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, uint64_t(11 + i), 1);
+  }
+  b.prefix();
+  b.queue_loop(0, false, 0, 2, 3);
+  b.finish_forward(false);
+  b.stage();
+  CHECK(b.forwards == 3);
+  CHECK(b.zero_forwards == 2);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafZeroLogitsBody] == 23);
+  CHECK(staged_leaf_sum(f.c->c) == f.c->o1_before_run_forward);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_anomalies == 0);
+}
+
+void prepare_open_record(region_ctl_t* c, uint64_t cursor) {
+  c->rec_open = true;
+  c->cursor = cursor;
+  c->o1_phase = kO1AwaitRecordedReturn;
+  c->c.rec_type = kRecCycle;
+  c->c.rec_version = uint8_t(kRecordVersion);
+  c->c.cycle_id = 1;
+  c->c.t_entry = cursor;
+}
+
+void test_copy_clear_order() {
+  fixture_t f;
+  segment_builder_t b(f);
+  b.prefix();
+  b.queue_loop(0, false, 0, 2, 3);
+  b.finish_forward(false);
+  b.stage();
+  rec_cycle_t staged = f.c->c;
+  prepare_open_record(f.c, tsc());
+  orch_cycle_end(f.c, &f.state, 1, 1, 0, 1, 1, 1);
+
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->records_written == 1);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->published_count == 1);
+  const auto* committed = reinterpret_cast<const rec_cycle_t*>(f.c->payload);
+  CHECK(committed->commit != 0);
+  CHECK(committed->d_o1a_record_tail == staged.d_o1a_record_tail);
+  CHECK(committed->d_o1a_qbatch_teardown == staged.d_o1a_qbatch_teardown);
+  CHECK(committed->d_o1a_previous_exit == staged.d_o1a_previous_exit);
+  CHECK(committed->d_o1a_queue_handoff == staged.d_o1a_queue_handoff);
+  CHECK(committed->d_o1a_request_apply == staged.d_o1a_request_apply);
+  CHECK(committed->d_o1a_dispatch_delay == staged.d_o1a_dispatch_delay);
+  CHECK(committed->d_o1a_forward_args == staged.d_o1a_forward_args);
+  CHECK(committed->d_o1a_forward_setup == staged.d_o1a_forward_setup);
+  CHECK(committed->d_o1a_state_entry == staged.d_o1a_state_entry);
+  CHECK(o1a_phase_load(f.c) == kO1aPreviousExit);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_executor_open && f.c->o1a_executor_kind == 1);
+  for (uint32_t i = 1; i < kO1aLeafCount; ++i)
+    CHECK(f.c->o1a_leaf[i] == 0);
+  for (uint32_t i = 0; i < kO1aCountCount; ++i)
+    CHECK(f.c->o1a_count[i] == 0);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitP] == 1);
+
+  // The following zero-logit R must preserve the new aggregate, add only its
+  // zero body, and seed the next P without publishing another record.
+  orch_o1a_executor_end(&f.scheduler);
+  uint64_t t = f.c->o1a_cursor;
+  auto advance = [&](uint64_t d) { return t += d; };
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_transition_at(f.c, kO1aPreviousExit, kO1aQbatchTeardown, advance(2)));
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(f.c, &f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitK]));
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_transition_at(f.c, kO1aQbatchTeardown, kO1aPreviousExit, advance(3)));
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(f.c, &f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitQ]));
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_transition_at(f.c, kO1aPreviousExit, kO1aQueueHandoff, advance(4)));
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(f.c, &f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitU]));
+  f.c->o1a_queue_loop_open = true;
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(f.c, &f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitQ0]));
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_flush_at(f.c, kO1aQueueHandoff, advance(2)));
+  o1a_phase_store(f.c, kO1aRequestApply);
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(f.c, &f.c->o1a_count[kO1aCountQueueLoops]));
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(f.c, &f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitD]));
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_transition_at(f.c, kO1aRequestApply, kO1aDispatchDelay, advance(2)));
+  f.c->o1a_queue_loop_open = false;
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(f.c, &f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitX]));
+  orch_o1a_executor_begin(&f.scheduler);
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_transition_at(f.c, kO1aDispatchDelay, kO1aForwardArgs, advance(2)));
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(f.c, &f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitF]));
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_transition_at(f.c, kO1aForwardArgs, kO1aForwardSetup, advance(2)));
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(f.c, &f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitG]));
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_transition_at(f.c, kO1aForwardSetup, kO1aStateEntry, advance(2)));
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_inc(f.c, &f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitH]));
+  orch_o1a_run_forward_begin_at(f.c, advance(2));
+  orch_o1a_zero_entry_at(f.c, advance(5));
+  uint64_t zero_before = f.c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafZeroLogitsBody];
+  f.c->o1_phase = kO1AwaitZeroReturn;
+  f.c->o1_cursor = t;
+  orch_cycle_end(f.c, &f.state, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->records_written == 1);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafZeroLogitsBody] > zero_before);
+  CHECK(o1a_phase_load(f.c) == kO1aPreviousExit);
+}
+
+void test_sentinel() {
+  fixture_t f;
+  f.bind_pre_sentinel();
+  CHECK(!f.c->have_prev);
+  CHECK(o1a_phase_load(f.c) == kO1aNoSeed);
+  prepare_open_record(f.c, tsc());
+  f.c->c.flags = kCFlagFirstRecord;
+  orch_o1a_stage_cycle_fields(f.c);
+  orch_cycle_end(f.c, &f.state, 1, 1, 0, 1, 1, 1);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->records_written == 1);
+  const auto* r = reinterpret_cast<const rec_cycle_t*>(f.c->payload);
+  CHECK((r->flags & kCFlagFirstRecord) != 0);
+  CHECK(staged_leaf_sum(*r) == 0);
+  CHECK(r->n_o1a_cv_waits == 0);
+  CHECK(r->n_o1a_empty_sweeps == 0);
+  CHECK(r->n_o1a_queue_loops == 0);
+  CHECK(r->n_o1a_other_executor_forwards == 0);
+  CHECK(f.c->have_prev);
+  CHECK(o1a_phase_load(f.c) == kO1aPreviousExit);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_hit[kO1aHitP] == 1);
+}

+void test_saturation_boundaries() {
+  fixture_t f;
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_narrow_duration(f.c, 0xFFFFFFFFull) == 0xFFFFFFFFu);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->saturated_durations == 0);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_anomalies == 0);
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_narrow_count(f.c, 255) == 255);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_count_overflows == 0);
+
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_narrow_duration(f.c, 0x100000000ull) == 0xFFFFFFFFu);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->saturated_durations == 1);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_anomalies == 1);
+  CHECK((f.c->o1_flags & (kCFlagSaturated | kCFlagO1aPartitionAnomaly)) ==
+        (kCFlagSaturated | kCFlagO1aPartitionAnomaly));
+
+  CHECK(orch_o1a_narrow_count(f.c, 256) == 255);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_count_overflows == 1);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_anomalies == 2);
+}

+void test_missing_duplicate_marker() {
+  fixture_t f;
+  segment_builder_t b(f);
+  auto expect_one = [&](auto&& fn) {
+    uint32_t before = f.c->hdr->o1a_anomalies;
+    fn();
+    CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_anomalies == before + 1);
+    CHECK((f.c->o1_flags & kCFlagO1aPartitionAnomaly) != 0);
+  };
+
+  // Q without K, then duplicate K and duplicate Q.
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1a_qbatch_cleared(&f.state); });
+  orch_o1a_qbatch_clear_begin(&f.state);
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1a_qbatch_clear_begin(&f.state); });
+  orch_o1a_qbatch_cleared(&f.state);
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1a_qbatch_cleared(&f.state); });
+
+  orch_o1a_scheduler_exit(&f.scheduler);
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1a_scheduler_exit(&f.scheduler); });
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1a_wait_begin(); });  // W0 without Q0.
+  orch_o1a_queue_loop();
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1a_queue_loop(); });
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1a_wait_end(); });  // W1 without W0.
+  orch_o1a_wait_begin();
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1a_wait_begin(); });
+  orch_o1a_wait_end();
+  orch_o1a_queue_drained();
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1a_queue_drained(); });
+  orch_o1a_requests_applied();
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1a_requests_applied(); });
+
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1a_executor_end(&f.scheduler); });
+  orch_o1a_executor_begin(&f.scheduler);
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1a_executor_begin(&f.scheduler); });
+  orch_o1a_executor_end(&f.scheduler);
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1a_executor_end(&f.scheduler); });
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1a_forward_entry(&f.scheduler); });
+
+  orch_o1a_executor_begin(&f.scheduler);
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1a_forward_args(&f.scheduler, false); });
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1a_empty_return(&f.scheduler); });
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1a_state_call(&f.scheduler, &f.state); });
+  expect_one([&] { orch_o1_run_forward_begin(&f.state); });
+}
+
+void test_wrong_owner_tid() {
+  fixture_t f;
+  segment_builder_t b(f);
+  uint32_t owner_before = f.c->hdr->o1a_owner_mismatches;
+
+  orch_o1a_qbatch_clear_begin(&f.wrong_state);            // K
+  orch_o1a_qbatch_cleared(&f.wrong_state);                // Q
+  orch_o1a_scheduler_exit(&f.wrong_scheduler);            // U
+  orch_o1a_forward_entry(&f.wrong_scheduler);             // F
+  orch_o1a_forward_args(&f.wrong_scheduler, false);       // G
+  orch_o1a_empty_return(&f.wrong_scheduler);              // Z
+  orch_o1a_state_call(&f.scheduler, &f.wrong_state);      // H
+  orch_o1_run_forward_begin(&f.wrong_state);              // A
+  orch_o1_model_begin(&f.wrong_state);                    // B
+  orch_o1_model_end(&f.wrong_state);                      // C
+  orch_o1_run_logits_call(&f.wrong_state);                // D
+  CHECK(orch_cycle_begin(&f.wrong_state, 1) == nullptr);  // E
+  orch_cycle_end(f.c, &f.wrong_state, 1, 1, 0, 1, 1, 1);  // R
+  orch_o1a_executor_begin(nullptr);                       // invalid V0 owner
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_owner_mismatches == owner_before + 14);
+
+  uint32_t tid_before = f.c->hdr->o1a_cross_thread_hooks;
+  std::thread wrong_tid([&] {
+    orch_o1a_qbatch_clear_begin(&f.state);
+    orch_o1a_qbatch_cleared(&f.state);
+    orch_o1a_scheduler_exit(&f.scheduler);
+    orch_o1a_queue_loop();
+    orch_o1a_wait_begin();
+    orch_o1a_wait_end();
+    orch_o1a_queue_drained();
+    orch_o1a_requests_applied();
+    orch_o1a_executor_begin(&f.scheduler);
+    orch_o1a_executor_end(&f.scheduler);
+    orch_o1a_forward_entry(&f.scheduler);
+    orch_o1a_forward_args(&f.scheduler, false);
+    orch_o1a_empty_return(&f.scheduler);
+    orch_o1a_state_call(&f.scheduler, &f.state);
+    orch_o1_run_forward_begin(&f.state);
+    orch_o1_model_begin(&f.state);
+    orch_o1_model_end(&f.state);
+    orch_o1_run_logits_call(&f.state);
+    (void)orch_cycle_begin(&f.state, 1);
+    orch_cycle_end(f.c, &f.state, 1, 1, 0, 1, 1, 1);
+    orch_o1a_clean_termination();
+  });
+  wrong_tid.join();
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_cross_thread_hooks == tid_before + 21);
+}
+
+void test_clean_terminal_prefix() {
+  fixture_t f;
+  segment_builder_t b(f);
+  CHECK(o1a_phase_load(f.c) == kO1aPreviousExit);
+  CHECK(leaf_sum(f.c) > 0);
+  orch_o1a_clean_termination();
+  CHECK(o1a_phase_load(f.c) == kO1aTerminalIdle);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_terminal_prefix_discards == 1);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_terminal_prefix_phase == kO1aPreviousExit);
+  CHECK(aggregate_is_zero(f.c));
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_anomalies == 0);
+}
+
+void test_terminal_zero_carryover() {
+  fixture_t f;
+  segment_builder_t b(f);
+  b.prefix();
+  b.queue_loop(0, false, 0, 2, 3);
+  b.finish_forward(true);
+  CHECK(f.c->o1a_leaf[kO1aLeafZeroLogitsBody] > 0);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->records_written == 0);
+  orch_o1a_clean_termination();
+  CHECK(o1a_phase_load(f.c) == kO1aTerminalIdle);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_terminal_prefix_discards == 1);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_terminal_prefix_phase == kO1aPreviousExit);
+  CHECK(aggregate_is_zero(f.c));
+  for (size_t i = 0; i < 4 * kRecSize; ++i)
+    CHECK(f.c->payload[i] == 0);
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->records_written == 0);
+}
+
+void test_missing_t_crash() {
+  fixture_t f;
+  segment_builder_t b(f);
+  CHECK(o1a_phase_load(f.c) != kO1aTerminalIdle);
+  o1a_dump_fail_if_open();
+  CHECK(f.c->hdr->o1a_anomalies == 1);
+  CHECK((f.c->o1_flags & kCFlagO1aPartitionAnomaly) != 0);
+}

+void test_arm_d() {
+  fixture_t f(false);
+  f.register_owner();
+
+  std::array<uint8_t, sizeof(region_ctl_t)> ctl_before{};
+  std::array<uint8_t, kRegionHdrSize> hdr_before{};
+  std::array<uint8_t, 4 * kRecSize> payload_before{};
+  std::memcpy(ctl_before.data(), f.c, sizeof(*f.c));
+  std::memcpy(hdr_before.data(), f.c->hdr, kRegionHdrSize);
+  std::memcpy(payload_before.data(), f.c->payload, payload_before.size());
+  uint64_t cycle_before = g.cycle_id.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  uint64_t tsc_before = g_observed_rdtsc_calls.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
+
+  orch_o1a_qbatch_clear_begin(&f.state);
+  orch_o1a_qbatch_cleared(&f.state);
+  orch_o1a_scheduler_exit(&f.scheduler);
+  orch_o1a_queue_loop();
+  orch_o1a_wait_begin();
+  orch_o1a_wait_end();
+  orch_o1a_queue_drained();
+  orch_o1a_requests_applied();
+  orch_o1a_executor_begin(&f.scheduler);
+  orch_o1a_executor_end(&f.scheduler);
+  orch_o1a_forward_entry(&f.scheduler);
+  orch_o1a_forward_args(&f.scheduler, false);
+  orch_o1a_empty_return(&f.scheduler);
+  orch_o1a_state_call(&f.scheduler, &f.state);
+  orch_o1_run_forward_begin(&f.state);
+  orch_o1_model_begin(&f.state);
+  orch_o1_model_end(&f.state);
+  orch_o1_run_logits_call(&f.state);
+  CHECK(orch_cycle_begin(&f.state, 1) == f.c);
+  orch_cycle_end(f.c, &f.state, 1, 1, 0, 1, 1, 1);
+  orch_o1a_clean_termination();
+  o1a_dump_fail_if_open();
+
+  CHECK(g_observed_rdtsc_calls.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == tsc_before);
+  CHECK(g.cycle_id.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == cycle_before);
+  CHECK(std::memcmp(ctl_before.data(), f.c, sizeof(*f.c)) == 0);
+  CHECK(std::memcmp(hdr_before.data(), f.c->hdr, kRegionHdrSize) == 0);
+  CHECK(std::memcmp(payload_before.data(), f.c->payload, payload_before.size()) == 0);
+}
+
+void test_meta_writer_contract() {
+  char dir_template[] = "/tmp/g2-o1a-v7-meta-XXXXXX";
+  char* dir = mkdtemp(dir_template);
+  CHECK(dir != nullptr);
+  CHECK(setenv("TRON_DIAG_RING_DIR", dir, 1) == 0);
+  CHECK(setenv("TRON_DIAG_RING_RUN_ID", "o1a-v7-meta-selftest", 1) == 0);
+  CHECK(setenv("TRON_DIAG_RING_RECORD", "0", 1) == 0);
+  CHECK(setenv("TRON_DIAG_RING_STRIDE", "1", 1) == 0);
+
+  init(55);
+  CHECK(g.map != nullptr);
+  dump();
+
+  FILE* mf = std::fopen(g.path_meta, "r");
+  CHECK(mf != nullptr);
+  char line[256]{};
+  CHECK(std::fgets(line, sizeof(line), mf) != nullptr);
+  CHECK(std::strcmp(line, "{\n") == 0);
+  CHECK(std::fgets(line, sizeof(line), mf) != nullptr);
+  CHECK(
+      std::strcmp(line, "  \"schema\": \"10.4g-o1a-substage-ring-schema.md\",\n") == 0);
+  CHECK(std::fclose(mf) == 0);
+
+  CHECK(std::remove(g.path_meta) == 0);
+  CHECK(std::remove(g.path_bin) == 0);
+  CHECK(rmdir(dir) == 0);
+}
+
+struct test_case_t {
+  const char* name;
+  void (*run)();
+};
+
+constexpr test_case_t kCases[] = {
+    {"ordinary", test_ordinary},
+    {"K split", test_k_split},
+    {"no wait", test_no_wait},
+    {"zero-duration wait", test_zero_duration_wait},
+    {"real wait fixture", test_real_wait_fixture},
+    {"repeated queue loop", test_repeated_queue_loop},
+    {"empty sweep", test_empty_sweep},
+    {"other executor", test_other_executor},
+    {"zero carryover", test_zero_carryover},
+    {"copy/clear order", test_copy_clear_order},
+    {"sentinel", test_sentinel},
+    {"saturation boundaries", test_saturation_boundaries},
+    {"missing/duplicate marker", test_missing_duplicate_marker},
+    {"wrong owner/TID", test_wrong_owner_tid},
+    {"clean terminal prefix", test_clean_terminal_prefix},
+    {"terminal zero carryover", test_terminal_zero_carryover},
+    {"missing T/crash", test_missing_t_crash},
+    {"arm D", test_arm_d},
+    {"meta writer contract", test_meta_writer_contract},
+};
+
+}  // namespace
+
+int main() {
+  for (const test_case_t& test : kCases) {
+    pid_t child = fork();
+    if (child < 0) {
+      std::perror("fork");
+      return 1;
+    }
+    if (child == 0) {
+      g_case_name = test.name;
+      test.run();
+      _exit(0);
+    }
+
+    int status = 0;
+    if (waitpid(child, &status, 0) != child) {
+      std::perror("waitpid");
+      return 1;
+    }
+    if (!WIFEXITED(status) || WEXITSTATUS(status) != 0) {
+      std::fprintf(stderr, "FAIL %s: child status 0x%x\n", test.name, status);
+      return 1;
+    }
+    std::printf("PASS %s\n", test.name);
+  }
+  return 0;
+}

+std::runtime_error system_error(std::string_view operation) {
+  return std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + std::strerror(errno));
+}
+
+int open_fifo(std::filesystem::path const& path) {
+  int const descriptor =
+      ::open(path.c_str(), O_RDWR | O_NONBLOCK | O_CLOEXEC | O_NOFOLLOW);
+  if (descriptor < 0) throw system_error("open test FIFO");
+  return descriptor;
+}
+
+void write_line(int descriptor, std::string const& line) {
+  ssize_t count = -1;
+  do {
+    count = ::write(descriptor, line.data(), line.size());
+  } while (count < 0 && errno == EINTR);
+  if (count < 0) throw system_error("write test FIFO");
+  if (static_cast<size_t>(count) != line.size()) {
+    throw std::runtime_error("partial test FIFO write");
+  }
+}
+
+std::string read_line(int descriptor) {
+  auto const deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + 2s;
+  std::string result;
+  while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline) {
+    struct pollfd item{.fd = descriptor, .events = POLLIN, .revents = 0};
+    int const ready = ::poll(&item, 1, 50);
+    if (ready < 0 && errno == EINTR) continue;
+    if (ready < 0) throw system_error("poll test FIFO");
+    if (ready == 0) continue;
+    if (!(item.revents & POLLIN)) {
+      throw std::runtime_error("test FIFO reported an error");
+    }
+    std::array<char, 256> buffer{};
+    ssize_t const count = ::read(descriptor, buffer.data(), buffer.size());
+    if (count < 0 && (errno == EAGAIN || errno == EWOULDBLOCK)) continue;
+    if (count < 0 && errno == EINTR) continue;
+    if (count <= 0) throw system_error("read test FIFO");
+    result.append(buffer.data(), static_cast<size_t>(count));
+    if (result.size() > 1024) throw std::runtime_error("test line is too long");
+    if (result.ends_with('\n')) return result;
+    if (result.find('\n') != std::string::npos) {
+      throw std::runtime_error("test received more than one line");
+    }
+  }
+  throw std::runtime_error("timed out reading test FIFO");
+}
+
+std::vector<std::string> fields(std::string line) {
+  if (!line.empty() && line.back() == '\n') line.pop_back();
+  std::vector<std::string> result;
+  size_t begin = 0;
+  while (true) {
+    size_t const tab = line.find('\t', begin);
+    result.push_back(line.substr(begin, tab - begin));
+    if (tab == std::string::npos) return result;
+    begin = tab + 1;
+  }
+}
+
+class fifo_fixture {
+public:
+  fifo_fixture() {
+    std::array<char, 64> pattern{};
+    std::strcpy(pattern.data(), "/var/tmp/tron-expert-stats-test-XXXXXX");
+    char* const created = ::mkdtemp(pattern.data());
+    if (!created) throw system_error("mkdtemp");
+    directory_ = created;
+    if (::chmod(directory_.c_str(), 0700) != 0) throw system_error("chmod directory");
+    if (::mkfifo(events_path().c_str(), 0600) != 0) throw system_error("mkfifo events");
+    if (::mkfifo(commands_path().c_str(), 0600) != 0) {
+      throw system_error("mkfifo commands");
+    }
+    if (::chmod(events_path().c_str(), 0600) != 0 ||
+        ::chmod(commands_path().c_str(), 0600) != 0) {
+      throw system_error("chmod FIFOs");
+    }
+    events_ = open_fifo(events_path());
+    commands_ = open_fifo(commands_path());
+  }
+
+  ~fifo_fixture() {
+    if (events_ >= 0) ::close(events_);
+    if (commands_ >= 0) ::close(commands_);
+    std::error_code ec;
+    std::filesystem::remove_all(directory_, ec);
+  }
+
+  fifo_fixture(fifo_fixture const&) = delete;
+  fifo_fixture& operator=(fifo_fixture const&) = delete;
+
+  capture::controller make_controller(std::chrono::milliseconds timeout = 2s) const {
+    return make_controller_for(directory_, timeout);
+  }
+
+  capture::controller make_controller_for(std::filesystem::path const& directory,
+      std::chrono::milliseconds timeout = 2s) const {
+    return capture::controller({.enabled = true,
+        .control_dir = directory.string(),
+        .token = token_,
+        .timeout = timeout});
+  }
+
+  void replace_commands_with_regular_file() {
+    ::close(commands_);
+    commands_ = -1;
+    if (::unlink(commands_path().c_str()) != 0) throw system_error("unlink commands");
+    int const regular =
+        ::open(commands_path().c_str(), O_WRONLY | O_CREAT | O_EXCL | O_CLOEXEC, 0600);
+    if (regular < 0) throw system_error("create regular commands file");
+    ::close(regular);
+  }
+
+  std::filesystem::path const& directory() const {
+    return directory_;
+  }
+  std::filesystem::path events_path() const {
+    return directory_ / "events.fifo";
+  }
+  std::filesystem::path commands_path() const {
+    return directory_ / "commands.fifo";
+  }
+  int events() const {
+    return events_;
+  }
+  int commands() const {
+    return commands_;
+  }
+  std::string const& token() const {
+    return token_;
+  }
+
+private:
+  std::filesystem::path directory_;
+  int events_ = -1;
+  int commands_ = -1;
+  std::string const token_ = "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef";
+};
+
+std::vector<std::string> release_one(
+    fifo_fixture const& fixture, std::string_view action = "RELEASE") {
+  auto event = fields(read_line(fixture.events()));
+  if (event.size() != 8 || event[0] != "READY" || event[1] != "1" ||
+      event[3] != fixture.token()) {
+    throw std::runtime_error("invalid READY event");
+  }
+  write_line(fixture.commands(), std::string(action) + "\t1\t" + event[2] + "\t" +
+                                     event[3] + "\t" + event[4] + "\n");
+  return event;
+}
+
+}  // namespace
+

+  capture::controller controller({});
+  CHECK_FALSE(controller.enabled());
+  CHECK_NOTHROW(
+      controller.barrier(capture::phase::before, "model", "/path/that/does/not/exist"));
+}

+      capture::config_from_values("/", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef", "100"));
+  CHECK_THROWS(capture::config_from_values(
+      "/var/tmp/example", "not-a-32-character-hex-token", "100"));
+  CHECK_THROWS(capture::config_from_values(
+      "/var/tmp/example", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef", "0"));
+  CHECK_THROWS(capture::config_from_values(
+      "/var/tmp/example", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef", "100ms"));
+  CHECK_THROWS(capture::config_from_values(
+      "/var/tmp/../tmp/example", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef", "100"));

+  CHECK_THROWS(capture::validate_capture_shape(enabled, 1, 1, true));

+TEST_CASE("expert stats capture: post-workload cleanup preserves failure precedence",
+    "[capture]") {
+  auto failure = [](char const* message) {
+    try {
+      throw std::runtime_error(message);
+    } catch (...) {
+      return std::current_exception();
+    }
+  };
+  std::vector<std::string> calls;
+  auto after = [&] { calls.emplace_back("after"); };
+  auto check_calls = [&] {
+    REQUIRE(calls.size() == 2);
+    CHECK(calls[0] == "shutdown");
+    CHECK(calls[1] == "after");
+  };
+
+  SECTION("success calls shutdown and AFTER exactly once") {
+    CHECK_NOTHROW(capture::detail::finish_after_release(
+        nullptr, [&] { calls.emplace_back("shutdown"); }, after));
+    check_calls();
+  }
+
+  SECTION("successful cleanup rethrows the original workload failure") {
+    CHECK_THROWS_WITH(
+        capture::detail::finish_after_release(
+            failure("workload"), [&] { calls.emplace_back("shutdown"); }, after),
+        "workload");
+    check_calls();
+  }
+
+  SECTION("shutdown failure replaces the workload failure and skips AFTER") {
+    CHECK_THROWS_WITH(capture::detail::finish_after_release(
+                          failure("workload"),
+                          [&] {
+                            calls.emplace_back("shutdown");
+                            throw std::runtime_error("shutdown");
+                          },
+                          after),
+        "shutdown");
+    REQUIRE(calls.size() == 1);
+    CHECK(calls[0] == "shutdown");
+  }
+
+  SECTION("AFTER failure replaces the workload after successful shutdown") {
+    CHECK_THROWS_WITH(capture::detail::finish_after_release(
+                          failure("workload"), [&] { calls.emplace_back("shutdown"); },
+                          [&] {
+                            calls.emplace_back("after");
+                            throw std::runtime_error("after");
+                          }),
+        "after");
+    check_calls();
+  }
+}
+
+TEST_CASE("expert stats capture: releases before and after in order", "[capture]") {
+  fifo_fixture fixture;
+  auto controller = fixture.make_controller();
+  std::vector<std::vector<std::string>> events;
+  std::exception_ptr supervisor_error;
+  std::thread supervisor([&] {
+    try {
+      events.push_back(release_one(fixture));
+      events.push_back(release_one(fixture));
+    } catch (...) {
+      supervisor_error = std::current_exception();
+    }
+  });
+
+  std::exception_ptr controller_error;
+  try {
+    controller.barrier(capture::phase::before, "gpt-oss", "/tmp/stats");
+    controller.barrier(capture::phase::after, "gpt-oss", "/tmp/stats");
+  } catch (...) {
+    controller_error = std::current_exception();
+  }
+  supervisor.join();
+  if (supervisor_error) std::rethrow_exception(supervisor_error);
+  if (controller_error) std::rethrow_exception(controller_error);
+
+  REQUIRE(events.size() == 2);
+  CHECK(events[0][2] == "before");
+  CHECK(events[1][2] == "after");
+  CHECK(events[0][4].size() == 32);
+  CHECK(std::all_of(events[0][4].begin(), events[0][4].end(), capture::detail::is_hex));
+  CHECK(events[0][4] != events[1][4]);
+  CHECK(events[0][6] == "gpt-oss");
+  CHECK(events[0][7] == "/tmp/stats");
+  CHECK_THROWS(controller.barrier(capture::phase::after, "gpt-oss", "/tmp/stats"));
+}

+    CHECK(::unlink(link.c_str()) == 0);

+    CHECK_THROWS(controller.barrier(capture::phase::before, "gpt-oss", "/tmp/stats"));
+    CHECK(::unlink(link.c_str()) == 0);
+  }
+
+  SECTION("wrong owner") {
+    struct stat fake{};

+    fake.st_uid =
+        geteuid() == std::numeric_limits<uid_t>::max() ? geteuid() - 1 : geteuid() + 1;
+    CHECK_THROWS(
+        capture::detail::validate_owner_and_mode(fake, S_IFIFO, 0600, "commands.fifo"));
+  }
+}

+#if TRON_DIAG_RING
+TEST_CASE(
+    "Latch finalizer is visible before wait returns", "[Threading::latch_finalizer]") {
+  constexpr size_t n_callers = 55;
+  constexpr size_t n_rounds = 20;
+
+  for (size_t round = 0; round < n_rounds; ++round) {
+    latch_arena arena;
+    with_latch_arena guard(arena);
+    latch_ref latch = arena.make_latch(n_callers, "finalizer_test");
+    std::atomic<uint32_t> finalizer_calls{0};
+    std::atomic<uint64_t> marker{0};
+    std::atomic<uint32_t> detailed_commits{0};
+    std::atomic<size_t> winner{n_callers};
+    std::latch ready(n_callers);
+    std::latch go(1);
+    std::vector<std::thread> callers;
+    callers.reserve(n_callers);
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_callers; ++i) {
+      callers.emplace_back([&, i] {
+        ready.count_down();
+        go.wait();
+        // The four instrumented notifier tasks publish their detailed records
+        // before decrementing. Once wait() returns, all four must be visible.
+        if (i < 4)
+          detailed_commits.fetch_or(uint32_t{1} << i, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+        latch.count_down_with_finalizer([&, i]() noexcept {
+          winner.store(i, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+          marker.store(0xC0DEC0DEu, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+          finalizer_calls.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+        });
+      });
+    }
+
+    ready.wait();
+    go.count_down();
+    latch.wait();
+    REQUIRE(marker.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == 0xC0DEC0DEu);
+    REQUIRE(detailed_commits.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == 0xFu);
+    for (auto& caller : callers)
+      caller.join();
+    REQUIRE(finalizer_calls.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == 1);
+    REQUIRE(winner.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) < n_callers);
+  }
+}
+#endif
+
+#if TRON_DIAG_RING
+TEST_CASE("O1c helper checkpoints preserve private order and reduce maxima",
+    "[Threading::g2_helper_checkpoints]") {
+  using namespace tron::g2ring;
+  constexpr uint32_t n_helpers = 15;
+  constexpr uint64_t epoch = 0xA500;
+  int scheduler_owner = 1;
+  int state_owner = 2;
+  const void* state_owner_ptr = &state_owner;
+  region_hdr_t owner_hdr{};
+  region_ctl_t& owner_ctl = g.ctl[kOrchRegion];
+
+  REQUIRE_FALSE(g.o1a_owner_registered);
+  g.map = reinterpret_cast<uint8_t*>(&owner_hdr);
+  g.recording = true;
+  owner_ctl.hdr = &owner_hdr;
+  owner_ctl.recording = true;
+  o1a_register_owner(&scheduler_owner, state_owner_ptr);
+
+  std::memset(g.o1c_helper_checkpoint, 0, sizeof(g.o1c_helper_checkpoint));
+  g.o1c.expected_helpers = n_helpers;
+  g.o1c.expected_helper_checkpoints = kO1cHelperCheckpointCount;
+  g.o1c.async_anomaly.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  g.o1c.active_epoch.store(epoch, std::memory_order_release);
+
+  std::vector<std::thread> helpers;
+  helpers.reserve(n_helpers);
+  for (uint32_t helper_ix = 0; helper_ix < n_helpers; ++helper_ix) {
+    helpers.emplace_back([=] {
+      for (uint32_t checkpoint_ix = 0; checkpoint_ix < kO1cHelperCheckpointCount;
+          ++checkpoint_ix)
+        o1c_helper_blend_returned(state_owner_ptr, epoch, helper_ix, checkpoint_ix);
+    });
+  }
+  for (auto& helper : helpers)
+    helper.join();
+
+  REQUIRE(g.o1c.async_anomaly.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == 0);
+  for (uint32_t helper_ix = 0; helper_ix < n_helpers; ++helper_ix) {
+    const auto& row = g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[helper_ix];
+    REQUIRE(row.epoch == epoch);
+    REQUIRE(row.seen_mask == 0xFu);
+    for (uint32_t checkpoint_ix = 1; checkpoint_ix < kO1cHelperCheckpointCount;
+        ++checkpoint_ix)
+      REQUIRE(row.returned_tsc[checkpoint_ix - 1] <= row.returned_tsc[checkpoint_ix]);
+  }
+
+  // Make different helpers own different maxima. This proves the reducer
+  // computes each checkpoint barrier independently after publication.
+  g.o1c.expected_helpers = 2;
+  g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[0].epoch = epoch;
+  g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[0].seen_mask = 0xFu;
+  g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[0].returned_tsc[0] = 200;
+  g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[0].returned_tsc[1] = 300;
+  g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[0].returned_tsc[2] = 900;
+  g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[0].returned_tsc[3] = 1000;
+  g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[1].epoch = epoch;
+  g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[1].seen_mask = 0xFu;
+  g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[1].returned_tsc[0] = 250;
+  g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[1].returned_tsc[1] = 800;
+  g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[1].returned_tsc[2] = 850;
+  g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[1].returned_tsc[3] = 1100;
+  region_ctl_t c{};
+  c.o1c_b = 100;
+  bool clock_anomaly = false;
+  REQUIRE(orch_o1c_reduce_helper_checkpoints(&c, epoch, 1200, &clock_anomaly));
+  REQUIRE_FALSE(clock_anomaly);
+  REQUIRE(c.o1c_helper_blend_return[0] == 250);
+  REQUIRE(c.o1c_helper_blend_return[1] == 800);
+  REQUIRE(c.o1c_helper_blend_return[2] == 900);
+  REQUIRE(c.o1c_helper_blend_return[3] == 1100);
+
+  // A skipped initial checkpoint, duplicate slot, wrong order, and stale
+  // epoch must fail loud.
+  g.o1c.expected_helpers = 1;
+  std::memset(&g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[0], 0, sizeof(g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[0]));
+  g.o1c.async_anomaly.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  o1c_helper_blend_returned(state_owner_ptr, epoch, 0, 1);
+  REQUIRE((g.o1c.async_anomaly.load(std::memory_order_acquire) & kO1cAsyncEpoch) != 0);
+  g.o1c.async_anomaly.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  o1c_helper_blend_returned(state_owner_ptr, epoch, 0, 0);
+  o1c_helper_blend_returned(state_owner_ptr, epoch, 0, 0);
+  REQUIRE(
+      (g.o1c.async_anomaly.load(std::memory_order_acquire) & kO1cAsyncDuplicate) != 0);
+  std::memset(&g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[0], 0, sizeof(g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[0]));
+  g.o1c.async_anomaly.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  o1c_helper_blend_returned(state_owner_ptr, epoch, 0, 0);
+  o1c_helper_blend_returned(state_owner_ptr, epoch, 0, 2);
+  REQUIRE((g.o1c.async_anomaly.load(std::memory_order_acquire) &
+              kO1cAsyncCheckpointOrder) != 0);
+  g.o1c.async_anomaly.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  g.o1c.active_epoch.store(epoch + 1, std::memory_order_release);
+  o1c_helper_blend_returned(state_owner_ptr, epoch, 0, 1);
+  REQUIRE((g.o1c.async_anomaly.load(std::memory_order_acquire) & kO1cAsyncEpoch) != 0);
+
+  g.o1c.expected_helpers = 2;
+  g.o1c.active_epoch.store(epoch, std::memory_order_release);
+  g.o1c_helper_checkpoint[1].returned_tsc[2] = 700;
+  clock_anomaly = false;
+  REQUIRE_FALSE(orch_o1c_reduce_helper_checkpoints(&c, epoch, 1200, &clock_anomaly));
+  REQUIRE(clock_anomaly);
+
+  g.o1c.active_epoch.store(0, std::memory_order_release);
+  g.o1c.expected_helpers = 0;
+  g.o1c.expected_helper_checkpoints = 0;
+  g.o1c.async_anomaly.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+  std::memset(g.o1c_helper_checkpoint, 0, sizeof(g.o1c_helper_checkpoint));
+  g.o1a_scheduler_owner = nullptr;
+  g.o1a_state_owner = nullptr;
+  g.o1a_owner_registered = false;
+  g.o1a_registration_failed = false;
+  owner_ctl.recording = false;
+  owner_ctl.hdr = nullptr;
+  g.recording = false;
+  g.map = nullptr;
+}
+
+TEST_CASE("first REC_CYCLE keeps its complete O1c identity",
+    "[Threading::g2_first_cycle_o1c]") {
+  using namespace tron::g2ring;
+  region_hdr_t hdr{};
+  region_ctl_t c{};
+  c.hdr = &hdr;
+  c.have_prev = false;
+
+  // The first record has no previous run_logits return, but Stamp B through C
+  // is complete. Keep that O1c evidence instead of discarding one forward.
+  c.o1_before_run_forward = 111;
+  c.o1_setup = 222;
+  c.o1_model_forward = 1200;
+  c.o1_post_model = 333;
+  c.o1_pre_run_logits = 444;
+  c.o1c_dispatch_sum = 300;
+  c.o1c_main_prejoin_sum = 500;
+  c.o1c_attention_done_sum = 600;
+  c.o1c_helper_done_sum = 900;
+  c.o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[0] = 400;
+  c.o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[1] = 500;
+  c.o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[2] = 700;
+  c.o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[3] = 800;
+  c.o1c_helper_checkpoint_mode = kO1cHelperCheckpointCount;
+  c.o1c_critical_ready_sum = 1000;
+  c.o1c_join_tail_sum = 200;
+  c.o1c_attention_tasks = 120;
+  c.o1c_helper_tasks = 45;

+  c.o1c_zero_logit_forwards = 2;
+  c.o1c_attention_forwards = 3;
+  c.o1c_helper_forwards = 3;
+  c.o1c_main_critical = 1;
+  c.o1c_attention_critical = 1;
+  c.o1c_helper_critical = 1;
+
+  orch_stage_cycle_prefix(&c, 9000);

+  REQUIRE(c.c.t_prev_return == 0);

+TEST_CASE("first REC_CYCLE preserves O1c sums above uint32",
+    "[Threading::g2_first_cycle_o1c_width]") {
+  using namespace tron::g2ring;
+  constexpr uint64_t hi = uint64_t(1) << 32;
+  region_hdr_t hdr{};
+  region_ctl_t c{};
+  c.hdr = &hdr;
+  c.have_prev = false;
+
+  // A real gpt-oss TP4 smoke carried nine zero-logit forwards into the first
+  // published row.  Its exact endpoint sums exceeded uint32.  The wire keeps
+  // each low word at its v5 offset and stores the matching high word in the
+  // former reserved tail; this must not be classified as saturation.
+  c.o1_model_forward = 2 * hi + 700;
+  c.o1c_dispatch_sum = hi + 100;
+  c.o1c_main_prejoin_sum = hi + 200;
+  c.o1c_attention_done_sum = hi + 300;
+  c.o1c_helper_done_sum = hi + 500;
+  c.o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[0] = hi + 150;
+  c.o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[1] = hi + 250;
+  c.o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[2] = hi + 350;
+  c.o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[3] = hi + 450;
+  c.o1c_helper_checkpoint_mode = kO1cHelperCheckpointCount;
+  c.o1c_critical_ready_sum = hi + 600;
+  c.o1c_join_tail_sum = hi + 100;
+  c.o1c_attention_tasks = 400;
+  c.o1c_helper_tasks = 150;
+  c.o1c_forwards = 10;
+  c.o1c_zero_logit_forwards = 9;
+  c.o1c_attention_forwards = 10;
+  c.o1c_helper_forwards = 10;
+  c.o1c_helper_critical = 10;
+
+  orch_stage_cycle_prefix(&c, 9000);
+
+  auto exact = [](uint32_t low, uint32_t high) {
+    return uint64_t(low) | (uint64_t(high) << 32);
+  };
+  REQUIRE(exact(c.c.dispatch_sum, c.c.dispatch_sum_hi) == c.o1c_dispatch_sum);
+  REQUIRE(
+      exact(c.c.main_prejoin_sum, c.c.main_prejoin_sum_hi) == c.o1c_main_prejoin_sum);
+  REQUIRE(exact(c.c.attention_done_sum, c.c.attention_done_sum_hi) ==
+          c.o1c_attention_done_sum);
+  REQUIRE(exact(c.c.helper_done_sum, c.c.helper_done_sum_hi) == c.o1c_helper_done_sum);
+  REQUIRE(exact(c.c.helper_blend8_return_sum, c.c.helper_blend8_return_sum_hi) ==
+          c.o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[0]);
+  REQUIRE(exact(c.c.helper_blend17_return_sum, c.c.helper_blend17_return_sum_hi) ==
+          c.o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[1]);
+  REQUIRE(exact(c.c.helper_blend26_return_sum, c.c.helper_blend26_return_sum_hi) ==
+          c.o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[2]);
+  REQUIRE(exact(c.c.helper_blend35_return_sum, c.c.helper_blend35_return_sum_hi) ==
+          c.o1c_helper_blend_return_sum[3]);
+  REQUIRE(exact(c.c.critical_ready_sum, c.c.critical_ready_sum_hi) ==
+          c.o1c_critical_ready_sum);
+  REQUIRE(exact(c.c.join_tail_sum, c.c.join_tail_sum_hi) == c.o1c_join_tail_sum);
+  REQUIRE(c.c.flags == kCFlagFirstRecord);
+  REQUIRE(hdr.saturated_durations == 0);
+  REQUIRE(hdr.lane_anomalies == 0);
+}
+
+TEST_CASE("O1c duration storage preserves the u32 boundary",
+    "[Threading::g2_o1c_width_boundary]") {
+  using namespace tron::g2ring;
+  uint32_t low = 0;
+  uint32_t high = 0;
+
+  orch_o1c_stage_u64(&low, &high, 0xFFFFFFFFull);
+  REQUIRE(low == 0xFFFFFFFFu);
+  REQUIRE(high == 0u);
+
+  orch_o1c_stage_u64(&low, &high, 0x100000000ull);
+  REQUIRE(low == 0u);
+  REQUIRE(high == 1u);
+}
+
+TEST_CASE("O1c true u64 accumulation overflow remains fatal",
+    "[Threading::g2_o1c_u64_overflow]") {
+  using namespace tron::g2ring;
+  region_hdr_t hdr{};
+  region_ctl_t c{};
+  c.hdr = &hdr;
+  uint64_t value = ~uint64_t(0) - 3;
+
+  orch_o1c_add64(&c, &value, 4);
+
+  REQUIRE(value == ~uint64_t(0));
+  REQUIRE(hdr.saturated_durations == 1);
+  REQUIRE(hdr.lane_anomalies == 1);
+  REQUIRE((c.o1_flags & kCFlagSaturated) != 0);
+  REQUIRE((c.o1_flags & kCFlagO1cLaneAnomaly) != 0);
+}
+
