10. Relevant project history and decisions

O1a scope correction. The owner identified that “previous return → A” must include the previous record/return tail, not only the scheduler half. P/K/Q/U were made mandatory to capture that omitted tail.
Helper-path pipeline first. A separate helper campaign completed before O1a. The O1a binary was built separately; helper and O1a evidence were not mixed.
Helper smoke found a real u32 wire defect. Five raw-TSC sums—main_prejoin_sum, attention_done_sum, helper_done_sum, critical_ready_sum, and helper_blend35_return_sum—exceeded u32 and were written as 0xffffffff. V7/V8 correctly failed. Record v6 added high-word sidecars and exact u64 reconstruction. This was a helper instrumentation-capacity failure, not an FPGA performance result.
O1a schema collision repaired before live work. The first draft attempted to reuse bytes already owned by helper-v6. Accepted O1a record v7 instead grew to 384 bytes and placed O1a fields at bytes 312–359.
Objdump declassified. Full llvm-objdump --source produced multi-gigabyte output and missing-source warnings. The owner declared complete or targeted disassembly optional supplementary evidence. It cannot block build freeze, install, smoke, or campaign. Exact source/manifest, tests, executable hash, installed-byte equality, and runtime validation are the required proof.
O1a R1 failed safely on metadata. Workload, ring, leaves, placement, and expert stats passed, but ring.meta.json emitted the wrong schema string. R1 was rejected and preserved; validation was not weakened.
O1a R2 passed. Metadata producer/test repaired; new patch and binary frozen; smoke and 20/20 recording-on draws passed; analysis and evidence frozen; platformd serving restored and lock released.
Expanded Perfetto followed. Later 120B and 20B 20-round campaigns enabled scheduler/model/matmul/driver/driver-detail/gen. Both completed and restored production. Optional category non-emission and content defects remained report-only as the owner requested.
Security follow-up from helper history. One failed historical preflight wrapper printed remote environment values into captured output. The execution report says affected credentials must be treated as exposed and rotated by their owners. Do not copy those values into a report or command. This issue is separate from O1a instrumentation validity.

11. Ring instrumentation, Perfetto, and the heap hypothesis

QuestionAccepted answerEvidence / limit
Can Perfetto replace the O1a ring entirely?No accepted decision to discard the ring. Keep the ring for compact per-cycle identity, exact O1/O1a telescoping, run/binary identity, sentinels, fail-closed flags, and immediate validation. Perfetto is supplementary for richer scheduler/model/OS context.The R2 ring checked two exact identities across 81,900 records with zero residual. The later Perfetto traces were useful but contained report-only content defects and did not reproduce these ring contracts.
Which method has lower overhead?Insufficient data.No recording-off or ring-vs-Perfetto overhead comparison was run. The O1a report explicitly makes no instrumentation-overhead claim.
Did heap allocation/deallocation cause the fluctuation?Insufficient data.Heap counting was explicitly excluded. K→Q measured q_batches.clear() source-scope wall time (+57.022 µs slow-fast) but does not identify allocator activity. F→G is mixed lock/work/preemption time.
Would the next F split test Ben’s heap suspicion?Only indirectly. It can determine whether F→G movement is before the lock, in lock acquisition, in tree traversal, or finalization. It cannot count allocations or prove allocator causality.A later, separately approved allocator measurement would be required if the F split still points to allocation-capable code.

Expanded Perfetto context

  • 120B: 20/20 successful; mean 65.736375 tok/s; slowest draw_03; fastest draw_01.
  • 20B: 20/20 successful; mean 89.928113 tok/s; slowest draw_19; fastest draw_04.
  • Exact MoE/routing intervals were present in all 40 scheduled traces.
  • matmul and gen were enabled/registered but emitted no standalone slices in all 40.
  • Parse was not distinguishable in 11/20 120B and 19/20 20B traces; generation was distinguishable in all 40.
  • Invalid/incomplete durations were present in all 40, exactly as a report-only observation.

The Perfetto campaign used vanilla runtron SHA-256 6f5cb54f6cd22536d7ce7a9fdb0c7573d95e3a9ad5784384f68dcf3af26d6fbb, not the O1a R2 ring binary. Do not combine their absolute timing as one simultaneous experiment.

12. Implementation and live protocol if the owner approves

  1. Audit current source on alpha. Locate the current Tron checkout; confirm the base and whether the R2 patch is already present. Read full_scheduler::forward(), recorder exports/state, record layout, parser structures, validator, analyzer, tests, and generator call sites before editing.
  2. Freeze a v8 design. Add F1/F2/F3 semantics, the nodes_traversed counter, exact parent/child identity, offsets/types, repeated-attempt rules, sentinel rule, saturation/overflow behavior, terminal-prefix behavior, and HTML contract to a new schema version.
  3. Implement surgically. Keep F and G unchanged as enclosing boundaries. Add exactly three owner-thread reads and one work counter. Do not add heap instrumentation or unrelated refactoring.
  4. Update the full chain. Writer, record identity, generated header/mapping if applicable, parser, validator, analyzer, code-evidence selector/spec, HTML generator, fixtures, mutation tests, live driver pins, and manifests.
  5. Build/test on alpha. Use a clean ring-enabled build with TRON_DIAG_RING=ON. Run focused runtime/layout/parser/validator/analyzer tests plus the relevant generated-model and threading tests. Record exact hashes and sizes.
  6. Freeze source and binary. Preserve base identity, complete patch, postimage manifest, generated-header hash, executable/library hashes, test logs, and live-tool manifest. Objdump remains optional and non-blocking.
  7. Before DUT mutation. On delphi-3bda, prove CI/runner and other workloads inactive, canonical lock absent, serving healthy, eight expected cards present/owned, disk space sufficient, and staged hashes exact. Use the supported platformd handoff; never control rinzler directly.
  8. Install and verify bytes. Installed binary/library hashes must match the frozen build exactly.
  9. One uncounted recording-on smoke. Preserve placement, expert_stats before/after, command/environment, untouched ring and metadata. Immediately validate record-v8 identity, marker counts/order, three stored children, derived tail, parent identity, O1 identity, sentinel, terminal prefix, placement, expert stats, and unchanged dump hash.
  10. Twenty scheduled recording-on draws. Same model/workload/captures. Validate each dump immediately. Save an ordinary workload failure without retry and continue. Stop the campaign on any instrumentation-integrity failure.
  11. Analyze and report. Keep all denominators separate. Compare F→F1, F1→F2, F2→F3, derived F3→G, time per nodes_traversed, the existing F→G parent, O1a, O1c, O8, full ring cycle, and request generation. Use a to-scale wall-clock lane only if companion evidence proves concurrency.
  12. Restore and prove serving. Restore normal production models, verify serving/cards, release the lock, freeze evidence, and stop for owner direction.

Previous O1a workload command shape

env -u SYSTEM_CONFIG -u SPDLOG_LEVEL -u TRON_NO_RTM \
  LD_LIBRARY_PATH=<frozen-lib-dir> \
  RUNTRON_BIN=<frozen-runtron> \
  CHECKERBOARD_SKIP_GIT_CHECK=1 \
  CHECKERBOARD_SKIP_RUNTRON_BIN_CHECK=1 \
  EXTRA_RUNTRON_ARGS='-s 2954953865 --dont-stop --threshold_p 0 \
    --output-token-file <draw>/tokens.out --fuse-mount <mount>' \
  TRON_LOG_DIR=<draw> \
  TRON_DIAG_RING_RECORD=1 \
  TRON_DIAG_RING_DIR=<draw>/ring \
  TRON_DIAG_RING_STRIDE=1 \
  TRON_DIAG_RING_RUN_ID=<run-id> \
  TRON_EXPERT_STATS_CONTROL_DIR=<control-dir> \
  TRON_EXPERT_STATS_CONTROL_TOKEN=<secret-via-safe-channel> \
  TRON_EXPERT_STATS_BARRIER_TIMEOUT_MS=600000 \
  TRON_LOG_PLACEMENT=1 TRON_LOG_LEVEL=debug \
  timeout -k 30s 1200s ./run-benchmark.sh \
    -m ingested-gpt-oss-120b-tp4 -u 4 -i 1 --slice-of 2 --slice-start 0 \
    --prompt-length 1024 --gen-length 4096 \
    --shared-system-prompt-length 256 --speculation 0

TRON_LOG_PLACEMENT=1 is a runtime environment variable, not a C++ preprocessor macro. The previous source review found a presence test via getenv; therefore even setting it to 0 would enable logging. Recheck the current trunk implementation before relying on that exact source line.

Do not place real control tokens in a report, command argument, shell history, or process environment when a safer inherited descriptor/control mechanism exists. The pseudonym above is descriptive only.

13. Required tests, validation, and stop rules

Offline intent tests

  • Layout/offset/size static assertions for record v8.
  • Reserved-byte zero rule updated only for newly assigned bytes.
  • F→F1→F2→F3→G happy path and exact raw-tick sum.
  • Repeated target attempts and empty-sweep accumulation.
  • Owner mismatch, cross-thread, phase-order, clock reversal, and saturation fail closed.
  • First sentinel is zero for parent, children, and work counter.
  • Terminal T discards the unpublished prefix cleanly.
  • nodes_traversed range/narrowing boundary and overflow mutation.
  • Parser mutation cases for every new field and checksum range.
  • Analyzer derives F3→G and rejects negative/nonclosing residual.

Smoke hard gates

  • Frozen/installed byte equality and record-v8 identity.
  • All revised V1–V18 checks pass.
  • Every F/G pair has a reconciled F1/F2/F3 sequence.
  • Three stored children ≤ parent; derived tail nonnegative.
  • Children + derived tail = forward_args, residual 0.
  • Eleven O1a leaves = O1a and O1a–O1e = O1, residual 0.
  • No drops, saturation, lane/O1a anomalies, owner/TID failure, or count overflow.
  • Placement/expert stats valid; untouched hashes unchanged.

Instrumentation-integrity failureStop campaign, save evidence, restore production, fix/refreeze/reinstall, return to smoke.No performance conclusion may use corrupt instrumentation.
Optional objdump failure/absenceRecord as supplementary observation; do not block.Owner removed this gate as overkill.
